# City 18 reference and the lessons for map pieces

Generated by `python3 _dev/tools/map_lessons.py --md` from `_dev/reference/lessons.json` (the lessons) and `_dev/reference/city18/pictures.json` (the pictures): edit those, not this page. The owner asked for both, 20 November: "Take 30 reference pictures of the city 18 map. Make a database or reference for lessons and concepts learned".

**Before building or changing a map piece:** `python3 _dev/tools/map_lessons.py --tag KIND` (doors, stairs, streets, facades, interiors, lighting, grime, sound, combine, props, geometry...) and look at the pictures of the same kind of place below. **When the owner corrects a piece:** add a lesson to `lessons.json` and rerun `--md`.

The pictures are drawn by `map_view.py` (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, so orange slabs are lampposts, teal and orange boxes furniture): they show shape, massing, light and layout, not textures. Each costs usage to look at: read only the ones for the kind of place at hand.

## City 18 in numbers (counted in c18_lc.vmf)

- Lights: 597 light, 391 light_spot, 174 point_spotlight, 126 env_sprite: rooms are lit by several fixtures and many lamps throw a beam.
- Fixtures: dome light (light_domelight02_on) 151, lab fluorescent tube (lab_flourescentlight002b) 149, Combine light (combine_light002a) 75, hanging lamp fixture (lampfixture01a) 52, cage light (light_cagelight02_on) 47, bare bulbs (lightbulb01a) 30, wall light (walllight001a) 25, lampposts (lamppost03a_on) 24.
- Grime: 249 info_overlay and 267 infodecal. Commonest overlays: decalstain002a 26, trashdecal04a 13, decalstain011a/013a 5 each, Breen poster 5, plaster cracks (plaster013a, 016a), danger signs (decalsigndanger001c/e), metal grates, speaker grilles, wires, graffiti 059a, the lambda spray.
- Sound: 155 env_soundscape (d1_trainstation.*, d1_town.*, prison.*, d1_canals.*, d3_citadel.*, construct.inside, eli_01_*), 41 ambient_generic.
- Doors: 134 prop_door_rotating, all door01_left, in openings 50 wide (from 2 behind the hinge) and 110 high, the door's middle 55 up; 42 func_door, 20 func_door_rotating, 15 func_movelinear.
- Streets: about 416 between the fronts; pavements 664 (a kerb about 8 over the gutter), gutters 648 to 656, a cobbled crown 656 (stonefloor006d_c17, sett strips 006e and 006f); pavements in concretefloor016a with a concretefloor039b kerb.
- Walls: trim stonewall050f 953 faces, concretewall008a 873, 059e 734, 036a 486, brickwall014l 473, brickwall045d 385, concretewall055b 273, plasterceiling008a_c17 272, concretewall073a 271, stonewall040f 270, plasterwall041a 251 (inside), brick 053e, 040b, 037a.
- Glass: glasswindow015a 159, 007a 74, combineglass001a 73, 011a 67, trainstation template windows 001a/b and 002d, 010a, 042a, 037a, 029c: many kinds, each laid to show whole windows.
- Props: 1477 prop_static against 72 prop_dynamic, 16 prop_dynamic_override and 21 prop_physics: almost everything fixed is a model built static.
- Pipes: pipe02 straights and connectors 127 together, pipe sets (pipeset32d, 08d, 02d), gutters down fronts (gutter_704_001a), prison pipes: walls and ceilings carry pipes.
- Flats: 14 toilets, 14 sinks, 10 stoves, 10 fridges, iron bunks (prison_metalbed001a) 15; their stoves, sinks, toilets, fridges and bunks stand backs to the wall.
- Combine: combine_fence01a/b 12 each, combine_light002a 75, combineglass 73 faces, metalcombine002 on their buildings.
- Optimisation: 62 func_areaportal, 41 func_areaportalwindow, 24 func_lod, 321 env_cubemap, 21 info_lighting, 1826 info_node and 580 info_node_air.
- Brushwork: 3612 func_detail entities: much of the city's detail is detail brushes over a simple sealed shell.

## Valve's City 17 uprising (d3_c17_01 to 13) (`_dev/reference/valve_c17/`)

Valve's own City 17 uprising maps (d3_c17_01 onwards; the map sources the owner uploaded, 21 November), measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_d3_c17_NN/measure.json) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic, green NPCs; a prop whose size is unknown is a small marker) or as true slices across a street (dark grey solid, white air, pale blue sky, a grid every 128 across and 64 up). Reference only, for shape and dressing: the VMFs are not in the repo, and the owner's own choices win where they differ (streets, stairs, rooms).

- d3_c17_01: Kleiner's lab after the teleport, the rebels' hidden lab; all indoors: rooms 348 by 971 (medians) under 306 (most often 320 and 352), corridors 189 wide under 120, one door01_left in an opening 48 by 108.
- d3_c17_01: the most crowded place measured so far: 1.31 props per 100 by 100 of play (the streets of d3_c17_02 to 05: 0.15 to 0.58), 71% of statics touching a wall; lab lights (lab_flourescentlight001a and b, 14), pipes, power boxes, crates and file cabinets.
- Ours: rooms 120 to 128 high in City 18's two tones with a dado rail. Valve's lab is a hall about 306 high, its walls banded (brickwall052e below brickwall052d, the first change 64 or 128 over the foot): a lab or workshop hideout can stand taller than a flat.
- d3_c17_02: from the lab through the old streets to the Combine wall; 52 street cuts, 30 with kerbs: road 256 (23 of 30, the rest 512), kerb 4 (all 60), crown 0 (all 30), pavements most often 192 (128 to 768: the yards are wide); walls median 544.
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's d3_c17_02 street by the checkpoint (x -5640) is about 676 between the fronts (647 at the barricades): pavements 256 and 164, a road of 252 laid in strips of 64, 128 and 60 (concretefloor033q_c17 at the edges, 033k_c17 in the middle), kerbs 4, flat; fronts 680 and 616 over it (height about the width).
- d3_c17_02 checkpoint: four combine_barricade_med02c, 128 apart, straight across the street from wall to wall (x -5582, y -5956 to -5315), two each side of a gap of 128 that holds a combine_gate_citizen, with metalcombine001 blocks (48 wide, 88 high) in the line; a combine_light001a on the end barricade at 136 up; the street beyond is shut by the Combine wall (metalcombine001) 707 further east.
- d3_c17_02 wires: utility poles (utilitypole02b) 843 to 1479 apart and wall mounts (utilitypolemount01a) 333 to 2299 (median 697) carry 16 rope spans, median 345 long, slack 100, width 2; 9 of 11 lights warm ('256 224 144' most often).
- d3_c17_03: the square of the colonnade (trainstation_post001 13 and trainstation_column001 10, each 128 apart, 9 arches, awnings 16) and the streets round it; 387 cuts, 114 with kerbs: kerbs 4 (156 sides) and 8 (59); the road widths there are muddled by the square's steps (the commonest, 608 at 30 cuts, is the square's edge), so the street below was measured by hand.
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's street beside the square (x -2496 y -1544): steps down from the square (8 by 12), a pavement of 176, a road of 380 (stonefloor006a with edge strips of 006c and 006b, 64 and 60 wide), kerbs 4, flat; a pavement of 124, then a wall 32 high with a fence round a grass bed 248 wide.
- d3_c17_03 note: the 'crown 14' measure.json gives at 40 cuts is the square's steps read as a crown; the roads measured by hand are flat. Trees: tree_deciduous_03b 10 (179 to 963 apart), poplars 6 (256 to 496); 27 rope spans, many long (median 751).
- d3_c17_04: back courts behind the blocks, with headcrab canisters and striders: courtyard_template fronts, barred ground-floor windows (woodwall030b_window01a_bars 6, courtyard_template001c_bars 8); 82% of the nodes outdoors; walls in the cuts median 608 (448 to 704); the big court 1728 across (walls 1568 and 576).
- d3_c17_04 flats: ceilings 128 (19 of 41 indoor nodes, 120 at 7), corridors 128 wide (19 of 32), rooms 176 by 409 (medians), nearly bare (a TV, a file cabinet, lightbulb01a 5). Roofs: antennaclusters01a 30 (the commonest model), big gutters (gutterlarge_512 and 640) 10; lampposts (lamppost03a_off) 480 to 768 apart; fence01a 11 at 96 to 136.
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's d3_c17_04 roads are 256 (38 of 64) or 384 (17), pavements most often 192 and 128, kerbs 4 (102 of 128), crowns 0 or 2.
- d3_c17_05: a block of flats and shops the Combine have taken: control desks with monitors on three floors 128 apart, 32 metropolice, 6 floor turrets, 11 combine_lock01 on its doors; 616 nodes, 33% outdoors; 411 props (0.58 per 100 by 100).
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's d3_c17_05 street (x 1376 y -4992) is 576 wall to wall: a road of 252 in three strips (stonefloor006e_c17 edges of 64 and 60, 006d_c17 in the middle), pavements 128 and 192, kerbs 4, flat; across its 154 kerbed cuts: road 256 (103), pavements 128 (93), kerb 4 (270 of 308), crown 0 (128 of 154).
- Ours: fronts with a 128 plinth. Valve's d3_c17_05 shop front: a shop storey 136 high (storefront_template001a, glass from 16 to 120) set 32 to 48 back, then a storey every 128 (a course of 32, building_template007f, standing 16 proud, and 96 of windows, 007d) up to 648; across the street, bands of 128 (building_template012f, then 012d) up to 392. At the d3_c17_02 checkpoint: a plinth of 64, a ground floor to 200, a course of 32, then 128 a storey.
- Ours: rooms 120 to 128 high in City 18's two tones with a dado rail. Valve's d3_c17_05 flats: 120 at 294 of 415 indoor nodes, corridors 99.5 wide (64 to 155), rooms 178 by 380, doorways 48 by 108 (door01_left 5) and 56 to 59 wide for door02_double (6); walls banded plasterwall022c_c17 below 022a; radiators 7, sinks 6; 42 of 45 lights warm (37 of them '256 224 144').
- d3_c17_06a: the road tunnel under the city (abandoned cars, zombies): 92% of the nodes indoors, ceilings 192 (130 of 357) and 224 (70); the tunnel at y -6319 is 768 wall to wall: a walk of 128 on a kerb of 4, two lanes of 232 (concretefloor033k with edge strips of 033p and 033q) either side of a row of pillars 64 wide, tiled walls (tilewall006c and d); light_cagelight02_on 350 to 1261 apart (median 611), prison_sprinkler001a 30.
- d3_c17_06b: the citizens' block the Combine raid (40 soldiers, 22 citizens): ceilings 120 (263 of 419 indoor nodes), corridors 96 wide, rooms 218 by 534 (medians); the rebels' barricades are props: shelves (furniture_shelf01a 8) pushed up in pairs, boards over windows as physics props three to a window (wood_board05a, 07a, 05a), mattresses on the floor; destroyedceiling01a to d (27) where the Combine wall broke through.
- d3_c17_07: the street war at the Combine wall; 131 street cuts, 123 with kerbs: road 256 (63), pavements 128 (75), 192 and 224 (27 each), kerb 4 (234 of 246), crown 0 (112 of 123); walls median 608 (544 to 704); lampposts (lamppost03a_off) 565 to 1431 apart (median 635), utility poles 954 to 1211, 25 rope spans (median 1010).
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's d3_c17_07 street at x 5648 y 2784 is 608 wall to wall: road 256, pavements 220 and 128, kerbs 4, flat; fronts 672 and 512 under pitched tile roofs (tileroof004a rising about 112 in 128); a shop storey of 128 (storefront_template001a), then building_template002 or 017 storeys; a hotel sign 256 tall standing 64 proud.
- d3_c17_07 Combine places: a post in a square 1179 across (combine_booth_short01a facing out, a combine_interface001 beside it, combine_generator01 2, a metalcombine001 block, two combine_barricade_tall02b 384 apart on the pavements of the street beyond); and a block of flats the Combine hold (combine_lock01 8, combine_interface001 4, combine_intmonitor001 3, 2 floor turrets) among bathtubs, beds and radiators.
- d3_c17_08: the pipe works (all indoors): 994 props, 1.49 per 100 by 100, most of them pipes (pipe01 to 03 straights and connectors over 500) and catwalk rails (handrail04 long 40, short 22, medium 19); statics touching a wall only 22%; ceilings 152 (28), 128 (25), up to 600 in the hall; 55 of 96 lights a dim blue fill ('32 64 80'), cage lights (light_cagelight02_on 20, median 394 apart); flooded tunnels 80 to 364 wide with water only 9 to 15 deep.
- d3_c17_09: the shop streets under the Nexus (5 snipers): 238 cuts, 177 with kerbs: roads 320 (42), 256 (35), 640 (30) and 192 (26), pavements 160 (63), 128 (55) and 64 (42), kerb 4 (347 of 354), crown 0 (170 of 177); walls 640 most often, and the Nexus wall 1536 high along 169 cuts; ceilings 184 (138 of 277 indoor nodes) and 120 (62).
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's d3_c17_09 street at x 4944 y 4584 is 724 wall to wall: road 320 (stonefloor006d with 006f edges), pavements 144 and 256, kerbs 4, flat, fronts 608 and 512.
- d3_c17_10a: the park and streets before the Nexus (d3_c17_11 and 12 come back to the same ground later, in the same coordinates); 335 street cuts, 84 with kerbs: roads 384 (54 of 84), pavements 128 (56 of 168) and 192 (17), kerbs 4 (128) or 8 (37), crown 0 (82 of 84); wall to wall most often 768; ceilings indoors 120 (43 of 165 nodes) and 312 (37).
- d3_c17_10a park: a strip about 800 wide between low stone walls (stonewall040f, 28 thick, 128 high at the sides, 28 high round the beds): grass and gravel beds (blendgrassgravel001a) 270 to 340 wide, paths of concrete (concretefloor019a) 260 wide across it; poplars (tree_poplar_01) 512 apart, benches (bench_indoor001a) 226 apart, lampposts (lamppost03a_off) 605 apart, combine_fence01a and 01b in pairs 488 apart along it.
- d3_c17_10a Combine vehicle gates: a combine_gate_vehicle with a combine_barricade_tall02b (141 by 133 by 261) either side, 384 apart (x -784 and -400, y 4474) and 380 apart (x -2278 and -1898, y 8502); a combine_light001a on a wall beside each, two combine_emitter01 64 apart by the north gate.
- d3_c17_10a rubble: walldestroyed09g heaps (385 by 440 by 122) about 330 apart along the west side of the street at x -2032 (y 5276, 5592, 5928) on rubble ground 8 to 23 high; a broken front beside it shows its floor at 388 (264 over the street) with concrete_section128floor and wall pieces at the break and rebar clusters (rebar_cluster001a 7, 002b 4) poking out of each.
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's d3_c17_10a streets are flat (crown 0 in 82 of 84 cuts) with roads of 384 (stonefloor006d_c17) and pavements of 128 (concretefloor028c_c17), kerbs 4.
- d3_c17_10b: inside the Overwatch Nexus, an old bank or government block the Combine have grown into (in the frame of d3_c17_10a, 11 and 12 it is the block east of the park, x 32 to 2304, y 4512 to 7884); 96% of the nodes indoors: ceilings 192 (145 of 350), 240 (54) and 256 (43); corridors 133 wide, rooms 226 by 768 (medians); 0.41 props per 100 by 100; walls banded in 93% of tall columns (tilefloor011a below plasterwall043c, 043c below 043b), the first change 16 or 64 over the foot.
- d3_c17_10b Combine architecture: the Combine's part is brushwork, not props: metalcombine001 on 4639 faces (the most used material, area 668 x 10000 against the old plaster's 762), 921 brushes, most of them thin plates (2 thick 356 times, 8 thick 167, 1 thick 104; longest side median 64); 14% of their faces off the right angles (the old building's plaster 4%), leaning 45 to 70 degrees from flat and turned 25, 45 or 65 degrees in plan; it fills the middle of the block (x 3072 to 4352, y -1024 to 0, 128 to 768 up) and an upper floor (x 2560 to 3584, y -2304 to -1536, 512 to 944 up).
- d3_c17_10b machines: combine_generator01 31, set in rows along walls 32 to 96 apart (x 2880, y -1280 to -1504; y 509, x 2629 to 2821); cell_01_pod 7 in rows of two and three 40 to 44 apart; consoles as a combine_interface001 or 002 with a combine_intmonitor001 or 003 on it (8 sets); turrets 8 on the ground and 6 on ceilings; prison_flourescentlight001a 39 and combine_light001a 11; a Breen bust in pairs (2, 372 apart); force fields (comshieldwall2) across the courtyard's ways, each between a combine_fence01a and a 01b post 256 apart (combine_fence01a and 01b 5 each).
- Ours: rooms 120 to 128 high in City 18's two tones with a dado rail. Valve's Nexus keeps the old building's rooms (192 high, plaster banded over tile) and sets the Combine's metal inside them as a second skin of thin, tilted plates, so the Combine reads as grown in, not built from scratch.
- d3_c17_11: out of the Nexus over its roof and down into the streets (the frame of d3_c17_10a); half the nodes outdoors, most of them on roofs: ceilings indoors 176 (35 of 120) and 128 (20); corridors 128 wide; no kerbed street cut at all (walls median 96 tall: parapets); fronts 64% of wall area (building_template017b, 029j, 029i).
- d3_c17_11 Nexus roof: flat roofs at 960 with parapets, a Combine post on them: a health charger and a suit charger side by side (28 apart) on one wall, ammo crates (item_item_crate) stacked three, substation_transformer01b (5, 192 apart in a pair), vent_large ducts turning over the roof, combine_mine on the lower roof at 905; combine_bridge walkways (6) link the roofs 560 apart; CombineInnerwallCluster1024 props (12) ring the area as the far Combine wall.
- d3_c17_11 checkpoint: nine combine_barricade_med01a 64 apart in one straight row from wall to wall across a street 576 wide (x 912, y 3744 to 4256), no gap left; d3_c17_12 has the same row as ten combine_barricade_med01b 63 apart (x 896).
- d3_c17_12: back through the park before the Nexus after the battle (the same ground as d3_c17_10a): 126 displacements (68 floors, 36 walls, 22 ceilings; power 2 in 83%), 16% of floor area; shell craters about 250 across and up to 190 deep in the paved ways (x -2232 to -1984 at y 7072, floor at -64 under a street at 128); wrecked Combine APCs (combine_apc_wheelcollision 5); rebel barricades of barricade_short04a (12), often in pairs splayed like a V (yaws 120 and 240, 166 apart; 75 and 105, 208 apart), and concrete_debris256barricade001a (4).
- d3_c17_12 rebel post: a shop on the park's west street blocked up from inside: dumpsters (trashdumpster02, two trashdumpster02b on it at 308), file cabinets stacked two high (filecabinet02 4, controlroom_filecabinet002a), wall001a_base boards over the broken windows (windowdestroyed01a, 01d), and behind them a table with a monitor, keyboard and hard drives: the rebels' radio desk.
- Ours: streets keep a crown, a kerb of 8 and pavements of 96. Valve's d3_c17_12 park streets are flat with kerbs of 4 and the craters cut straight through road and pavement.
- d3_c17_10a and 12, the Nexus from outside: an old civic block (about x -444 to 1700, y 4500 to 7900) facing the park; a portico 256 proud in the middle of its park front (columns, steps up 8 by 12 from the plaza), storeys in building_template029 (g, f, d, b, then a cornice of 029j stepping out 32 at 800), a metal mansard roof (metalroof008a) to 960 and a lower middle wing at 896. The Combine's mark outside is props: CombineInnerwall plates (001a 306 by 296 by 999, 001c 90 by 253 by 805, some leaning 5 degrees) set into the front from the ground to 1025, two more standing on the roof to 1441 and 1601; inside it is brushwork (d3_c17_10b).
- d3_c17_12b: the last ruined block and the road under it (striders outside): 70% of the nodes indoors: ceilings 184 (96 of 275), 120 (59) and 384 (40); floors 192 apart (-320, -192, 0, 192, 384); 0.24 props per 100 by 100, physics tilted 50% (bins, tyres, trash), statics touching a wall 73%; walls banded in 70% of tall columns (tilewall006c below 006a, concretewall032a below plasterwall021a), the first change 128 or 120 over the foot.
- d3_c17_12b broken block: its front blown off and the floors left open like a doll's house: building_brokenexterior002c (8) and 002a (2), each 40 by 257 by 128, stand along the open edge, concrete_section floor and wall pieces (64 and 128) at the breaks, rebar (rebar003c_64 9, rebar_cluster001a 6) out of them; strip lights (lights_florescent01a 10) and sprinkler pipes 512 long (prison_sprinkler001a 9) on the ceilings; the rebels hold it with barricade_short04a (8) and barricade_tall02a (6).
- d3_c17_12b road tunnel: tiled walls in bands (tilewall006c below 006a, 006d), a floor of concretefloor013a, cage lights; dumpsters and garbage piles (trashdumpster01a, garbage256_composite002b) against the walls.
- styleguide_c17_rubble: Valve's style room for City 17 in ruins (a sample, not a level): 30 props in play and no lamps lit (sun '213 152 85 500' at pitch -25, ambient '154 123 61', fog '181 146 121' from 100 to 5000: a warm dusty haze); the ruin is made of big models (collapsedbuilding01awall 4, collapsedbuilding02c 2, building_brokenexterior002a, b and c 6, concrete_debris256pile001a 2, parliament_dome_destroyed_exterior, project_destroyedbuildings01) on displacement ground (21 displacements, still in dev_blendmeasure on 414 faces); lampposts 801 apart along the road, a car, a tanker and a truck cab left on it.
- styleguide_c17_rubble skeleton: a block stripped to floors every 128 (0 to 512) on posts 32 by 32 and 512 tall (8), with a broken-exterior model (40 by 257 by 128, one storey tall) hung on each open floor edge; d3_c17_12b uses the same models on floors 192 apart.
- styleguide_c17_rubble_2: a ruined street corner (813 Hammer-hidden objects left out, among them 295 path nodes): 219 props in play, 46 physics (33% of them lying: boards, crates, chairs); wires on utility poles (utilitypole02b 11, 856 apart): 17 spans, median 816 long, slack 100, width 2; fronts 39% of wall area (courtyard_template003f, building_template017o, storefront_template001g); walls banded in 52% of tall columns, the first change 128 or 256 over the foot; 48 displacements; sun '219 191 148' at pitch -25, fog from 100 to 8000.
- styleguide_c17_rubble_2 ruined shop: a corner shop open to the street (display_cooler01a 4, kitchen_fridge001a 3, propane canisters 3, chairs knocked over) under broken floors: concrete_section64floor001b and 001c pieces (24) along each break, rebar (rebar001c_64 6, rebar003c_64 4) out of them, walldestroyed09c (4) and debris piles (128 and 256 across) under the holes; bottles and cans (garbage_glassbottle 12, popcan01a 5) scattered as physics props.

## Valve's Route Kanal (d1_canals_01 to 13) (`_dev/reference/valve_canals/`)

Valve's own Route Kanal (d1_canals_01, 01a, 02, 03, 05 to 13; the Source SDK copies the owner uploaded, 21 November), measured from the VMFs and drawn by map_view.py (R7 to R20) or as true slices across the channel (R1 to R6: dark grey solid, white air, blue water, pale blue sky, a grid every 128). Valve's maps are reference only, for shape and dressing: their VMFs are not in the repo, and the owner's own canal stays dry.

- How measured: the 13 Route Kanal VMFs (d1_canals_01, 01a, 02, 03, 05 to 13) were cut across at 1,283 places (from path nodes and water brushes, 448 apart), each cut a true slice 4 units fine through world, detail and displacement geometry; 372 cuts are open canal (sky above, long both ways), 281 tunnels. Heights below are over the lowest floor of the cut; widths between the walls.
- d1_canals_01: from the train station's back yard; the first canal runs beside a railway, a narrow wet channel inside a wider dry one; a dry channel 224 to 256 wide and 256 to 320 deep at the start; train tunnels, the junkyard and a boxcar hideout (a citizen and a vortigaunt); 18 Civil Protection.
- d1_canals_01a: the canal of the outfalls: 512 between walls, water 168 deep at the middle; train cars fallen into it by the road bridges, barnacle tunnels (21 barnacles), a pipe chamber, citizens massacred by the cops.
- d1_canals_02: the helicopter's slide tunnels (d1_canals.heli_slide_tunnels): dirty water (water_lowdirty001) about 20 deep, 72 keyframe ropes and 16 barnacles.
- d1_canals_03: underground: the drain room, the water puzzle room, a steam tunnel, 138 breakables (the boards the cops come through); d1_canals.general_dripping_tunnel 9 times.
- d1_canals_05: the slime: toxicslime002a 20 to 32 deep in 512 wide channels and a wide basin; the Resistance shanty where the airboat is handed over (5 citizens); 64 lights, the most of any of these maps.
- d1_canals_06: the first airboat run: open canals 1024 wide with walls 256 high, box culverts 512 by 248, the river (canals_river), a red barn, an infested pipe.
- d1_canals_07: docks and the floodgate base: canals 1024 wide, box tunnels 704 wide and 288 to 352 high, 34 Civil Protection, 22 point_spotlight.
- d1_canals_08: the floodgates and a warehouse: the deep canal with leaning walls (784 wide at the floor, 1008 at the top, 476 deep), lock gates with fishing boats in the lock, round pipes 384 across.
- d1_canals_09: the entry tunnel and slime tunnels: round pipes 344 to 384 across; almost empty of loose things (22 physics props, 3 path nodes).
- d1_canals_10: the banked canal (45 degree banks 284 high), bridges with their decks' undersides 380 to 420 over the floor, a warehouse; a hunter-chopper overhead.
- d1_canals_11: the Resistance station where the airboat gets its gun (2 citizens, a vortigaunt, a curved roofed hut), the puzzle arena, slime and murky water channels.
- d1_canals_12: canals among wooden and metal docks (canals_canal_water_creaking_wood, _metal), a deep channel 1024 wide with water 128 deep, three pairs of lock gates; 85 decals, the most of any of these maps.
- d1_canals_13: the reservoir and the island under the dam's floodgate: the deep channel (1024 wide, leaning walls, water 128 deep), two pairs of lock gates, 520 displacement faces, a vortigaunt at the end.
- Cross-section, city canal (01, 01a; 36 open cuts): width 128 up most often 512 (11 cuts) or 448 (8), from 256 to 704; the wall top, where the channel opens out for good, 384 (320 to 472); the floor is a dish, reaching the wall line 44 up (8 to 81); water where there is any 92 deep at the middle (60 to 106), 168 in 01a.
- Cross-section, airboat canals (06 to 13; 318 open cuts): width 128 up most often 1024 (70 cuts), then 896 (23), 832 (21), 512 (20), 704 (19); wall tops 512 (68 cuts) or 256 (64), median 456 (275 to 508); the floor dishes up to the walls, reaching them 90 up (36 to 120); water 34 deep at the middle (28 to 95).
- Cross-section, slime canal (05; 11 cuts): 512 wide channels (4 cuts) with walls 240 to 256, a wide basin (1600 to 2048); slime 28 deep (15 to 32).
- Typical profiles, measured exactly: 01a x -3744 y 9088 (R1): 512 between upright walls 452 high, the floor a dish 88 deep (104 wide 8 up, 288 wide 64 up). 07 x 8052 y 8192 (R3): 1024 between upright walls 252 high, the floor dishing 36 to 48 over the outer 250 (8 to 10 degrees), water 32. 08 x -8204 y -4096 (R4): 784 at the floor, 1008 at 448 up, walls leaning back 14 degrees, a step of 36 at 352 and a coping, 476 deep. 10 x 13312 y -1248 (R5): a floor 256 wide with upright sides 32 high, banks at about 45 degrees rising 284 over 312, a berm at 316 and an upright wall to 508.
- Two channels in one (01 x 704, y -1300 to 2200, R2): a wet channel 400 to 440 wide and 184 to 232 deep (its dish and its water, 52 to 106 deep, deepen along the stretch), with shelves at its top (164 on one side, 36 on the other) and the upper walls standing back to 540 to 600.
- Banks: true 45 degree banks are rare (the banked canal in 10); most walls stand upright from a dished floor. Ledges a player can stand on (32 wide or more, 72 clear over them): in the city canal 72 wide (42 to 106) at 212 up (146 to 256), on at least one side in 28 of 36 cuts; in the airboat canals 102 wide (64 to 148) at 316 up (244 to 492), on at least one side in 200 of 318 cuts.
- How the profile changes: in 01 the dish under the water narrows from 400 to 124 and widens again to 304 within 2,500 units; the city canal goes from 440 (01) to 512 (01a); in the airboat maps one canal steps between 1024, 832 and 512 and its walls between 256 and 512. Where a canal enters a culvert, the mouth is a hole 252 to 260 high and 512 wide (896 in 08 and 09) in a wall 48 to 80 thick rising to 504 to 512 (R12, R9).
- Tunnels and culverts (128 cuts of tunnels up to 760 wide): round pipes 384 across and 352 to 384 high (19 cuts), 320 across (7), 448 by 288 (5); box culverts 512 wide by 256 high (9) or 224 (4), their floors dished 64 into the walls (R6); 512 by 320 with a rounded floor (8); arches 320 wide and 256 to 352 high (9); big box tunnels 704 wide and 288 to 352 high (7); 384 by 320 boxes (4). Small openings are closed by canal_bars002 (128 wide, 124 high; 57 placed).
- Drain outfalls: a round hole 128 across running 588 deep into the wall (420 to 966; 58 measured), framed by the canal_cap001 ring (154 across; 65 placed), its middle 139 over the floor in front (93 to 172); they come in groups 383 apart (288 to 768), sometimes a pair either side of the middle (R13); decalconcretedrain002a is the commonest drain stain (17 decals, 11 overlays).
- Bridges (found where a deck crosses the open canal with sky beyond it): 10 street and road bridges, decks 56 to 96 thick (one 124), 80 to 576 along the canal (most 128 to 304), spanning 513 to 896; their undersides 380 to 422 over the floor in the airboat canals (at the walls' tops), 252 to 284 in the city canal (01, 01a). 7 footbridges: 64 to 160 wide, only 4 to 12 thick, 128 to 424 up, spanning 416 to 1072. Bridges carry canal_bridge_railing01 or 02 (32 to 40 high; 108 placed) and 14 canal_bridge_railing_lamps (R14). Where the canal enters a culvert, the wall over the mouth reads as a bridge 214 to 320 thick.
- Ladders and stairs: ladders are metalladder002 or 001 props in pieces of 128 (61 and 13 pieces), most one or two pieces high (128 or 256), the longest 640; the 27 func_useableladder climb 192 (127 to 238). Every brush step measured (205) rises 8 and runs 12.
- Fences and railings at the top: mostly fence props: fence03a (108 high, 260 long; 190 placed, end to end 258 apart) and exterior_fence002e (152 high, 512 long; 171 placed), their feet on the wall tops (fence03a about 480 over the canal floor, 351 to 626); handrail04_long (40 high, 128 long; 114 placed) along walks, most about 130 over the canal floor. Brush rails too (metalrail013a/b/c on 1,292 faces) on walks and stairs; brush chain link (metalfence004a, 205 faces) is rare.
- Locks: a pair of lock gates is locks_large and locks_large_b (each leaf 205 thick, 446 wide, 572 high), set 441 apart so the pair closes about 880; pairs stand 5,185 to 7,300 apart, the length of a lock chamber (08, 12, 13; R15, R18).
- Lights: the 13 maps hold only 208 light, 315 light_spot and 68 point_spotlight. In the canals: light_cagelight02_on 100 times, 311 over the canal floor (233 to 342), nearest neighbour 811 (581 to 956), and 99 of the 100 have their own light within 96; the light entities' brightness 70 (25 to 100), light_spot 1200 (500 to 1850); colours warm 346, cool 126, white 51. prison_lamp001b (19) hang 314 up, 224 apart; lights_florescent01a (38) about 125 up.
- Commonest wall materials in the canals (by area of faces within the channels): concretewall074a 8%, 004c 8%, 010c 7%, 074c 7%, 022a 5%, 022b 5%, 004a 4%, 010b 3%, 010a 3%, 064b 3%, 032a 3%, 004b 3%, stone/stonewall051a 3%, concretewall061a 2%, 046i 2%, 073b 2%, 074b 2%, 023b 1%, 061b 1%, wood/woodfloor001a 1%.
- Commonest floor materials in the canals: concrete/concretefloor018a 17%, nature/dirtfloor012a 14%, nature/blendsandweed005a 9%, concretewall074a 6%, concretefloor013a 4%, concretewall004b 4%, 004c 4%, nature/sandfloor005a 3%, concretefloor010a 3%, 031a 2%, concretewall004a 2%, concretefloor006a 2%, 038a 2%, 039a 2%, 025a 1%, concretewall022b 1%, 074c 1%, 032a 1%, 051a 1%, 022a 1%; sloped faces mostly concretewall004b (36%). Water: water_canals_water2 (243 faces), toxicslime002a (128).
- Commonest models: oildrum001 264, fence03a 190, shrub_01a 190, exterior_fence002e 171, oildrum001_explosive 141, driftwood_01a 132, tree_deciduous_card_01 130, handrail04_long 114, ivy_01 110, driftwood_02a 106, wood_crate002a 103, light_cagelight02_on 100, rebar001c_64 97, cargo_container01 93, wood_crate001a 81, tree_deciduous_01a 75, concrete_debris256pile001a 74, canal_bridge_railing01 72, concrete_barrier001a 69, canal_cap001 65.
- Dressing: 178 to 541 prop_static and 22 to 194 prop_physics a map. Within 224 of the canal's middle line: about 4 fixed and 4 loose props per 1024 of city canal, but only about 1 per 1024 in the airboat canals (the boat's lane kept clear; the rest stands on the edges).
- Decals and overlays: 613 infodecal and 75 info_overlay; commonest ivy02 92, trashdecal01a 90, ivy01 69, trashdecal02a 31, ivy03 26, decalstain002a 17, decalconcretedrain002a 17, lambdaspray_2a 14 (and 5 overlays: the Resistance's way marks, 1 to 5 a map), graffiti001b/c/d 31, decalsigndanger001e 9; overlays: decalconcretedrain002a 11, slimeoverlay001a 8, decalstain006a 6, sign_gate5 5.
- Soundscapes: 173 env_soundscape (8 to 24 a map) and 173 ambient_generic. Names: canals_slime_tunnel 17, canals_river 12, d1_canals.general_dripping_tunnel 13, canals_slime_outside 10, canals_canal_water_industrial 10, d1_canals_barnacle_tunnel 7, d1_canals_cop_alerted 7, d1_canals_pipe_chamber 6, canals_canal_water 4, canals_tunnel_dry 4, canals_tunnel_wet 3, canals_canal_nowater 1, d1_canals.floodgate_base_ambience 5.
- Ours: the canal is 512 between walls everywhere (v -256 to 256). Valve's city canal is the same (512 the commonest, 448 next), but its airboat canals are 1024, and Valve changes the width from stretch to stretch (320 to 1216); ours keeps one section so modules chain.
- Ours: walls 384 high to the rim (z 400 to 784) with a trim at 256 and a fence on top. Valve's city canal walls 384 (320 to 472) and its airboat canals 256 or 512: our height is Valve's city height.
- Ours: a flat floor with a 45 degree bank (128 out, 128 up) along every wall (the owner asked for the sloped sides). Valve dishes the floor instead: 44 to 90 of fall from the walls to the middle, 8 to 10 degrees in the wide canals; its 45 degree banks are rare (10 only) and much bigger (284 high).
- Ours: no ledge along a plain canal. Valve puts a walk on at least one side in most stretches (28 of 36 city cuts, 200 of 318 airboat cuts), 72 to 102 wide at 212 to 316 up, with handrail04 rails.
- Ours: the canal is dry with mud (the owner's own canal is dry; keep it). Valve's are wet: 92 deep in the city canal, 34 in the airboat canals, slime 28.
- Ours: drain stubs 48 across at 176 up, one per wall per 1024, a stain decal under each. Valve's outfalls are holes 128 across in a canal_cap001 ring, centred 139 up, grouped 383 apart, with decalconcretedrain002a under them.
- Ours: caged lamps (light_cagelight02) on every third rib, 1536 apart at 260 up, a third dead, light 60. Valve's are 811 apart (581 to 956) at 311 up, each lit one with its own light (brightness 70 typical).
- Ours: the fence on the rim is brushes (metalfence007a). Valve's are mostly props: fence03a (108 high) and exterior_fence002e (152 high) end to end on the wall tops.
- Ours: walls concretewall064a, floor concretefloor026a, trim concretewall036a. Valve's canal walls mix many: concretewall074a, 004a/b/c, 010a/b/c, 022a/b, 064b (3%), stonewall051a; floors concretefloor018a, dirtfloor012a, blendsandweed005a; slopes concretewall004b.
- Ours: a plain stretch scatters about 2.4 odds and ends per 1024 (L / 420 from CANAL_JUNK). Valve's city canal holds about 8 props per 1024 near its middle (half of them loose), its airboat canals about 1 (kept clear), the rest on the edges; oil drums, crates, pallets, tyres, driftwood, shrubs and ivy are the commonest.
- Ours: the gate (culvert) is 512 by 256, the same as Valve's box culverts (512 by 224 to 256). Valve also uses round pipes 384 across and arches 320 wide; small ones closed by canal_bars002.
- Ours: m.bridge carries a street at the trim (656), its underside 216 over the floor, 168 under our wall top, 48 thick. Valve's city canal bridges are close to that (undersides 252 to 284, 64 to 96 thick); its airboat canals' bridges sit on the wall tops (380 to 422); its footbridges are thin (4 to 12) and narrow (64 to 160).
- Ours: m.steps rise 16 and run 24 (the owner's choice for the long flights). Valve's canal steps all rise 8 and run 12.
- Ours: grime kinds stain, drain, trash, crack, poster, danger, graffiti, number, lambda and others; no ivy. Valve's commonest decal is ivy (ivy01 to 03, 187 of 613) and trash (trashdecal01a/02a, 121).
- Ours: the plain canal names one soundscape (d1_trainstation.QuietCourtyard). Valve's canal maps use 8 to 24 each, from HL2's own canal set: canals_river, canals_canal_water(_industrial), canals_canal_nowater, canals_slime_tunnel/_outside, canals_tunnel_dry/_wet, d1_canals.general_dripping_tunnel.

## Valve's Citadel (d3_citadel_01 to 05, d3_breen_01, ep1_intro, ep1_citadel_00 to 04, and two style rooms) (`_dev/reference/valve_citadel/`)

The Combine's own stronghold, in Half-Life 2 and Episode One: the pod bays and their rings of pods, the shafts the pod rides cross, the confiscation field, the energy ball rooms, the walkway reactors, Breen's office and the dark-energy core, and the Citadel falling apart in Episode One; with Valve's style rooms for the core and the destroyed Citadel. Chosen for the Combine's odd, lopsided build (the owner's special interest), the pods' rails, the core and the walkways. Measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_NAME/measure.json; the logic and NPC study in valve_citadel/notes/logic_npc_notes.md; the architecture study in valve_citadel/notes/architecture_*) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic; big hollow models and fences drawn as outlines). Many Citadel brushes reach thousands of units below the play floors, which shrinks a framed cut-away to a sliver; those views are drawn from a scratch copy of the VMF without the brushes reaching below a set height (said in each 'where'). d3_citadel_01's own set (C1 to C6) stays in valve_d3_citadel_01/.

- d3_citadel_01: 733 entities, 1859 brushes, 110 static props, 29 dynamic, 2 physics; 13 AI nodes only (the player rides); 22 func_tracktrain on 46 path_tracks: two rings of 7 prisoner pods (4749 and 4549 long) that stop together, a ring of 3 pods (6858 long, 46 s a lap) and three diggers started 1, 4 and 6 s apart. The map reaches from z -14336 to 3104. Ours: no Combine machine moves on its own anywhere in our map.
- d3_citadel_02: 1120 entities, 3241 brushes, 387 static props; 25 AI nodes; the player's pod ride is 56 path_tracks and 24581 units, about 147 s at 100 to 250, with 13 stops that set something off (a thing to see about every 11 s); 3 container trains on one ring of 15206 (72 s a lap), 2 razor trains of 7488; 6 stalkers, 6 soldiers, 3 striders, 5 scanners, all seen from the pod.
- d3_citadel_03: 1564 entities, 4877 brushes; 245 AI nodes (indoor ceilings 192 to 320 in half the rooms, up to 2815 in the shafts); 25 soldiers (5 elites), 17 of them in point_templates; 8 energy ball spawners, 7 shield walls, the confiscation field, 12 consoles that tear off the wall, 26 spark timers; materials 97% metal by area (citadel_metalwall063a and 077a).
- d3_citadel_04: 2297 entities; 58 soldiers (12 elites), 37 of them in point_templates, 5 env_entity_makers; 37 assault rally and 37 assault points; 50 timers (42 for sparks); the walkway reactors in the stasis room (three pairs of energy cores at x 144 to 160, y -1464, -1728, -2104, z 6377 to 6767, each with a trigger_vphysics_motion of gravity 0 and a trigger_physics_trap); the lift wait's waves capped at 4 soldiers alive.
- d3_citadel_05: 1224 entities; a ring of 7 pods (10 path_tracks, 4058 long, 7 stops), 4 more on a ring of 6931; the player's pod climbs one shaft from z -808 to 3788 and then the Citadel's height from -15328 to 10304 at up to 1000 (about 25 s); one pod carries a stalker past at 70; two crab synths walk the gunship bay 1.5 s after the pod passes; 3 stalkers on act busy hints.
- d3_breen_01: 2027 entities; 43 choreographed scenes (Breen's office chain of six, seven mocking lines on the climb, Alyx's lines); 16 soldiers (9 elites); Breen's countdown of 190 s spoken by Overwatch at 3, 2, 1 min, 45, 30, 15, 10 s; Breen's lift (path_breen_lift, 1552) and the main lift (5 stops, 1424) up the portal chamber; the office holds breendesk, breenbust, breenconsole, masterinterface, 3 breenwindow and 4 combinebuttress models, 12 cell pods.
- ep1_intro: 443 entities, 624 brushes, 16 dynamic props, 7 AI nodes: a cinematic built as closed sets for the camera; the blast is a sprite whose scale is multiplied by 1.0065 every 0.05 s from 8; debris on tracktrains at 170 to 2000. No picture: its views show only the camera sets.
- ep1_citadel_00: 1942 entities, 4063 brushes, 181 static and 56 physics props; 137 AI nodes; the van ride (51 path_tracks, 15156 long, up to 2000), a ring of 7 prisoner pods (7 stops), a razor train ring of 13576; a chunk shot off the Citadel's top every 6 to 12 s from 5 env_shooters at z 13532 to 13580.
- ep1_citadel_01: 2522 entities, 5823 brushes, 317 static props, 325 AI nodes; 10 soldiers whose guns dissolve on death, 7 rollermines, 3 stalkers; the confiscation field again; the advisor pod room with 9 docked advisor pods; rumbles every 12 to 49 s.
- ep1_citadel_02: 2299 entities, 4855 brushes, 314 static props, 305 AI nodes; 26 soldiers (8 elites), 16 of them asleep until woken, 13 woken by a comrade's death; 2 stalkers, 2 striders, 6 enemy finders; 5 ball spawners and 3 launchers.
- ep1_citadel_02b: 929 entities, 2217 brushes, 68 AI nodes; the lift up the shaft (13 path_tracks, 4124 long, up to 400) with tremors every 6 to 12 s and one stalker.
- ep1_citadel_03: 3411 entities, 6592 brushes, 214 AI nodes; 59 soldiers (26 elites), 52 asleep until woken; 12 manhacks; 29 energy cores, 7 ball spawners, 9 launchers; the core (prop_coreball at 2687 12142 5161) mended by 6 balls and 3 lasers, its loop changed from unstable to partly stable to stable; 51 timers.
- ep1_citadel_04: 1282 entities, 2895 brushes, 81 dynamic props, 122 AI nodes; 13 soldiers whose guns dissolve on death; the escape train standing still while a ring of spotlights passes it every 1.2 s; the wrecked stalker train.
- styleguide_core: 90 entities, 348 brushes, no AI nodes: the core alone in its shaft, its wobble on three timers of 0.2 to 0.9 s.
- styleguide_destroyed_citadel: 960 entities, 2888 brushes, 138 AI nodes; ep1_citadel_02's bridges as a sample with 9 soldiers (7 elites) and 8 stalkers; rumbles every 12 to 49 s.

| # | File | Where | What it teaches | Lessons |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Z1 | `valve_citadel/01_c01_pod_bay_cut.png` | d3_citadel_01, the pod bay where the two rings of prisoner pods run, from the south-west, cut at -1350 (box 9000 3600 11500 6000; drawn from a copy without brushes reaching below -2600 or above 0) | The pod bay is not a room but Combine machinery hung in a void: slabs and boxes at different heights, none mirrored, the pods' rails threaded between them on posts and stepped walkways, thin supports (orange) dropping far below. Two rings of 7 pods run here, 14 and 9 path_tracks long, each pod stopping at its own stop and the ring moving on 3 s after the last arrives. Ours: our Combine pieces are props set square on a street; nothing hangs or moves. | valve-machines-on-a-beat, valve-combine-lean, valve-pod-racks |
| Z2 | `valve_citadel/02_c01_zapper_station_cut.png` | d3_citadel_01, the 'zapper' stop on the pod ring (the pod halts between two energy cores at z -1871 and -1567), from the south-west, cut at -1450 (box 10200 3700 11500 4800; drawn from the same copy) | One station on the ring does a job: the rail runs out on a walkway cantilevered off a box, the box hangs over a drop on bundles of thin legs, and the pod stops in a beam between two energy cores for 6 s (warm-up at 2.5 s, discharge, beam and sparks at 4 s, all off 6 s later). Lopsided in plan and in section: the walkway leaves one face, the legs hang under another. Ours: no station of this kind; a Combine processing stop would be new. | valve-machines-on-a-beat, valve-combine-lean |
| Z3 | `valve_citadel/03_c02_pod_ride_overview.png` | d3_citadel_02, the whole pod ride from the south-west (box -6600 3500 4000 13400; drawn from a copy without brushes reaching below 1000 or above 5200) | The player's pod ride as a ribbon of rail laid over the tops of the Citadel's blocks: it doubles back on itself, climbs and drops (z 1754 to 4404), and the blocks under it are each a different height and depth, stepped and set off one another, never a mirrored pair. 24581 units in about 147 s with 13 stops that set something off. Ours: no ride; our Combine quarter is walked on streets. | valve-lift-diorama, valve-combine-lean |
| Z4 | `valve_citadel/04_c02_shaft_from_the_pod.png` | d3_citadel_02, eye in the pod's seat near the start of the ride (eye -2560 4200 3140, looking north-east, yaw 60, 8 degrees down); same copy | What the rider sees: the rail runs off as a thin beam into the dark, the pod arrays (Citadel_pods and cell pods, cyan outlines, 4 and 28 in the map) hang across the shaft at angles of their own, and the far walls are lost in the dark. The scale and the lack of a floor are the point. Ours: nothing in our maps hangs over a void; a Combine shaft would be new. | valve-pod-racks, valve-combine-lean |
| Z5 | `valve_citadel/05_c03_stasis_room.png` | d3_citadel_03, the stasis room, eye level looking south-east (eye 4192 520 2113, yaw 315); the auto view of map_ingest.py views | A round Combine room around a field: the stasisfield model (orange outline, centre 4513 225 2562) over a stasisshield on the floor, three energy cores on one vertical line (z 2080, 2432, 2928, scales 2, 3 and 4, always on), a ring of rail, and columns of pipe 4000 tall (orange) rising past it from far below. The walls are flat plates turned at slightly different angles round the ring, not a true circle. Two squads of four soldiers (green) hold it. Ours: no Combine room built round a machine. | valve-combine-lean, valve-combine-cold, valve-npc-assault-lanes |
| Z6 | `valve_citadel/06_c03_ball_room_cut.png` | d3_citadel_03, the first energy ball room (dispensers at 3336 to 3496, -48, 2304; the socket holding shield wall 3 at 3532 140 2446), from the south-west, cut at 2700 (box 2900 -700 4100 800; drawn from a copy without brushes reaching below 1200 or above 4200) | The room hangs on a frame of struts set at angles, not posts: square tubes run off diagonally under the floor and end in the void. Three dispensers of 10 balls each (respawn 4 s) stand in a row 80 apart; one socket of a single ball powers the shield wall: pull the ball and the wall drops, put it back and it rises (with a 0.1 s kill pulse). Ours: nothing in our maps is powered by a thing you can carry. | valve-ball-socket-power, valve-forcefield, valve-combine-lean |
| Z7 | `valve_citadel/07_c04_walkway_reactors_cut.png` | d3_citadel_04, the stasis room with its walkway reactors, from the south-west, cut at 6620 (box -700 -3100 1000 -700; drawn from a copy without brushes reaching below 5600 or above 9000) | The walkways cross the room as a lopsided frame: one arm runs out long to the left, another short to the right, a ring sits off the middle, and each walkway has a reactor in it (the energy cores, scale 3, at y -1464, -1728 and -2104): step in and you float, float to its middle and it kills you. Tall columns (orange) drop from it far below. Soldiers (green) stand off to one side. Nothing in it is mirrored. Ours: no Combine walkway; the reactors are being lifted as a piece by the other helper. | valve-combine-lean, valve-combine-cold |
| Z8 | `valve_citadel/08_c04_pod_array_hall.png` | d3_citadel_04, a catwalk hall past the pod arrays, eye level looking south (eye -1760 -4896 6208, yaw 270); the auto view of map_ingest.py views | A catwalk down a hall whose walls lean in and step out in plates of different widths, cell arrays and pods (yellow and cyan boxes) hung along it, thin rods dropping from the dark overhead to the rail; the far end is a dark slot. The rail and the catwalk are the only straight things. Ours: our corridors are boxes with square walls. | valve-combine-lean, valve-pod-racks |
| Z9 | `valve_citadel/09_c05_synth_hall.png` | d3_citadel_05, the hall the crab synths walk (path_gunshipbay_synths from x 9242 to 7450 along y -3074), eye level looking east (eye 8000 -3072 3352, yaw 0); the auto view of map_ingest.py views | A long Combine hall: a sunken track down the middle, walls of upright ribs of many widths and depths, no two bays alike, a dark round mouth at the far end. The synths' walk is laid down this track: they appear as the pod passes, walk it and are removed at the far end. Ours: no hall of this scale; the ribbed wall is the Combine's rhythm without repeats. | valve-work-shown-in-passing, valve-combine-lean |
| Z10 | `valve_citadel/10_c05_gunship_bay_cut.png` | d3_citadel_05, the gunship bay where the player's pod climbs past (shaft at x 8400, y -5232), from the south-west, cut at 4400 (box 7300 -5800 9800 -1800; drawn from a copy without brushes reaching below 2600 or above 5200) | The bay hangs in the shaft like a growth: plates jut out on one side only, beams leave it at angles, and bundles of pipe (orange) hang beneath it far down. The pod rail climbs straight through it. Ours: our Combine props stand on the ground; nothing of ours hangs. | valve-combine-lean, valve-lift-diorama |
| Z11 | `valve_citadel/11_breen_office_cut.png` | d3_breen_01, Breen's office, from the south-west, cut at 860 (box -2900 -500 -1100 1700; drawn from a copy keeping only brushes between 300 and 1200 and the props inside the box) | Breen's office is a pale floor (the only light floor in the Citadel) set off-centre in Combine metal: the window wall is held by four combinebuttress models (orange) leaning in at an angle, the desk and console on a raised part to one side, a pod frame and Breen's pod at another; the room's sides are not parallel. The talk here is one chain of six scenes with a jump-ahead and an idle loop at the end. Ours: the Administrator's office is a room of our own; Valve's is the Combine's office, not the city's. | valve-combine-lean, valve-overseer-on-screens |
| Z12 | `valve_citadel/12_breen_portal_chamber.png` | d3_breen_01, the portal chamber where Breen's lift climbs to the dark-energy reactor (lifts on path_lift_start from z -1007 to 1364), from the south-west, cut at 1500 (box -1100 -1300 1300 1300; drawn from a copy keeping brushes between -1500 and 2600 and the props inside the box) | The chamber is a tower of balconies stacked unevenly up one side, the other side bare metal, struts leaving its foot at angles over the drop. Breen's lift climbs through it in stops, two of them in step with Overwatch's calls of the time left (at 147 and 162 s). Ours: no tower; the climb as the clock is the idea to keep. | valve-countdown-spoken, valve-progress-in-the-world, valve-combine-lean |

Above it a chunk breaks off the Citadel's top every 6 to 12 s at random (5 env_shooters), with lightning every 4 to 12 s and its thunder 1 and 2 s after. Ours: the endings could break the city's ground this way only with the owner's yes. | valve-place-falling-apart, valve-collapsed-deck, valve-blast-lean |

Ours: our Combine walls are one flat material. | valve-combine-lean, valve-combine-cold |

The core (prop_coreball at 2687 12142 5161) wobbles on three axes on its own timers and its sound tells how far it is mended: unstable, partly stable, stable. Ours: reference for a broken Combine approach. | valve-combine-lean, valve-place-falling-apart |

## Valve's coast (d1_trainstation_06, d2_coast_01 to 11) (`_dev/reference/valve_coast/`)

The rest of Half-Life 2's way out of the city: the train yard and the start of the canal (d1_trainstation_06), then Highway 17 and Sandtraps: the dock post where the buggy is lifted ashore, New Little Odessa (a Resistance village fighting off a gunship), the Combine-held dock and warehouses, the cliff road and its ruined houses, the great bridge and the village under it, the shacks and yards on the shore, the lighthouse the Resistance holds against dropships, and the vortigaunts' camp on the beach past the sand. d2_coast_12 is its own set (valve_d2_coast_12). Measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_NAME/measure.json; the study in valve_coast/notes/notes.md) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic, green NPCs). Most of the coast is displacement ground and sea, so most eye views are of grass and sky: the cut-aways teach more.

- d1_trainstation_06: 240 props in play, 69% of AI nodes outdoors; 4 trains on path_track (54 OnPass > SetSpeed lines) and a level crossing (two red lamps blinking every 0.5 s, a bell for 25 s); Combine force fields across the tracks (five humming pairs at two places); 8 metrocops and 2 scanners chase the player while cop voices placed round the yard (radius 5000) call him out. The canal starts as a concrete tunnel 256 wide under a roof 352 up, with ledges 116 wide at 224 up. Ours: our canal pieces start in the open.
- d2_coast_01: the dock post: a crane (prop_vehicle_crane, npc_cranedriver, phys_magnet) lifts the buggy ashore; 18 item_dynamic_resupply; 9 caged tubes (prison_flourescentlight002b, light_spot 209 228 228 at 166, cone 70) light the post; the sea is 1344 across at the dock; 22 cables span a median 1764 with slack 105; poles 1773 apart along the road. Ours: no vehicles on our maps.
- d2_coast_03 (New Little Odessa): 14 rebels, 35 choreographed scenes, 150 props round the village houses (bunks, mess tables and benches, a radio room of four receivers and a cart of two, a generator trailer, six floodlamps, eleven explosive drums); three rebels come back 10 s after dying from makers under the house; a lookout in a tower sounds a hand siren; the gunship switches among three loops every 7 to 12 s; 11 RPG rounds lie about and the ammo rebel carries more; four thumpers reach 1000. Ours: the Resistance base has no lookout, no siren and no comebacks.
- d2_coast_04: the Combine-held dock: 18 soldiers in four squads named for what they hold (dock, crane, warehouse 1 and 2), a second crane, 49 cables (median span 1052, slack 102), poles 1022 apart, 8 antlion repellent points (radius 200 to 576) over the docks and roads; the walls are cheap metal (metalwall014a, 032a, 070e). Ours: our Combine posts are props on our streets.
- d2_coast_05: no light entity at all (sun only, 400); the road runs 384 wide on gravel (blendgrassgravel001b) under cliffs (cliffface001a); 21 soldiers in a gas station, a ruined house and a rock pile; 8 rollermines; 12 env_explosion and an APC that blows up in steps; 24 func_breakable, most of them window panes. Ours: our streets are paved and lit.
- d2_coast_07 and 08: the bridge: a truss about 1150 wide carries the road deck at 1856 over the sea at 0; under it, catwalks (metalfloor003a) at 1240 to 1792; 9 zones along the deck keep the gunship beside the player; force fields of strips 32 apart with a solid clip block the far end; razor trains (train003) cross overhead; the village at the landward end has 16 house window pairs. Ours: no bridge.
- d2_coast_09: shacks and yards on the shore: wood_fence01a 128 apart (23 of them), cinder blocks, poplars 659 apart; 9 zombies and 3 fast zombies in the houses; rollermines dropped by a dropship (roller_dropship, 5 at a time). Ours: our yards are walled, not fenced in boards.
- d2_coast_10 (the lighthouse): the tower is one spiral stair model 128 high stacked ten times, each turned 180 from the last (z 836 to 1988), the lamp at 2108; round it a farm of wooden houses (woodwall030b windows, a fireplace, a stove, beds) and a barn for the car; 4 dropships and a gunship; rebels come back every 15 s; three rebels' shout counters (to 6 each) call for rockets. Ours: no tower.
- d2_coast_11: the vortigaunts' camp on the beach (7 mattresses, crates, a generator trailer, a cooling tank, 3 vortigaunts) past a sand crossing done on rocks and boards: stepping on sand (env_player_surface_trigger, material 78) wakes antlions 1000 round the player, 5 alive; after the bugbait, antlions turn neutral (env_global antlion_allied). Ours: nothing on sand.

The frame is mostly empty: a brush far below the tunnel widens it (a known viewer fault). Ours: our canal pieces start in the open. | valve-canal-varies, valve-canal-culverts |

## Valve's d1_town_03: Ravenholm, for the infestation quarter (map_raven.py) (`_dev/reference/valve_d1_town_03/`)

Valve's own d1_town_03 (Ravenholm, the town) (the Source SDK copy the owner uploaded, 21 November), measured and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props are boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic, green NPCs; trees and poles drawn as trunk and pole). Reference only: the VMF is not in the repo. It is the reference for the infestation quarter still to be built (the zombie-overrun quarter behind the sealed gate, map_raven.py), not for the Resistance town.

- Things: 117 prop_static, 61 prop_physics, 17 prop_dynamic, 0 prop_door_rotating, 1 prop_ragdoll, 82 infodecal, 2 info_overlay, 21 keyframe_rope and 20 move_rope, 85 func_detail, 40 npc entities (makers included).
- Lights: 8 light (median brightness 8.0), 18 light_spot (median 700.0), 11 point_spotlight, 5 env_sprite; sun "175 230 239 50" at pitch -45, ambient "43 45 57 5"; no fog controller; sky sky_day01_09.
- Sound: 7 env_soundscape (d1_town.ConcreteBuilding 3, d1_town.Street 2, d1_town.WoodBuilding 1, d1_town.CorpseRoom 1), median radius 128; 27 ambient_generic (town.d1_town_03_zo_attack2, town.d1_town_03_metalgrate3, town.d1_town_03_fz_alert_far1, town.d1_town_03_tram_hit1).
- Commonest models: pipe02_straight01_long 17, propane_tank001a 13, pulleywheels_small01 12, tree_deciduous_01a-lod 11, oildrum001_explosive 10, pipe02_connector01 9, handrail04_long 6, light_spotlight01_lamp 6, pipe02_straight01_short 5, utilitypole01b 5, lightbulb01a 5, garbage256_composite002b 5, handrail04_corner 4, metalladder002 4, sawblade001a 4.
- Commonest materials (faces): woodfloor005a 961, plasterwall029d 458, concretefloor013c 403, concretewall032d 342, stonefloor006a 327, plasterwall029a 285, brickwall048i 222, metaltruss022c 221, plasterwall029g 184, brickwall048d 171, brickwall048a 166, rubberroof002a 166, metalwall026a 163, metalwall014a 162, stonewall002a 146, metalwall070a 139, plasterwall029c 132, plasterwall041a 111, metalwall070f 103, metaltruss002a 96.
- Ceilings: floor to ceiling indoors (cast up from 41 AI nodes) is most often 120 (19 of them), then 168 and 152; 74% of the nodes are outdoors; rooms at those nodes measure about 232 by 376 (median, wall to wall).
- Stairs: 17 flights of 3 or more steps; steps rise 8 and tread 12 (147 steps), then 8 by 32 (3); median 10 steps a flight.
- Walls in bands: 82% of wall length over 256 tall changes material at least once; the first change is most often 128 over the wall's foot, then 48 and 16; commonest pairs (below > above): concretewall032d > metalwall070f, brickwall048d > brickwall048a, stonewall016a > plasterwall029c, concretefloor013c > rubberroof002a.
- Cables: 32 rope spans, median 914 long, slack 100, width 2.0.
- Spacing (nearest of the same model): utilitypole01b 887 apart (n 5), tree_deciduous_01a-lod 487 apart (n 11), lampfixture01a 374 apart (n 4), light_spotlight01_lamp 870 apart (n 6), tree_cliff_02a 910 apart (n 3).
- Slopes: 95 sloped faces over 2000 square units; pitch most often 25, 70, 20 degrees; metalwall026a, tileroof003a, metalwall014a.
- How dark: no env_fog_controller at all; the sun is cold and weak ('175 230 239' at 50, where the City 17 maps use '237 218 143' at 800) and the ambient is '43 45 57' at 5 (the city's: 100). The gloom is a dim cold sun and a dusk sky (sky_day01_09), not fog and not a blocked sky.
- Pools of light: 11 point_spotlight, each with a light_spot at the same place (warm '255 245 145' or '254 233 146', 400 to 1000), most hung on light_spotlight01_lamp props on house walls, about 500 apart (nearest neighbour median 506); 8 plain light at brightness 8 fill under them; an env_fire with an orange light ('254 174 10', 300). 22 of the 26 lights are warm.
- Traps lie ready as loose physics props: 13 propane_tank001a, 10 oildrum001_explosive, 4 sawblade001a, one propeller trap (trappropeller_engine, _blade, _lever, a func_rotating); 18 item_healthkit and 6 weapon_frag as rewards.
- The dead: 15 npc_zombie and 2 npc_fastzombie placed, 10 npc_maker and 7 npc_template_maker, 14 aiscripted_schedule walking them at the player (the same machinery map_raven.py uses); 1 prop_ragdoll (corpse1, a pinned zombie) with 5 npc_crow round it; 1 npc_monk on a roof.
- No gore decals: 0 blood decals among 82 infodecal. The ground reads abandoned through trashdecal01a 26, ivy03 19, leaves_ground02 17, plaster020a 5 and a little graffiti; the 2 overlays are scorch marks.
- Streets: no kerbs and no pavements (0 of 119 cross-sections found one); cobbles (stonefloor006a) and displaced ground (36% of the floor area) run house to house; wall to wall most often 640 (384 to 768); house walls along the streets median 352 tall (256 to 512), height over width 0.42.
- Houses: timber and plaster (woodfloor005a is the commonest material, 961 faces; plasterwall029a/c/d/g; brickwall048a/d/i); a concrete plinth (concretewall032d) under plaster or sheet metal, its top most often at 128, 48 or 16; pitched roofs most often 25 degrees (20 to 35) in corrugated metal (metalwall026a, 014a) and tile (tileroof003a, 004b), and steep 65 to 70 degree faces for mansards and the spire.
- Boards: 39 plank brushes (most 4 thick, 8 wide, 112 long, or 4 by 16 by 64) in woodfloor005a and wooddoor015a; 25 timber beams 6 to 16 square.
- Overhead: 21 keyframe_rope and 20 move_rope, spans median 914, slack 100, width 2; utilitypole01b about 887 apart; 12 pulleywheels_small01 and 9 path_track for the cable tram. 74% of the AI nodes are outdoors, many of them on roofs joined by planks and metalladder002 (4).
- Indoors: ceilings most often 120 over the floor (19 of 41 nodes), rooms about 232 by 376; inside walls are one material in 118 of 163 sampled, else a concrete base to 32 or 64 under plaster.
- Sound: four soundscapes (d1_town.Street, .WoodBuilding, .ConcreteBuilding, .CorpseRoom, radius 128) and 27 ambient_generic one-shots placed where a scare happens (zombie attack, a metal grate, a fast zombie's far call, the tram hitting, a small fire loop).
- This part of Ravenholm has no church; its refuges are the rooftops (pictures R4 and R6).

| # | File | Where | What it teaches | Lessons |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | `valve_d1_town_03/01_overview_sw.png` | the whole of this part of Ravenholm from the south-west | Tight blocks of two and three storey houses, every roof a different height and pitch, wires strung over the streets, a cliff and trees behind. | raven-streets-and-houses, skyline-corners |
| R2 | `valve_d1_town_03/02_overview_ne.png` | the same from the north-east | Streets are narrow gaps between houses, no pavements; yards and passages behind; roofs joined by planks into a second way through. | raven-streets-and-houses, raven-rooftop-refuge |
| R3 | `valve_d1_town_03/03_street.png` | a street at eye height looking east (eye -3328 -1064 -3544) | House walls straight onto uneven cobbles, a utility pole and its wires, boards and a ladder on the walls, the street closing in at the far end. | raven-streets-and-houses, valve-overhead-cables, valve-boarded-openings |
| R4 | `valve_d1_town_03/04_rooftops.png` | over the rooftops (eye -2800 -1352 -3416) | The refuge route: plank bridges, ladders and timber scaffolds between roofs, the spire as a landmark; a corpse (red) and the dead (green) below and on the roofs. | raven-rooftop-refuge |
| R5 | `valve_d1_town_03/05_trap_passage.png` | from a walkway down through timber props to the propeller trap (eye -1479 -1385 -3392) | A trap is a place: a narrow passage under leaning timbers where the dead are drawn in, the trap (engine, blade, lever) at its throat. | raven-traps-and-makers |
| R6 | `valve_d1_town_03/06_corpse_alley.png` | down onto the yard of the pinned corpse and the crows (eye -1642 -1328 -3326) | One corpse with crows round it tells the story; the yard is otherwise litter and leaves, seen from a roof. | raven-litter-not-gore, raven-rooftop-refuge |
| R7 | `valve_d1_town_03/07_houses_cut.png` | the houses round the corpse room and the saw blades, cut at 190 over the floor | Small rooms, low ceilings (120), stairs of 8 by 12, furniture on the walls, the corpse room full of the dead waiting. | valve-flat-interiors, raven-traps-and-makers |

## Valve's d1_trainstation_01 (reference only) (`_dev/reference/valve_d1_trainstation_01/`)

Valve's own d1_trainstation_01 (the Source SDK copy the owner uploaded, 21 November), drawn by map_view.py. Valve's map is reference only: its VMF is not in the repo.

- Things: 299 prop_static, 178 prop_physics, 74 prop_dynamic, 116 infodecal, 29 info_overlay, 35 keyframe_rope, 122 func_detail: about as many loose physics bits (bottles, crates, suitcases, boxes, push carts) as fixed props.
- Lights: only 23 light, 40 light_spot and 19 point_spotlight in the whole station: it is dim, lit in pools where Valve wants the eye to go (the way on, a door, a guard).
- Walls in bands: stonewall050c from the floor to 96 (base), 050a to about 192 (body), 050b from 192 to 320 (a band course), 050a again to about 448, 050e at the top: one wall, five courses, each a ledge of its own.
- Rhythm: columns and canopy posts every 368 along the platforms (198 in the narrower halls); ceiling trusses (Ceiling_Truss001a) 20 times over the platforms.
- Stairs: 8 high per step (under a player clip ramp so they walk smooth).
- Sound: 10 env_soundscape, two at each change of place: d1_trainstation.Platform, .Turnstyle, .Interrogation, .QuietCourtyard and general.concrete_quiet.
- Commonest models: outdoor benches 29, ceiling trusses 20, suitcases 20, train seats and racks 18 each, chain-link fence pieces (exterior_fence002b/c/d) 38, file cabinets 13, bottles 13, push carts 13, wooden crates 22, cardboard boxes 11.
- Commonest materials (faces): stonewall050c 1515, 050a 1028, 050b 726, metalwall085a 476, metalwall074a 264, metaltrain001a/b 478, 050e 235, 050d 221, concretewall037d 210, concretewall004a 203, metalwall077a 200, building_trainstation_template001c 174, plasterwall024b 169, metalwall076a 166, concretewall041e 143, metalwall013d 140, metalstair002a 124.

| # | File | Where | What it teaches | Lessons |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| V2 | `valve_d1_trainstation_01/02_overview_ne.png` | the whole station from the north-east | The roofs step down from the station to the courtyard; the walls are banded, not one flat texture. | valve-wall-bands |
