## Valve's Black Mesa East (d1_eli_01, d1_eli_02) (`_dev/reference/valve_eli/`)

Black Mesa East, the Resistance's base dug in under the dam: the way in, the airlock, the lift past the living quarters, the labs, the corridors, the second airlock and the scrapyard, measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_d1_eli_01/measure.json and valve_d1_eli_02/measure.json; the study in valve_eli/wip/notes.md) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size). Views marked floor were drawn with nothing under that height (the base has floors over floors).

- d1_eli_01: 1754 entities, 2599 brushes, 607 props (437 static, 106 physics, 61 dynamic, 8 ragdoll mattresses); 31 choreographed scenes, 18 scripted sequences, 55 relays, 25 func_doors, 10 areaportals; 7 vortigaunts, 5 citizens, Eli, Alyx, Mossman. No text on screen.
- d1_eli_01: one material does most of the work: concretewall002a on 4610 of 15794 faces, and in the lower lab 46% of the wall, 55% of the floor and 50% of the ceiling; the wall changes material first at 112 (422 walls) or 128 (565) of 2412.
- d1_eli_01: the way down is a freight lift (func_tracktrain, speed 65) from z -1214 to -2672, 1458 in about 22 s, past three floors 288 apart (dormitory, kitchen, repairs) that have no AI nodes: seen, never walked.
- d1_eli_01: the airlock is 208 wide, 173 high, 264 between its doors; a guard's booth beside it (115 by 192, ceiling 96) sees the outer door on a monitor; letting someone in takes about 31 s.
- d1_eli_01: 147 pipe props, 70 chain-link fence props, 51 prison lamps and 14 keypads (51 to 54 up beside every door) dress the old concrete; the lower lab is lit pale green (169 205 188, 18 of 43 lights), median brightness 140.
- d1_eli_02: 56 scenes (30 Alyx, 18 the dog), 102 relays, 12 npc_template_makers, 11 env_entity_makers; one env_hudhint is the only text; the attack comes 80 to 180 s into the scrapyard play.

| # | File | Where | What it teaches | Lessons |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | `valve_eli/01_eli01_overview.png` | d1_eli_01, the whole base from the south-west | The base is a stack: the upper hall and the airlock at the top (z -1280), the brick lift shaft (the red tower) dropping 1458 past three small floors of quarters to the labs and corridors at z -2736; the green block is the ground over it. Ours: our Resistance sites lie flat or go down by stairs (the forest bunker: 15 steps to z 296, then 23 to the deep level at z 104); none is reached by a lift. | valve-lift-diorama |
| E2 | `valve_eli/02_eli01_airlock_cut.png` | d1_eli_01, the outer airlock from the south-west, cut at -1120, nothing under -1300 drawn (box -260 2540 260 2960) | The airlock: an arched concrete tunnel 208 wide and 264 between its two blast doors (cyan, two leaves each), the scanner between them and the guard's booth on the right with its window onto the tunnel. Ours: the forest bunker's way in is one pair of sliding silo doors (256 by 192, 4.6 s) opened from inside, with no second door, booth or camera. | valve-base-airlock, valve-turrets-follow-by-cells |
| E3 | `valve_eli/03_eli01_kitchen_floor.png` | d1_eli_01, the kitchen floor (z -1872), eye 480 2300 -1812 looking south toward the lift's gate (yaw 270) | One of the floors the lift passes: counters and stoves (yellow and orange) along the walls, fridges, the middle clear, the lift's gate (cyan) at the far end where riders see in; two vortigaunt cooks work here only while the lift goes by. Ours: no base kitchen yet; the town's flats have kitchens lit by the owner's tube, the bunker and the cave office a bunk room each. | valve-base-quarters, valve-base-at-work, valve-lift-diorama |
| E4 | `valve_eli/04_eli01_lower_corridor.png` | d1_eli_01, the lower corridor toward the second airlock, eye -656 1192 -2624 looking north (yaw 90) | An old vaulted concrete tunnel dressed with what was carried in: pipe props (orange) run along the curved wall and the ceiling, crates and pallets stand at the side, chain-link frames and cage lamps; the floor in the middle is clear. Ours: the bunker's rooms are plain-walled with silo doors and steady lights; no pipe runs or fence partitions yet. | valve-base-concrete-and-salvage |
| E5 | `valve_eli/05_eli02_scrapyard_cut.png` | d1_eli_02, the scrapyard from the south-west, cut at -2300, nothing under -2760 drawn (box -2700 -1000 -300 1700) | The scrapyard is an open pit about 1240 by 2200 under cliffs, its floor strewn with junk to throw (99 props, half physics: drums, crates, pallets, washers, a dryer, sinks, a tyre, a carousel), five light towers on stands, fence pieces and a gate to the airlock; a ledge 130 up by the gate is the first test. Ours: no yard of junk at our sites, and no job game teaches the gravity gun; the forest bunker's valley (4096 square inside a 440 ridge) is the nearest open ground behind a Resistance door. | valve-play-teaches, valve-teach-then-give-up |
| E6 | `valve_eli/06_eli02_scrapyard.png` | d1_eli_02, the scrapyard at eye level, eye -1901 -323 -2624 looking north (yaw 90) | From the floor of the pit: the cliff all round, the light towers (16 spotlight beams, 159 237 215, median 450) standing over a floor kept mostly clear, crates and pallets in small heaps at the sides, the base's doors in the far wall. Ours: our camps are lit by the city's lamps and fires; no light towers. | valve-play-teaches, valve-raid-on-the-base |
| E7 | `valve_eli/07_eli02_airlock2_cut.png` | d1_eli_02, the second airlock and the gravity gun room from the south-west, cut at -2530, nothing under -2700 drawn (box -950 760 -300 1320) | The way out to the scrapyard: the airlock (blast doors in cyan, 272 apart, 208 wide), its own guard's booth on the west side (x -720, monitors angled at the seat), and beyond it the room where the gravity gun waits in a lit glass case and junk (yellow) lies ready for the first lesson. The same airlock is where the attack shuts the doors behind the player and the monitor breaks to static. Ours: as E2, no airlock. | valve-base-airlock, valve-raid-on-the-base, valve-prison-cctv |
| E8 | `valve_eli/08_eli02_raven_tunnel.png` | d1_eli_02, the tunnel west toward Ravenholm, eye -1812 1912 -2672 looking east back to the base (yaw 0) | A long service tunnel (192 wide, 176 high, 1717 long) with a pipe run along it; drawn with the attack's red emergency lamps (255 55 45) lit, as they blink in the attack (on 1.2 s, off 0.7 s; the viewer draws lamps that start dark as lit, so in play they are off until then). Ours: the infestation quarter is reached through the city's own gate at its south end (map_raven.py), not from a base. | valve-raid-on-the-base, valve-alarm |

## Valve's Episode One City 17 (ep1_c17_00 to 06, and five style rooms) (`_dev/reference/valve_ep1c17/`)

Episode One's City 17 after the Citadel's blast: the dark road tunnel and car parks under the city, the streets with everything tall leaning, the Combine's last walls, the hospital, a Resistance hideout in the flats, the station yard and the last train; and Valve's style rooms for the hospital exit, the car park whole and fallen, the train loading dock and the antlion-dug tunnel. Measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_NAME/measure.json; the props and craft study in valve_ep1c17/wip/props_craft_notes.md) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic; NPCs left out where the view says so). The test map ep1_c17_03 is left out.

- ep1_c17_00: 2594 entities, 2404 brushes, 588 props (355 static, 183 physics, 28 ragdoll corpses); no light_environment and no fog: all underground, 539 AI nodes and none outdoors; 21 zombines, 18 zombies, 15 barnacles, 8 antlions. Ours: our deepest place, the Ravenholm tunnel, is one corridor 192 wide and 1698 long.
- ep1_c17_00: the road tunnel is 608 wall to wall and 196 clear, tiled to 120 or 128 over a concrete ceiling, two carriageways of 240 and 272 either side of a strip 96 wide raised 4; broken pillar clusters on its centre line 875 apart; 15 vehicles over its length, only one square. Ours: no road tunnel; the Ravenholm tunnel is 192 wide and 144 high, for walking.
- ep1_c17_00: Combine metal is 28% of what can be seen, at texture scale 0.25 on 94% of it; in the hall under the Citadel 826 brushes, most often 8 or 4 thick (plates), 94% of the wall area square to the axes in plan but 23% leaning (panels 128 high at 14 degrees, 208 at 4, 176 at 57). Ours: our tools build no Combine structure; what Combine we have are props set square.
- ep1_c17_00: lit only by its lamps: cool tubes (159 237 215, spot 600, cone 70) and warm cage lamps (254 216 146 at 75) far apart (cage lamps 2328 apart along the tunnel); median light 35. Ours: our tunnel and canal lamps are each a lamp with its own light, as Valve's.
- ep1_c17_00a: 988 props in play (763 static), 1.18 props per 100 by 100 of play, the densest of the episode; ceilings where the nodes are 128 (125 nodes), 256 (111) and 120 (100); corridors 113 wide under 124. Ours: our canal side rooms are 120 to 128 high too.
- ep1_c17_00a: the service ways carry 112 pipe03 straights and 112 connectors, one at every joint, 88 of them on skin 1, in three runs at 80, 104 and 120 over the floor and 20 to 44 off the wall. Ours: our modules place a pipe02 straight and connector pair here and there, never a run.
- ep1_c17_00a: the lift out is a timed hold of 79 to 80 s with four zombie waves (at 5, 35, 65 and 85 s) and its clock is a row of floor lamps lit one more every 20 s; the car leaves only when both riders are in (a counter of 2). Ours: our timed holds show no clock in the world (valve-progress-in-the-world).
- ep1_c17_01: 511 props in play, 79% of the 94 AI nodes outdoors; streets 320 roadway, kerbs 4, pavements 160 or 128, flat (crown 0 in 35 of 46 cuts), wall to wall 640, street walls 448 tall; brown haze 210 176 130 from 300 to 3000. Ours: road with a crown, kerb 8, pavements 96 (the owner's choice), and no haze.
- ep1_c17_01: after the blast 9 of 12 lampposts lean 13 to 45 degrees (22 and 30 commonest), 10 of 12 utility poles 10 to 45, 7 of 13 signs 22 to 30, 5 of 18 trees 13 to 22, all in round steps and many ways; benches, bins and phones stay upright. Ours: every lamppost and pole stands upright.
- ep1_c17_01: the Combine's wall through the old city is metalcombine001 brushes 48 thick and 160 to 336 high in straight runs 208 to 984 long, each turned 3 to 17 degrees from the last (yaws 357, 357, 13, 30, 77, 94, 105), outer faces leaning back 4 degrees; about 3000 long from y -64 to -1944 and east to x 3152. Ours: no Combine wall; our walls follow the street grid.
- ep1_c17_01a: 423 props in play, 63% of 158 nodes outdoors; roadway 320, kerbs 4 (243 of 255 cuts), pavements 96 to 128, flat in 115 of 145 cuts, wall to wall 576, street walls 544 tall; indoors most often 184 high. Ours: pavements 96 as here, but kerb 8 and a crowned road.
- ep1_c17_01a: physics props lie down more than anywhere in the episode (81% tilted); 13 of the creeping Combine walls (combineinnerwall001a) stand high in the skyline turned in 15 degree steps; force fields are comshieldwall2. Ours: no creeping walls on our skyline.
- ep1_c17_01a: the block the player crosses is broken open: 29 concrete_section64floor slab pieces, 16 short rebar pieces and destroyed-wall models (walldestroyed09c, collapsedbuilding01awall) in about 1800 by 1800, 64 of 177 props there tilted. Ours: the `collapse` module is our only broken building piece.
- ep1_c17_02: 339 props in play; a wide street, roadway 512, kerbs 4 (16 where a step), wall to wall 832, walls 512 tall; indoors most often 120 high (81 of 120 nodes); 26 explosive drums, all upright, in clusters 32 to 48 apart. Ours: rooms 120 to 128 high as here.
- ep1_c17_02: the Combine's last hold is a control room set inside an old office (about 560 by 660): Combine metal lines its walls and ceiling from 16 to 455 over an old plaster floor, six combine_light002a turned to aim (yaws 120, 300, 315, and pitched), monitors and interfaces square to the walls, emitters in a pair, a lock and a bracket barricade at each door, nine soldiers. Ours: our Combine rooms are the owner's map's own; no tool lines an old room in Combine metal.
- ep1_c17_02a: the hospital: 838 props in play (162 hospital props), 629 AI nodes and 2% outdoors; ceilings 192 (250 nodes), 128 (182) and 112 (78); corridors 224 wide most often (160 to 256), the lobby 224 by 192, ward corridors 240 by 128. Ours: the clinic is one vault 1024 long, its rooms 120 to 128 high.
- ep1_c17_02a: a ward is 232 by 304 under 128 with its own washroom 152 by 112 (tub, sink, toilet, radiator); the bed is a free physics prop 32 to 56 off the wall (median 40), turned 3 to 20 on 7 of 13, with an IV stand 42 to 51 away, a cart 52 to 58, a bedside shelf and a reading tube over its head at 64 up. Ours: four beds in a row along the back wall with cloth screens between them, a field hospital rather than wards.
- ep1_c17_02a: 113 wall rails (hospital_railing 64, 160 and 32) all static, flush on the wall, their top about 37 up; 91 lights, 10 of them named power lamps that start off and come on with the power. Ours: the clinic has a stone dado rail, no hand rail.
- ep1_c17_02b: 367 props in play, 41% of 282 nodes outdoors; roadway 256, kerbs 4, pavements 192 or 128, flat in 76 of 82 cuts, street walls 640 tall; indoors 120 high most often (157 nodes); storeys here 128 to 132 apart. Ours: storeys 120 to 128 high inside, as here.
- ep1_c17_02b: the Resistance hideout is the top flat of a block (floor 132, ceiling 120 over it): two slotted doors with a peephole (door03_slotted, at the street and at the flat), two tall barricades leaned back 30 degrees on the windows of the floor below, a radio corner (citizenradio on a shelf, a hard drive), a kitchen (stove, sink, fridge, pot), five chairs, one warm dome light, and a pulley hoist with two crowbars wedged in it as the way up; Barney and two citizens. Ours: our hideouts use the city's ordinary doors and no radio corner.
- ep1_c17_02b: the evacuation's Combine barricades stand in a straight row 147 apart, all turned 30 degrees (x -2710, y 2907 to 3348). Ours: none.
- ep1_c17_05: 389 props in play, 35% of 231 nodes outdoors; the station's sheds and yard lit by 37 bell lamps (light_industrialbell01_on) hung 480 to 704 over the floor, each with a light_spot 221 249 241 at 3000 straight down; fog 210 176 130 out to 15000. Ours: our train sheds are brick boxes lit by a few lamps (`shed`).
- ep1_c17_05: the diagonal street to the station keeps its lampposts 724 apart (8 lampposts, the 512 grid's diagonal), the two rows staggered by half a spacing; the repair shed is 1296 wide with the train at floor level, a sunk walk 64 deep and 120 wide each side of it and galleries 256 up along both walls. Ours: our lamps run on the street's own rhythm; the train runs between two sheds with no walk beside it.
- ep1_c17_06: 333 props in play, 5% of 202 nodes outdoors; the last train's shed is tall: ceilings 608 (39 nodes), 480 (33) and 704 (24), under 25 bell lamps; force fields (comshieldwall3) on func_brush pieces 70 wide and 340 high in a row across the yard. Ours: our sheds are as long as the train and low; no force field yet.
- ep1_c17_06: two great Combine walls stand off beyond the yard: slabs of citadel_metalwall077a 768 high, 96 thick at the foot and 48 at the head, faces turned 10 and 21 degrees off the grid while their end caps stay square; one face leans back 4.4 to 4.7 degrees and the other is plumb, so each section is lop-sided. Ours: no Combine walls; our walls are plumb and on the grid.
- styleguide_hospital_exit: a street junction behind the hospital with 11 trucks, vans and cars jammed in about 1200 by 750, yaws 60 to 270, one tilted -10; lampposts along the diagonal street 724 apart (the 512 grid's diagonal), the two rows 641 apart and staggered by half a spacing; 23% of old-material brushes at odd angles (ruin cuts). Ours: our streets run on the grid, with lamps on one rhythm and no jams.
- styleguide_garage: a car park deck 2288 by 1072, 112 clear (beams down to 96), columns 32 by 32 every 256 in three rows with a drive aisle of 384; two cars to a column bay (128 a car), bays 176 to 192 deep, 2 cars in 3 nose to the wall; ramps 1 in 6, 224 to 240 wide; bay lines, arrows and letters are overlays. Ours: no car park.
- styleguide_garage_destroyed: the same deck after the blast: 90 of its 197 props tilted, spawn chunks and rebar where the slab broke, 18 up-facing faces tilted 5 to 70 degrees; ep1_c17_00a shows the same in play (the slab pieces 96 to 300 across, 27 to 56 thick). Ours: no fallen floor in a building.
- styleguide_train_loadingdock: a brick shed 1824 wall to wall: a dock 256 wide on one side and a loading floor 800 wide on the other, both 96 over a gravel bed 736 wide with three sidings 224 apart; the roof 608 over the docks and 704 over the bed; 48 bell lamps in rows 192 to 224 apart; 17 cargo containers, 20 lockers, forklifts; track is a texture strip 128 wide on the gravel. Ours: the `shed` roofs one track with the train at street level.
- styleguide_antlion_tunnel: a service tunnel (mine concrete, cage lamps 248 apart, pipe sets along one wall) broken open into a cave of 119 displacements (power 3 on 74), 120 to 680 high; at the break 11 concrete spawn chunks within 80 of each other and five rebar pieces at pitch 60 to 90; grub clusters each with a faint green light (154 168 122 80) 12 over them, the cave lit by one cool blue light (109 192 207 1500). Ours: the antlion nest behind the forest cave's emergency door is cave alone; no built tunnel is broken into it.

## Valve's Episode Two (ep2_outland_01 to 12a, and nine EP2 style rooms) (`_dev/reference/valve_ep2/`)

The countryside of Episode Two: the mine and the antlion caves under it, the nest, the forests and roads of the outlands, the farm, the radio tower, and White Forest with its rocket silos; with Valve's style rooms for the forest, the caves, the bunkers, the silo, the farmhouse, the rotting barn, the inn, the radio tower and an outland building. Chosen for the owner's three asks: the forests (how trees stand, how a wood is bordered, what lies in it), the mines, antlion tunnels and nest (for our cave), and White Forest's base and missile silo (for our bunker). Measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_NAME/measure.json; the props and craft study in valve_ep2/notes/props_craft_notes.md, the logic and NPC study in valve_ep2/notes/logic_npc_notes.md) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic; trees as trunk and crown; big hollow models and fences as outlines; NPC marks left out of the extra cut-aways). Cut-aways of caves and bases are drawn without what lies below a set height (said in each 'where' as clip_below) so the frame is not shrunk by deep rock; border views (the 'clips' key) draw the invisible walls (toolsplayerclip) hatched in magenta; 'leave_out' names models left out (the rocket).

- ep2_outland_01 (the mine's yard, the lift down): 3395 entities, 2955 brushes, 452 displacements; 180 trees in play, the nearest other tree median 154, 43% leaning over 2 degrees, 57% of the 512 squares with ground hold no tree, tree_pine05 69 and tree_pine06 31 the commonest. Ours: Forest.plant 132 apart, none leaning.
- ep2_outland_01 indoors: ceilings most often 192 (58 of 206 nodes), corridors 116 wide (medians); mine posts struts_single02 16, ibeam01a_cluster01 14; rail fence_border_256 15 chained 256 apart. Ours (raven-mine-sets): a timber set every 512.
- ep2_outland_01a (the antlion caves under the mine): all 424 nodes under a roof; natural passages narrowest median 298, height median 243; ceilings by the cast most often 192 then 176; 93 grubs, 40 stalactites (stalactite_static01 to 03), 12 mining cars. Ours: the cave on a 320 by 320 square, 19 grubs one at a time.
- ep2_outland_01a light: 51 light (median brightness 40) and 18 light_spot, no sun; fog 18 23 35 from 1 to 2800, the same in every cave map; grubs in groups of two to four, each group with a small yellow light 30 to 43 from it. Ours: six lights (190 255 130) in the 2560 nest.
- ep2_outland_02 (the mine arena holdout): 193 of 211 nodes under a roof; passages narrowest median 131, height 148; rooms 384 by 2681 wall to wall (medians); four tunnels into one room, each with a siren and three lamps (antlion_detector_lights_lamp 12) that show which tunnel opens next and how big the wave; seven waves. Ours: no defence that shows where the next wave comes from.
- ep2_outland_03 (the long caves): 757 of 814 nodes under a roof; 158 timber props (struts_tunnel192_01 31, struts_single02 29, struts_tunnel192_02 15, struts_tunnel320_01 15); light_cagelight02_on 26 lamps 687 apart; ceilings most often 192 (177 of 751 nodes); passages narrowest median 266; 143 grubs. Ours: a timber set every 512, 7 lights in the 3072 cave.
- ep2_outland_03 light: 80 light (median 40), 46 light_spot (median 150), 12 point_spotlight; nodes median 165 to 185 from the nearest light in the cave maps, 90% within 430 to 600. Ours: 7 lights in the 3072 cave, one to about every 1160 by 1160.
- ep2_outland_04 (the nest): nest_lrg_flat 78, nest_med_flat 30, websackhalf 16, grubtunnel_door 15, stalactites 59 of four models; 96 grubs; corridors 178 wide under 200; the guardian's run 190 to 380 across at the narrowest, 1000 to 1740 long. Ours: the nest about 345 wide, 760 in its chambers, 6 lights.
- ep2_outland_04: 286 sloped faces over 2000 square units (most often 45, 20, 70 degrees; blendrockslime02a, antlion_web01a), the most of any map; 88 light (median 30) and 40 light_spot. Ours: the nest 345 wide, 760 in its chambers.
- ep2_outland_05 (out of the caves into the woods and the sand): 155 trees, the nearest other tree median 148 (the closest of all), 61% leaning; 84% of nodes outdoors; three thumpers about 1420 and 1680 apart make safe islands. Ours: 132 apart, none leaning.
- ep2_outland_05 sky: sun 240 238 217 at 650, pitch -30, fog 64 78 94 from 128 to 8000; tree fades 4200 to 7000. Ours: fades 2800 to 3400 under the city's one sun.
- ep2_outland_06 (the factory and its slime pits): 48% of nodes outdoors; indoor ceilings most often 128; fence01a 23 every 132; only 22 trees, tree_pine_card_cluster_01 cards 2991 apart round the edge.
- ep2_outland_06a (the radio tower in the woods): 446 trees (tree_pine06 119, tree_pine05 94, tree_pine04 83, tree_pine_large 26), the nearest other tree median 261, 59% of the 512 squares empty; rail fences fence_trail_256 25 and fence_border_128/256 48 chained about 254 apart; the mast held by three anchors 1051 to 1662 out. Ours: trees 132 apart, no fences in the woods.
- ep2_outland_07 (the farm): 313 trees, the nearest other tree median 294, 73% of the 512 squares empty (the most open of all); chain-link exterior_fence_notbarbed002e 58 every 512 round the yard, rail fence_border 59 in the woods; 28 stumps; fades 7000 on every tree. Ours: fades 2800 to 3400.
- ep2_outland_08 (the rail yard and the helicopter): 173 trees, the nearest other tree median 298, 23% leaning; 30 train cars and 21 containers; 76% of nodes outdoors.
- ep2_outland_09 (the yard of the Combine cannon): 158 trees, the nearest other tree median 252, none leaning; 56 cargo containers, 25 pylons, 22 security lights; 19 tree card clusters round the edge.
- ep2_outland_10 (the forest road and the roadblock): 439 trees, the nearest other tree median 206, 48% of the 512 squares empty, 10% leaning; seven combine_barricade_tall01b 58 apart, each turned 15 from the last. Ours: 132 apart, a forest without a road through it.
- ep2_outland_10a (the river woods): 613 trees, the nearest other tree median 165, 44% of the 512 squares empty (the thickest wood); fern01 146 (0.23 a tree); fades 2200 on 164 small trees; 95% of nodes outdoors. Ours: about 14 trees per 1000 square over the valley, 0.74 undergrowth a tree.
- ep2_outland_10a: only 2 light and 3 light_spot in the whole wood (forests have no lights of their own), fog 53 69 83 from 512 to 7000; terrain power 2 on 60 to 80% of floor displacements in the forest maps (a vertex every 128). Ours: 512 at power 3 (every 64).
- ep2_outland_11 (White Forest and the rocket silo): the silo block x 1280 to 2368, y -10480 to -9392, z -1792 to 64 (1088 by 1088 by 1856), 1765 brushes wholly inside; the shaft lining (538 brushes of concretewall010a and metalwall069a) from z -1414 to 64; a 24-sided shaft 254 from the axis at x 1824 y -9936, narrowed to 159 by solid rings at -1152 to -832 and -640 to -576. Ours: the bunker has no shaft.
- ep2_outland_11 base: all 167 nodes under a roof; ceilings most often 192 then 224; corridors 139 wide (medians); prison_flourescentlight001a 29, light_security 12. Ours: bunker doorway 256 by 192, inner doors 128 wide.
- ep2_outland_11a (Silo 2 and its halls): 474 of 489 nodes under a roof; ceilings most often 128, then 256 and 160; corridors 128 wide under 160; light_security 32; the second silo, same 24 sides at 254, shut by props_silo/missile_doors on two func_movelinear sliding 136 at speed 5. Ours: the bunker's sliding leaves.
- ep2_outland_11a light: 91 light and 70 light_spot, 18 point_spotlight, 67 env_sprite; cold tubes 224 254 248 (61 of 161), warm 252 241 180 in work rooms, red light_security.
- ep2_outland_11b (the hangar and the snowy yard): 80 nodes, 15% outdoors; ceilings most often 192; magnusson_device 19, wood_crate001a 36, footlocker01_closed 14.
- ep2_outland_12 (the battle round the barns): 479 trees, the nearest other tree median 244, 47% of the 512 squares empty; no real tree beyond 512 from the walk, 42 tree cards past them; 37 stumps and 53 tree slices. Ours: a ridge 440 high planted thickest, the city's skybox behind.
- ep2_outland_12 sky: sun 240 185 130 at 800, pitch -35 (late afternoon), fog 45 58 74 from 512 to 4000; 177 player clip brushes, median 54 to 570 from the walk in the forest maps, tall (median 336 to 896) and half off the axes. Ours: a ridge 440 high instead of clips.
- ep2_outland_12a (launch control and the last barn): sun at pitch -15, 240 184 130 (evening); 90 trees; ceilings most often 264; the rocket is rocket_low on a func_tracktrain in an open frame, not in a shaft.
- styleguide_forest_01: Valve's forest sample: 62 trees (cards and stumps apart) on a hillside strip about 2700 by 4900, nearest other tree median 237, 84% alone within 160; 7 stumps, 11 rock clusters, 8 ferns; 6 tree cards 689 to 1924 past the nearest real tree; 10 nodes only. Ours: Forest.plant 132 apart, 27% alone.
- styleguide_caves: Valve's cave sample: 100 grubs, 63 of them in one 2048 square, 90 egg models, 6 antlion workers and a guard placed; 23 lights; all 23 nodes under rock; rockwall015a and blendrockdirt_tunnel02a walls, water_tunnels01 in the low floors. Ours: 19 grubs one at a time every 110 in our cave.
- styleguide_bunkers: Valve's bunker sample: walls 32 thick (concretewall_bunker01a/b/c), piers 24 by 32, a blastdoor001a, the concrete running into the hill through blendbunk_conc01 displacements; 29 trees on the hill over it. Ours: lc_forest_bunker's doorway 256 by 192, inner doors 128 wide.
- styleguide_silo: Valve's silo mock-up: a round shaft centred near x 10077 y -3213 through stacked floors from z -1024 to 32, faced mostly in dev textures (reflectivity_50 on 2345 faces), 38 light and 11 light_spot, 6 cage lamps, 5 ladders.
- styleguide_farmhouse1: a bare farmhouse: 15 window frames with 13 _bars twins on the same spots; inside only 2 chairs, a kitchen sink, a pot, a bucket and a shovel.
- styleguide_barn_deterioration: against the sound barn (620 wood brushes each): 59% of its timber off the axes against 45%, func_detail 75% against 20%, boards median 62 long against 111, 539 displacements against none (power 2 on 479). Ours: a rotting house would need its boards bent, not only holed.
- styleguide_inn: the one furnished country building: chairs 13, tables 7, radiators 7, unlit dome lamps 4, boards over openings 15, a bath, sink and toilet; 139 displacements (power 2) bend its shingles and stone.
- styleguide_radiotower: the radio station: 164 nodes; exterior_fence002d 21, substation_transformer01d 8, utilityconnecter006b/c 8, handrail04 pieces 28, prison_lamp001b 9; a control room of file cabinets, closets, a desk and a monitor.
- outland_building_large_01: a timber house the Combine hold: combine_barricade_tall01a/b 12 and med01a 4 round it, a combinetower001, 7 soldiers; 20 nodes. Ours: map_forest.py places no Combine barricades.

## Valve's Nova Prospekt (d2_prison_01 to 08) (`_dev/reference/valve_prison/`)

Valve's own Nova Prospekt maps d2_prison_01 to 08, measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_NAME/measure.json; the props, craft and section study in valve_prison/wip/props_craft_notes.md, the logic and NPC study in valve_prison/wip/logic_npc_notes.md) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic, red ragdolls; big hollow models and see-through fences as outlines; green NPC markers, left out where a view says leave_out_npcs, and the train cars left out where a view lists them under leave_out: a scratch script dropped them before drawing) or cut away from above. Reference only, for Living City's Combine prison and holding cells: the VMFs are not in the repo, and the owner's own choices win where they differ (City 18's two tones with a dado rail, our cells module as it is).

- d2_prison_01: the way in, outdoors (80% of the AI nodes): yards wall to wall 420 to 1632 (median 1104 in 217 cuts), walls median 256 tall, no kerbs; the ground a grass and gravel blend; 7 gun towers (bunker_gun01, each with a point_spotlight 147 226 240, 1024 long, parented to it) and 5 combine_light002a on the walls, each with its blue beam.
- d2_prison_01: a gun tower is an octagon of concrete 160 across (corners cut, func_detail) on the wall top, its floor 8 thick at z 1784 to 1792, about 450 over the yard; posts 32 by 44 and 128 high with gaps between them, and a roof slab 176 across and 8 thick on the posts; the gun 40 over the floor and 58 off the parapet, facing out.
- d2_prison_01: the old prison's front has 16 barred windows, each a pair of props (plasterwall029g_window01a with its bars as a prop_dynamic) 40 apart; breaking the glass sets the bars' bodygroup (OnBreak > SetBodyGroup, 28 lines); 92% of wall columns over 256 tall change material, most often at 128 or 64 over the foot.
- Ours: Living City has no yard or gun tower yet; Valve's yard walls are about 256 tall, lower than our canal's 384, and its towers stand about 450 over the yard, so a tower on our canal's rim would see the whole canal floor.
- d2_prison_02: the first cellblock and the showers, almost all indoors (8% of the AI nodes outdoors): 440 prop_static to 113 prop_physics; ceilings most often 120 (41 nodes), then 256 (17) and 512 (14); corridors 134 wide under 128; 104 flights of 3 or more steps, every step 8 by 12.
- d2_prison_02: the cellblock hall (x -1280 to -1056) is 224 wall to wall with a row of cells on each side, floors at 256 and 384; cells 128 wide and 176 deep behind partitions 16 thick at a pitch of 144; 15 barred doors (prison_celldoor001a) and 14 solid ones (prison_heavydoor001a), each under a prison_doortrack001a; a cage lamp on each cell's front 86 over its floor, 144 apart, only 4 of the map's 40 lit.
- d2_prison_02: the showers are three shower_system001a 320 apart, 40 off one wall of a tiled room, under hanging tubes; 66 infodecal (stains 23, blood 9, trash 8), wall decals median 56 over the floor.
- Ours: the cells module's three cells are 128 by 160 by 128 off a corridor 128 wide and 128 high, one storey; Valve's cellblock is 128 by 176 cells two storeys high on both sides of a hall 224 wide, with the upper walks 80 wide and a gap of 64 between them.
- d2_prison_03: cellblocks, washrooms and showers: 267 prop_static to 169 prop_physics, 15 prison_toilet01 and 14 prison_sink001b; ceilings most often 256 (94 nodes), then 192 (45) and 128 (34); corridors 152 wide under 160; 334 trim strips; walls change at 128 over the foot, tile under plaster in the washrooms (tilewall009b under plasterwall026a).
- d2_prison_03: the cells are the same everywhere in Nova Prospekt (71 found in 16 rows): 128 wide, 176 deep, partitions 16 thick at a pitch of 144, floor slabs 8 so a storey is 128 and a cell 120 clear; 36 of the 71 are empty and only 3 have the full kit (bed, toilet, sink, heater): the cells a player walks into.
- d2_prison_03: washrooms have toilets 64 apart in two rows facing each other and sinks 64 apart in facing rows; the showers are shower_system001a 40 off the wall, 320 apart, on both long walls of a tiled hall.
- Ours: our cells are 160 deep and 128 high inside where Valve's are 176 deep and 120 clear; ours have a toilet, a bucket and a drain each, and a mattress or a bed frame, where Valve leaves most cells bare and kits out only the ones a player enters.
- d2_prison_04: the tall cellblock, guard offices and the camera room: 190 prop_static to 78 prop_physics; ceilings most often 192 (43 nodes), then 120 (39) and 128 (38); corridors 136 wide under 192; 12 controlroom_desk001b with 8 file cabinets; 30 flights of 3 or more steps (8 by 12, median 10 steps).
- d2_prison_04: the tall cellblock (x -1088 to -448) is a hall 448 wide and about 512 high with cells on one side only, three tiers at 128, 256 and 384 (partitions at a pitch of 144, 176 deep), walks 80 wide and 8 thick hung off the cell fronts at 256 and 384, a column 32 across rising the full height in the hall, interior_fence002c panels closing the walks' ends.
- d2_prison_04: guard office: the desk 24 to 58 off a wall, the chair 44 from it, file cabinets on the walls 73 to 131 away, a monitor on the desk, a switch box by the door 97 up; the camera room's Combine screens (combine_intmonitor001) each carry a faint light 147 226 240 at 50 and a spot at 225, cone 60.
- Ours: our cells corridor is 128 wide and 128 high; this cellblock's hall is 448 wide and about 512 high, three tiers of cells looking onto it, so a guard on the floor or a walk sees every door at once.
- d2_prison_05: the prison's service rooms (laundry, kitchen, offices) and a cellblock brought down: 422 prop_static to 136 prop_physics; ceilings most often 176 (121 nodes), then 256 (79) and 128 (34); corridors 139 wide under 190; rubble as props (rebar002d_96 23, plaster_ceilingpile001b 23, concrete_debris256pile001a 20); the fallen hall's columns are concrete_column001a in three physics pieces, 256 apart in two rows 480 apart.
- d2_prison_05: the laundry (x -600 to 92, y 293 to 1744): 12 laundry_dryer002 (static) in rows of 2 or 3, 76 to 92 apart, 8 of them turned 1 to 12 degrees off the grid; washers along the walls; laundry carts as physics props turned freely (7 laundry_cart001, 5 laundry_cart002).
- d2_prison_05: the kitchen (x -2160 to -1695, y -896 to -615, about 465 by 281): kitchen_counter001d in an L, two kitchen_stove001a 217 apart on one wall, two small stoves as physics props (named, for a scripted moment), gas cans on the counters, a shelf.
- Ours: Living City has no prison kitchen or laundry; our cells' guard room is 256 by 192, the size of Valve's small guard office, where the laundry is about 690 by 1450 and the kitchen about 465 by 281.
- d2_prison_06: the interrogation room, the depot and the train yard, all indoors: 325 prop_static, 102 prop_physics, 110 prop_dynamic (the trains and their pods: 56 cell_01_pod); ceilings most often 208 (93 nodes), then 184 (36) and 128 (33); corridors 126 wide under 192; 620 output lines, 44 relays, 7 counters.
- d2_prison_06: the Combine's own metal is 20% of the map's brush area (0 to 1% in the old prison maps): its walls stand square in plan but 14% of the map's wall area leans 14 to 17 degrees off upright (panels 128 high leaning 32, or 40); its brushes are 8 thick (140) or 2 (82), and where a plate lies on an old wall it touches it (gap 0 on 23 of 25).
- d2_prison_06: the train trench at x 3000: Combine walls 192 thick at the foot, the track bed 256 wide at z -592, the way between 336 wide up to -464, 400 to -336, 464 above: out 32 a side every 112 to 128; train cars (combine_train02a/b) 632 apart on two lines, each car a prop_dynamic parented to its train, the pods parented with it.
- Ours: our depot hall is 768 by 384 by 192 with upright clad walls; Valve's Combine halls lean 14 to 23 degrees and stand under 184 to 208 most often, and its interrogation room is one Combine model (introomarea) set into an old room rather than built of brushes.
- d2_prison_07: the last cellblocks and the way to the pod lines, all indoors: 527 prop_static to 66 prop_physics; 44 prison_cagedlight001a (9 lit); ceilings most often 128 (121 nodes), then 120 (68) and 192 (36); corridors 128 wide under 128; 121 flights of 3 or more steps; 528 output lines, 51 relays (the longest chain 7), 11 timers.
- d2_prison_07: prisoner pods hang on racks: 18 cell_01_pod each on a cell_01_rigging, two high (193 apart) and 185 to 203 apart along a row, 12 in the row before the control post and 6 in a second rack; Combine fence pairs (combine_fence01a and 01b) 576 apart; a force field is comshieldwall3 on a func_brush 2 thick, 126 high and 192 wide across a way.
- d2_prison_07: the Combine bolts its metal onto the old prison rather than building: metalcombine001 panels 2 to 10 thick (func_detail) laid on plaster; in the control post 30 such brushes (2 or 4 thick) and consoles (combine_interface001 and 003) turned to free angles in a room of the old prison's own glazed walls.
- Ours: the Combine bay from City 18 (c18_cell_a) is 188 by 237 by 216, one bay with a bunk; Valve keeps its prisoners in pods racked two high about 190 apart, watched from a glazed post over the row.
- d2_prison_08: the teleport works, built by the Combine: its metal is 81% of the map's visible brush area; metalwall077a laid at texture scale 0.5 (so only 54% of faces are at 0.25, against 83 to 95% in the other prison maps); lightmapscale 16 on 46% of faces; 0 infodecal, 8 overlays: the Combine's halls are clean.
- d2_prison_08: 163 prop_static, 43 prop_physics, 25 prop_dynamic; ceilings most often 128 (106 nodes), then 192 (45) and 256 (27); corridors 142 wide under 192; 945 output lines, 81 relays, 33 makers (the last fight is scripted); 11 cell_array_01 pod arrays in the shafts; canteen tables thrown on their sides as cover, two stacked.
- d2_prison_08: the pod shaft (x 1680 to 2032, y 2816 to 4096, z -960 to 192): 256 wide at the foot, opening out by 32 a step (128 high, 1 in 4) to about 400, straight for about 600, then closing in by 40 a step (5 in 16) to 224 at the head; the two sides step at different heights, so the section is a lop-sided lozenge; 98% of its wall area is Combine metal and 16% of it leans, half at 14 degrees and half at 17.
- Ours: our depot's walls are upright plate in a box section; Valve's Combine sections lean out and in by 1 in 4 and 5 in 16 and are never mirrored, a shape our pieces do not have yet (valve-combine-lean).

Nothing else: no table, no shelf. | valve-prison-cell, valve-prison-rooms |

Lit by narrow cold spots on the subject (155 220 255 at 500, cone 12, twice; 204 255 255 at 400 to 600, cone 21) and faint console glows (74 209 217 at 20); the rest dark. | valve-interrogation-light, valve-prisoner-in-beams, valve-combine-aimed, combine-kit |

The plates are 2 to 8 thick and laid flush on the old walls, never stood off them; the sections step out and in unevenly, not mirrored side to side. | valve-combine-lean, valve-combine-cold, combine-kit |

Dark: the shaft has no lights of its own in the drawing. | valve-combine-lean, valve-pod-racks, valve-combine-cold |

## Valve's Ravenholm (d1_town_01, 01a, 02, 02a, 04, 05 and two style rooms) (`_dev/reference/valve_ravenholm/`)

Valve's own Ravenholm maps after d1_town_03 (whose set is in valve_d1_town_03/), with the style rooms styleguide_ravenholm_01 and styleguide_mining, measured from the VMFs by map_ingest.py (each map's numbers in valve_NAME/measure.json; the props, craft and section study in valve_ravenholm/wip/props_craft_notes.md) and drawn by map_view.py (flat colours; props as boxes of their true size, orange static, yellow physics, cyan dynamic, red ragdolls; green NPC markers, left out where a view says leave_out_npcs: a scratch script dropped every npc_ entity before drawing) or cut away from above. Reference only, for the infestation quarter (map_raven.py): the VMFs are not in the repo, and the owner's own choices win where they differ (the quarter's main street keeps its crown, kerb 8 and pavements 96).

- d1_town_01: the town's first streets, Grigori's traps and the pyre: 166 prop_physics to 157 prop_static (the city maps: one physics prop to every 1 to 3 static), 36 prop_ragdoll, 281 infodecal (146 of them blood), 28 point_spotlight, 63 ambient_generic, 37 soundscapes (d1_town.Start 15, d1_town.WoodBuilding 12).
- d1_town_01: no pavements: a kerb in 4 of 320 cross-sections; wall to wall median 620, the commonest narrow lane 192 (34 cuts) and gaps between houses 128 (10); street walls median 352 (height over width 0.4); the ground dirt blend 36%, cobbles (stonefloor006a/b/c) 31%, planks 8%.
- d1_town_01: the dead lie in two set pieces of 9 and 7 bodies within 128 (the blade shack, the pyre yard), then a 4, three 2s and singles; 47% of the blood decals lie within 128 of a body; 13 meathook001a, each tied to a body by a phys_constraint.
- d1_town_01: lit by clamp lamps on the walls: light_spotlight02_lamp 16, 638 apart, each with a light_spot '255 245 145' 600, cone 45/30, pitch -50; point_spotlight nearest neighbour median 294; the sun '175 230 239' at 50, ambient '43 45 57' at 5, no fog.
- Ours: the quarter's alleys are 128 wide (Valve's narrowest gap between two houses, not its lanes of 192 to 620) and its south street 192; our walls rise to the sky lid at z 1408, about 750 over the road, where Valve's stop at about 352 under pitched roofs of 25 degrees with 16 of eave.
- d1_town_01a: a short run of houses and a corridor: 40 prop_physics to 57 prop_static, 12 prop_ragdoll, 14 oildrum001_explosive (the commonest model), 72 infodecal (every blood decal within 107 of a body), 8 point_spotlight about 452 apart; 41% of the AI nodes outdoors.
- d1_town_01a: the narrowest lanes in Ravenholm: no kerb in 50 cuts, wall to wall most often 384 (median 376), walls median 448 (most 384 to 512), so height over width 0.9: a lane here is as tall as it is wide.
- d1_town_01a: indoors ceilings most often 120 (11 of 71 nodes), then 152 and 184; corridors 106 wide under 152; rooms 238 by 668 (medians); lightmapscale 64 on 36% of faces (city maps 12 to 13%); breakable plaster patches (func_breakable 1 by 40 by 84, health 1) to smash through.
- Ours: the quarter's alleys (128) are a third of Valve's commonest lane here (384) under walls about 750 tall, so ours are deep slots (height over width near 6) where Valve's lanes are about square; our rooms are 144 high, Valve's 120 to 152.
- d1_town_02: the town square, the yards and the tram up to the church: 286 prop_static, 84 prop_physics, 6 prop_ragdoll; 63% of the AI nodes outdoors; 21 point_spotlight about 368 apart; pipes chained along the walls (pipe02_straight01_long 56 with 27 connectors) and handrails along the roof walks (handrail04_long 17, handrail04_corner 11).
- d1_town_02: the square is the only place in Ravenholm with kerbs (21 of 275 cuts, kerbs 4 to 15 high); wall to wall median 589, most often 320 and 448; walls median 288; cobbles 23% of the outdoor ground, grass and gravel 15%, flat roofs 13%; yards on displacements of power 3 cut to the plot (224 by 128, 352 by 304).
- d1_town_02: indoors ceilings most often 120 (72 of 171 nodes), then 128 and 152; rooms 234 by 475, corridors 104 under 128 (medians); a laundry of 5 washing machines 40 to 87 apart beside 4 radiators and a boiler; one bedroom (a mattress on a bed frame, a radiator, a drawer, a bulb, a body).
- Ours: the quarter's square and main street keep the owner's crown, kerb 8 and pavements 96, which is close to Valve's own square here (kerbs 4 to 15); the back ways get none, as Valve's lanes.
- d1_town_02a: the graveyard and the church roof (Grigori's goodbye): 255 prop_static to 24 prop_physics, no bodies at all, 14 crows on the roofs and walls; 0.185 props per 100 by 100 of play (the trap streets 0.48), empty 256 cells 53%: the graveyard is the quiet after the town.
- d1_town_02a: 26 grave pieces over about 1300 by 1300 of grass (one per 65,000 square units), all prop_static: gravestone004a 9, 002a 4, crosses 4, coffin lids 5, a statue; yaw square on 11, turned 3 to 20 on 9, free on 5; only the three against the west wall stand in a row (86 and 78 apart, one yaw).
- d1_town_02a: the ground is 91% displacement (blendgrassgravel001a, blenddirtgrass008a); the walls round it stonewall036b, the west one up to about 600; the only fog in Ravenholm, black '0 0 0' from 1500 to 4000; 6 gas canisters (canister_propane01a) upright in twos and fours about 33 apart, held still, with 46 env_fire round them lit together; 5 saw blades lying out 150 to 1050 apart to be picked up.
- Ours: the quarter has no churchyard yet; if it gets one, Valve's numbers say a walled patch of displaced grass about 1300 across with 15 to 30 pieces scattered and turned, never rows, and its own black fog.
- d1_town_04: the mine: no sun at all (no light_environment), every AI node indoors, 0.088 props per 100 by 100 (the lightest of the town); light from 11 clamp lamps (light_spotlight02_lamp, cool '159 237 215' 800, cone 50/25, pitch -65) about 1311 apart and bare bulbs (lightbulb01a, '254 216 146', a spot 300 straight down).
- d1_town_04: 327 displacements, power 2 on all but 3, 256 square on 290, lightmapscale 16 on every one, pushed out a median 60 (q75 128): a closed tube, walls 181, floors 76, roofs 70; rockwall015a on the walls, blendrockgravel001a under foot with its alpha painted.
- d1_town_04: the long tunnel's timber sets (woodfloor005a, func_detail): two 16 by 16 posts 240 apart centre to centre (224 clear) under a 16 by 16 cap, about 128 clear over the floor, one set every 512 on the level (x -3600 to -2064) and every 768 down the slope; deeper, sets 256 apart with knee braces 12 by 80 by 80 and boards 4 by 64 by 92 behind the posts.
- d1_town_04: ceilings over the nodes most often 248 (23 of 163), then 128 and 240; corridors 166 wide under 219; 30 flights of 8 by 12 steps, median 5 a flight; one propeller trap (trappropeller_engine 2) and 9 explosive drums.
- Ours: the quarter's tunnel house is a brush corridor (tunnel 192 wide inside, x 1216 to 1408); Valve's mine is a tube of power-2 displacements about 166 to 224 wide with a timber set every 512, which is the look a mine or cellar tunnel under the quarter would want.
- d1_town_05: the way out to the railway (Combine and rebels, not infested): the light comes back, sun '254 242 160' at 400 and ambient '172 196 204' at 80 (the town: '175 230 239' at 50, ambient 5), warm fog '254 242 160' from 1024 to 10000, a day sky (sky_day02_01); 6 point_spotlight only.
- d1_town_05: yards and rail cuttings rather than lanes: wall to wall most often 384 (54 cuts) and 1920 (40), walls 256 (331 of 474); ground gravel 53%, red_grass 20%; chain-link fence props (exterior_fence002d 40), crates and pallets, 13 Combine soldiers placed.
- Ours: the quarter's edge is the owner's street; Valve marks leaving the dead town by light alone (sun eight times as strong, ambient sixteen times), which our one-sun map can only hint at with lamps at the quarter's mouth.
- styleguide_ravenholm_01: not the timber town but an early idea for it, a snowy industrial yard at dusk: sun '235 155 71' at 700, ambient '105 142 165' at 120, orange fog from 512 to 15000 (dusk_01hdr); 642 displacements (546 of them snow floors, power 2, 85% painted, and 56 drifts 128 by 32 along wall feet); 809 prop_static, pipes chained everywhere (pipe01 and pipe02 connectors 76 each).
- styleguide_ravenholm_01: its houses still keep the town's numbers: roofs pitched 25 most (then 45 and 65) in tileroof003a_snow, slabs 4 to 12 thick, timber posts 2 by 8, eaves 16; walls change material first at 128 (70% of tall walls banded).
- styleguide_mining: a mine head, not Ravenholm's mine: a round shaft with tunnels off it, all lit by day (sun '245 238 197' at 900, ambient 200, fog '43 51 57' 2000 to 9000) and lightmapscale 16 on every face; in the tunnel, timber sets of woodwall041a every 248: posts 16 by 16 and 128 tall, 368 apart, knee braces 72 by 72 carrying a cap 256 long at about 176 over the floor.
- styleguide_mining: cage lamps (light_cagelight02) on the sets 117 over the floor, one lit in three (2 of 3 along the tunnel are the _off model), each lit one with a light_spot '255 245 145' 500 to 700, cone 70/30, pitch -70; pipe sets (pipeset 512) along the wall, 15 vol_light cones for the beams.
- Ours: the quarter has no mine or cellar tunnel with timber; Valve's two mines agree on 16 by 16 timber, a set every 248 to 512, and a lamp on every second or third set, the rest dark.

| R118 | `valve_ravenholm/18_style_mining_overview.png` | styleguide_mining, the whole style room from the south-west (overview, no box) | A mine head as one piece: a round shaft with two tall static models standing over it (grainelevator01, orange, drawn as their boxes) and tunnels running off in a cross, timber sets, handrails (handrail04_long 15) and pipes in them. | raven-mine-sets |
| R119 | `valve_ravenholm/19_style_mining_timber_sets.png` | styleguide_mining, the east tunnel looking east, eye 2620 -456 0 (yaw 0) | Timber sets down a mine tunnel: 16 by 16 posts with knee braces carrying the cap, a set every 248, warm pools from the one lit cage lamp in three, dark between them. | raven-mine-sets, raven-clamp-lamps, valve-light-pools |

## Valve's vehicles test map (`_dev/reference/valve_vehicles/`)

Valve's own sdk_vehicles (a vehicle test map in the Source SDK copy the owner uploaded, 21 November), drawn by map_view.py. Reference only: the VMF is not in the repo.

- Things: 31 prop_static (rockcliff_cluster03a 20, 03b 9, driftwood_01a 2), one prop_vehicle_jeep (the buggy), one info_player_start, one light_environment; no lights, no sound, no decals.
- Ground: 49 world brushes; every floor is displacement (blendsandgrass008a, blenddirtgrass001a/b) over about 7600 by 12900.
- Light: sun '255 244 213' at 512, pitch -45, ambient '172 196 204' at 50; sky sky_wasteland02; no fog.

| # | File | Where | What it teaches | Lessons |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| VH1 | `valve_vehicles/01_overview_sw.png` | the whole test map from the south-west | One big rolling displacement field ringed by rock cliff props: room to drive, nothing else. |  |

## The lessons

Tags: `props`, `owner`, `compiler`, `check`, `performance`, `doors`, `stairs`, `geometry`, `bug`, `entities`, `lighting`, `city18`, `grime`, `sound`, `streets`, `facades`, `layout`, `interiors`, `combine`, `process`, `gameplay`, `canal`, `viewer`, `materials`, `valve`, `infestation`, `ravenholm`, `logic`, `recipe`, `multiplayer`, `story`, `water`, `npc`, `forest`, `cave`

### A thing is a prop, never brushes (`props-not-brushes`)

Doors, bars, gates, beds, tables, shelves, lamps, speakers, fences, railings, pipes, signs, machines, vehicles, guns, tools: find the Half-Life 2 model and place it. Brushes are for architecture (walls, floors, slabs, steps, frames, kerbs, roofs) and for things no game has a model of (say which in the module's 'Left as brushes' line).

- **Why:** "You use almost entirely brushes to make things when there are perfectly good props ... it literally looks like something from 1998 ... cell doors out of brushes, but there is a whole selection of cell doors and prison bars from HL2 Nova Prospekt ... a loudspeaker out of brushes but there is literally a loudspeaker prop" (20 November).
- **How:** grep -i WORD _dev/tools/dl/ValveQCs.txt | grep -iE 'source/(hl2|episodic|lostcoast)/'; sizes with map_assets.size(model). docs/dev/map-modules.md, 'Things'.
- **Checked by:** review (no automatic check)
- **Tags:** props, owner

- **See:** `valve_canals/15_lock_iso.png`

### The prop must be the right thing, not just a thing (`prop-fits-its-job`)

Use the model made for the job: valve wheels (props_pipes valvewheel001/002) not a cart wheel, steel pipes for pressure mains not concrete culvert sections, the Combine's own crates (models/items/item_item_crate) and ration packages (models/weapons/w_package) in a Combine depot, weapon world models (models/weapons/w_*) on a gun rack.

- **Why:** Review, 20 November: a cart wheel on a flood valve, drain culverts as service mains, brush guns on a rack.
- **How:** Search the lists by what the thing is, not by what is near in size.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** props

### A physics prop is never a prop_static (`physics-props-dynamic`)

A model with prop_data (tables, chairs, shelves, lockers, boards, pots, crates, mattresses...) is deleted by the compiler as a prop_static. Place it as prop_dynamic_override (standing still) or prop_physics (loose).

- **Why:** The owner's compile of the town, 20 November: "almost none of the props are showing"; vbsp printed "prop_static using model X, which must be used on a dynamic entity (i.e. prop_physics). Deleted." for every one.
- **How:** m.static does it by itself (map_assets.is_static / is_physics_prop).
- **Checked by:** map_assets.py fails a physics model used as prop_static or plain prop_dynamic
- **Tags:** props, compiler, check

### Keep networked props to what a place needs (`networked-budget`)

Every prop_dynamic(_override), prop_physics and prop_door_rotating is an entity the server networks (the game has about 8,000 for everything). About 60 a module at most; clutter that does not need to move should be a model built static.

- **Why:** Follows from physics-props-dynamic: the town went to about 300 such entities.
- **How:** grep -c the classnames in the built VMF. Valve's maps hold a median of about 105 prop_physics each (22 to 199; 2 in d3_citadel_01), and 421 prop_dynamic in 22 maps (45% parented to moving things such as trains and lifts, 35% named for the game, 20% unnamed train seats, racks and locks) and only 15 with a DefaultAnim (bunker guns); wheels, windmills and rings turn as func_rotating.
- **Checked by:** review (counts in the build)
- **Tags:** props, performance

### A door that opens is the city's own, fitted as the owner's (`real-doors`)

Doors players use are prop_door_rotating with door01_left, set up as the owner's 134 are, in an opening 50 wide (from 2 behind the hinge) and 110 high, the door's middle 55 up. The jambs fill the reveal so nothing is seen past the door.

- **Why:** "The apartments' door frames are slightly too large for the doors and I can see past the door into the apartments" (20 November). Measured round 42 of the owner's doors. Valve fits door01_left (49 wide, 109 tall) in a doorway 48 wide and 108 high, two side by side in 96 by 108 (d1_trainstation_02 to 05).
- **How:** map_town.real_door and door_hole.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** doors, owner

- **See:** `05_flat.png`

### A doorway nobody can use never shows a real door (`no-fake-doors`)

Never put the door01_left model (or any door that looks openable) where it does not open: board the doorway up (planks, wood_board props), chain it, or brick it.

- **Why:** "The door prop you used for all these unopenable doors is the same prop ... for actual doors that are supposed to open, so it's confusing and I can't tell where I can actually go in" (20 November).
- **How:** map_town.facade boards unused doorways; heavy doors that never open (lock, swing bridge) still to fix.
- **Checked by:** map_lint.py door (a door model that is not a prop_door_rotating)
- **Tags:** doors, owner

### Steps about 16 high and 24 deep (`stairs-16-24`)

Fewer, larger steps: rise about 16, tread about 24 (the same slope as 8 and 12, half the steps). (Valve's train station steps rise 8 under a player clip ramp, as the owner's own map does.)

- **Why:** "It's like a stairway meant for toddlers or old ladies? these steps should be fewer and larger" (20 November). Measured in City 18 (20 November): the owner's own flights are mostly 8 up and 12 deep (about 300 steps of 8, 36 of 16), so 16 by 24 is their choice for the long flights of the new pieces (the canal's way up), not a copy of their map. Valve's other maps (21 November): 8 by 12 in all six with stairs (100 to 251 steps a map); 16 by 24 only at d1_trainstation_02's outdoor steps (42).
- **How:** m.steps does it; hand-made stairs should match. Valve's canals: every one of 205 brush steps measured rises 8 and runs 12; ladders are metalladder002 pieces of 128, most one or two pieces high.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** stairs, owner

### A stair along a wall has a solid open side (`stair-open-side`)

Give a wall stair an upstand (a parapet about 32 high following the flight) on its open side and fill under it to the floor, so it reads as one built stair, not a row of blocks hung on the wall.

- **Why:** Review, 20 November: from the canal the quay, arcade, lift and pipes stairs read as a sawtooth.
- **How:** m.stair_side(start, run, v1, v2, z_low, z_high, sign): an upstand on the open side of a flight along u.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** stairs

### Pieces meet: no gaps between walls, banks and steps (`no-gaps`)

Where something stands at a cut in the canal's banks, it fills to the cut's edge: the banks are cut a whole 64 square at a time by m.no_bank. Side walls of steps go down to the floor.

- **Why:** "The geometry in the canal entrance thing has a gap for the sloped walls and the grey concrete brush underneath the railings, there is a gap on both sides" (20 November).
- **How:** map_town.landing: cheeks 64 wide, solid to the floor.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** geometry

### Nothing floats, nothing is sunk, nothing clips (`nothing-floating`)

Every prop touches a floor, a ceiling, a wall or another prop; lamps sit on their ceilings; bodies lie on the ground they are on (work out a mound's height from the build's bumps).

- **Why:** Floating dome lamps and props in walls found in several modules (20 November); the owner saw floating ropes in the garden.
- **How:** m.static takes the floor z the thing stands ON; map_assets sizes.
- **Checked by:** map_floating.py (props touching nothing), the build's LOOK lines (props in walls, through roofs, under floors)
- **Tags:** geometry, check

### No ropes hanging in the air (`no-floating-ropes`)

A rope or cable is tied at both ends to something that is there (a pole, a wall, a hook), or it is not placed.

- **Why:** "Please remove the floating ropes in the garden" (20 November).
- **How:** m.rope: check both ends.
- **Checked by:** map_lint.py rope (a rope end tied to nothing)
- **Tags:** geometry, owner

### No open pipe end shows its inside (`cap-open-ends`)

Cap every pipe end players can see (props_pipes pipe02_connector01, pipe_cap005, a cone or plate brush), and never leave a nodraw face where it can be seen.

- **Why:** "At the end of the pipe for the distillery we need a cone brush or something since the pipe has nodraw on the exposed end" (20 November).
- **How:** The still: a cap and a funnel (map_mods_b.frustum).
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** geometry, owner

### Water never shares a plane with a floor (`water-plane`)

A water brush's top sits at least 2 under any floor it lies beneath, or the two flicker through each other.

- **Why:** Review, 20 November: the flood room's drained water lies exactly at its floor.
- **How:** Fix in map_mods_c.py (the func_water_analog's box to FL - 2).
- **Checked by:** map_lint.py water (a water brush's top level with a floor)
- **Tags:** geometry, bug

### A fire in a drum stays in the drum (`fire-in-drums`)

env_fire falls to the ground under it unless spawnflag 16 (Don't drop) is set: set it for any fire placed above the ground (drums, braziers, stoves).

- **Why:** "The burn barrels flanking the podium have fire on the ground inside the barrel instead of the fire coming out the top" (20 November).
- **How:** map_mods_b.fire(..., drop=False).
- **Checked by:** map_lint.py fire (an env_fire on a prop without spawnflag 16)
- **Tags:** entities, owner

### The loose mattress lies on its back unless turned (`mattress-turned`)

Turn furnituremattress001a over (roll 180) when placing it.

- **Why:** "The mattresses in the clinic are all rotated 180 degrees on their side" (20 November).
- **How:** map_mods_b.mattress(). Valve places furnituremattress001a as a prop_ragdoll (17 of 18 in 22 maps), angles 0 yaw 0, spawnflags 4, on the floor of bunk rooms and hideouts (c01a, c03, c05, c06, c10, c12, ts03, ts04), never as a static.
- **Checked by:** map_lint.py mattress
- **Tags:** props, owner

### What Half-Life 2 made move, moves (`moving-things-move`)

Swings, roundabouts, hanging things: physics props on phys_hinge (or ropes parented to them), not frozen models.

- **Why:** "HL2 had a working merry-go-round, and working swings" (20 November).
- **How:** map_mods_b.swing; the playground's roundabout.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** entities, owner

### A gate goes in its frame (`gates-in-frames`)

Nova Prospekt's interior_fence002e is the frame for the gate interior_fence001g: hang the gate in the frame (or use the gate alone), never the frame as the gate.

- **Why:** "The gates you used are the gate/door frames for the real gate interior_fence001g; so they have to be fitted inside of the gate frames, or just used instead" (20 November).
- **How:** The pit: frame static, the gate riding an unseen swing door.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** props, owner

### A lamppost's arm reaches over what it lights (`lamppost-arm`)

lamppost03a's arm runs along the model's +y (90 degrees left of its yaw): turn it so the arm is over the street or the place, never over a wall or a drop behind.

- **Why:** "The lamppost at the top of the canal entry stairs is backwards and should be facing the street not the stairs" (20 November).
- **How:** Yaw so model +y points at the street.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** lighting, owner

### A lit lamp uses the lit model (`light-model-matches`)

Where a light shines, the fixture is its _on model (light_cagelight02_on, not _off with a light under it); a dead lamp has no light.

- **Why:** Review, 20 November: overflow, thumper, railbridge and armoury.
- **How:** m.lamp uses real fixtures. Valve's lit lamp models carry an env_sprite at the bulb (114 of 149, distance 0): materials/sprites/light_glow03.vmt, scale .75, rendermode 9 (world space glow), renderamt 255; 147 of 149 have a light entity within reach (all lamps: light_spot 475, point_spotlight 202, light 111). Our m.lamp glows at scale 0.25.
- **Checked by:** map_lint.py offlamp (an _off fixture with a light at it)
- **Tags:** lighting

### Light as City 18 does: many small fixtures, some with beams (`city18-lighting`)

Light a room with two or three fixtures, not one; point some (light_spot, point_spotlight); use the city's own fixtures: dome lights, lab fluorescent tubes indoors, cage lights in service places, the Combine's light on Combine places, lampposts outside.

- **Why:** The owner's map: 597 lights, 391 light_spot, 174 point_spotlight; dome light 151, lab fluorescent 149, Combine light 75, lamp fixture 52, cage light 47. Lampposts in City 18 stand 360 to 870 apart (most about 600 to 750). Valve's day streets use the unlit lamppost03a_off with no light on it, 818 (d3_c17_13) and 962 (d1_trainstation_04) apart; lampfixture01a 784 apart round the plaza (d1_trainstation_02).
- **How:** m.lamp, m.spot, m.light; every canal stretch now gets a caged lamp on every third rib, a third of them dead (dress_canal_walls).
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** lighting, city18

- **See:** `03_street.png`, `06_canal.png`, `07_cells.png`, `10_waiting.png`, `16_ladder.png`, `17_street.png`, `18_cells.png`, `20_payphone.png`, `23_spot.png`, `24_toilet.png`, `25_station_plan.png`, `29_payphone.png`, `30_waiting.png`

### Lay grime on: stains, cracks, trash, posters, signs (`grime`)

Every place gets overlays and decals: water stains down walls and under drains, cracks, trash in corners, posters where people are, the lambda where the Resistance is, danger signs on hazards, numbers on machines.

- **Why:** The owner's map: 249 info_overlays and 267 infodecals (decalstain002a 26 times, trashdecal04a, plaster cracks, Breen posters, danger signs, metal grates, speaker grilles, graffiti 059a, the lambda spray). The modules had almost none.
- **How:** m.grime(kind, u, v, z) with GRIME's kinds (stain, drain, trash, crack, poster, danger, graffiti, number, lambda, burn, puddle, grate, debris, police: all checked in Valve's lists); every canal stretch now gets drain stains and some graffiti by itself (map_modules.dress_canal_walls). Valve's 13 canal maps: 613 infodecal and 75 info_overlay; the commonest are ivy (ivy01 to ivy03: 187), trash (trashdecal01a and 02a: 121), decalstain002a 17, decalconcretedrain002a 17 (and 11 overlays), lambdaspray_2a 14 (1 to 5 a map: the Resistance's way marks), graffiti001b/c/d 31. GRIME has no ivy: add a kind "ivy" (decals/ivy01, ivy02, ivy03) for canal walls. Decals go with props in Valve's maps: loose trash has a trash decal (trashdecal01a, 02a) within 48 in 57% of cases, bins 42%, benches 45% (decalstain014a, debris_concrete001a), shelves 43%, chairs 40% (decalstain002a, bloodstain_001), corpses 25% (blood, scorch1), barrels and crates only 11%.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** grime, city18

### Every place sounds like itself (`soundscapes`)

Each module and each part of the town names a soundscape (env_soundscape with a radius), not only an ambient_generic loop.

- **Why:** The owner's map: 155 env_soundscapes (d1_trainstation.*, d1_town.*, prison.*, d1_canals.*, construct.inside...) and only 41 ambient_generics.
- **How:** m.soundscape(SCAPES[kind], u, v, z, radius): SCAPES holds the soundscapes the owner's own map uses (tunnel, watery, slime, pump, cave, flat, house, inside, combine, laundry, metal, courtyard, square, rooftop). Valve's station puts two env_soundscape at each change of place (d1_trainstation.Platform, .Turnstyle, .Interrogation, .QuietCourtyard, general.concrete_quiet). Valve's 13 canal maps: 173 env_soundscape (8 to 24 a map), commonest canals_slime_tunnel 17, d1_canals.general_dripping_tunnel 13, canals_river 12, canals_slime_outside 10, canals_canal_water_industrial 10; also canals_canal_water, canals_canal_nowater (a dry canal: fits ours), canals_tunnel_dry and canals_tunnel_wet.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** sound, city18

- **See:** `valve_d1_trainstation_01/12_courtyard_plan.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_02/05_courtyard.png`

### Streets as the owner's: pavements, kerbs, a crowned road (`street-profile`)

Pavements 96 wide at the floor's height with a kerb of 8; gutters 8 down; cobbles (stonefloor006d_c17, sett strips 006e/006f) rising to a crown 4 over the gutters.

- **Why:** "The street is just flat cobblestone? We have sidewalks and a sloped street (higher in middle) all over the main map"; "sidewalks are not 2 feet tall irl, they are like 6-12 inches" (20 November). Measured in the owner's map. Valve's own City 17 streets for comparison (measured 21 November): roadway 256 (d1_trainstation_03, 04) or 320 (d3_c17_13), flat (crown 0 in 34 of 36 and 79 of 83 sections), the kerb a plain step of 4 (8 on 62 of 166 kerbs of the war street), pavements 128 to 192 of concretefloor028c, roads concretefloor033c or stonefloor006d_c17 with 006f_c17 edge strips. The owner's crown and kerb of 8 are their own choice: keep them.
- **How:** map_town.lay_street. The street corridor beside the canal does not have it yet.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** streets, owner, city18

- **See:** `03_street.png`, `09_payphone.png`, `10_waiting.png`, `11_street_main.png`, `13_toilet.png`, `17_street.png`, `21_waiting.png`, `22_street_section.png`, `valve_c17/03_checkpoint_street_section.png`, `valve_c17/05_square_street_section.png`, `valve_c17/10_shop_street_section.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_03/03_street.png`, `valve_d3_c17_13/03_street.png`

### Building fronts, not brick walls (`facades`)

Dress a building line as houses: lots of different heights, a plinth, a string course, a cornice, pilasters between neighbours, windows recessed with sills and lintels, downpipes, real doors where there is a way in.

- **Why:** "There's no design, windows, building facades, nothing! I was hoping you'd make something more similar to the city just on a much smaller scale" (20 November).
- **How:** map_town.facade.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** facades, owner, city18

- **See:** `01_overview_sw.png`, `09_payphone.png`, `12_block_above.png`, `13_toilet.png`, `23_spot.png`, `27_square_above.png`, `28_covered_above.png`, `valve_c17/10_shop_street_section.png`, `valve_c17/12_shop_street_eye.png`, `valve_c17/22_shop_window_street_eye.png`

### Not every window the same glass (`glass-variety`)

Use several of the owner's glass materials, each laid exactly as their map lays it, a different one per house.

- **Why:** "The window texture fits right but it's the same texture and it looks awful because every window looks like that; we need to use the varieties of glass" (20 November).
- **How:** map_town.GLASS: six fits read from the owner's map.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** facades, owner

- **See:** `valve_coast/07_coast07_bridge_village.png`

### Cut corners and a broken skyline (`skyline-corners`)

Corner buildings get a cut (chamfered) corner, often with a doorway; rooflines get chimneys, aerials, dishes, the odd pitched roof; facades get balconies, shutters, shopfronts with signs and awnings.

- **Why:** City 18's massing (octagonal columns, cut corners, stepped blocks); review of the town, 20 November.
- **How:** map_town.facade puts chimneys, aerials, dishes and vents on the roofs (roof_prop, one to a slot so they never stand in each other), a pitched roof on about one house in three, balconies on some upper floors, awnings and name boards over shop windows. The houses are filled 640 deep behind their fronts and the sky over inner roofs starts at the lid, so things can stand on them. Cut corners are still to do.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** facades, city18

- **See:** `01_overview_sw.png`, `02_overview_ne.png`, `03_street.png`, `08_combine.png`, `09_payphone.png`, `11_street_main.png`, `12_block_above.png`, `13_toilet.png`, `27_square_above.png`, `valve_c17/08_back_court_blocks_iso.png`, `valve_c17/32_nexus_outside_iso.png`, `valve_d1_town_03/01_overview_sw.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_01/01_overview_sw.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_02/02_overview_ne.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_03/02_overview_ne.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_04/02_overview_ne.png`, `valve_d3_c17_13/06_block.png`

### A town is not one straight line (`town-shape`)

Lay a town as an L or a U (corners), keeping the two arms' modules apart behind a corner (a stretch of house fronts alone, 1280 long, on the inner side).

- **Why:** "Yes I think the town needs to be in an L shape or a U" (20 November).
- **How:** Layout words 'turn right|left' and 'houses N' (map_town.corner).
- **Checked by:** the build's clash check
- **Tags:** layout, owner

### On a street, places sit at the pavement's height (`flush-with-street`)

A module lifted from the canal to a street drops the terraces it needed against the canal's floods: its yards sit level with the pavement; where something must be higher, steps from the pavement reach it.

- **Why:** "Please lower the playground to be flush/even with the sidewalk and not raised up on a massive platform"; "the playground needs stairs to get up the slope" (20 November).
- **How:** Modules read m.fr.dz (on a street when non-zero); map_town.terrace_slopes makes steps.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** layout, owner

### Rooms with room in them (`room-to-move`)

Leave walking room: furniture against walls, a clear path through every room and into every stall; split a crowded room's things into two rooms; no prop inside another.

- **Why:** "You put a couch inside the table and the wood furnace/stove is almost touching the table and couch and it's just too cramped, half of that can go in the bedroom(s)"; "all the market stalls are filled with boxes and random props and there's no room to even walk into them" (20 November).
- **How:** Place by the models' real boxes (map_assets.size).
- **Checked by:** map_lint.py overlap (a solid prop inside another), printed as LOOK lines by every build
- **Tags:** interiors, owner

- **See:** `14_flats_plan.png`, `valve_c17/09_court_flats_cut.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_03/05_flat.png`, `valve_d1_trainstation_05/05_lab_cut.png`

### Interiors in City 18's way (`city18-interiors`)

Plaster walls in two tones split by a dado rail, wooden floors, door casings proud of the wall, a ceiling trim; stoves, sinks, toilets, fridges, iron bunks backs to the wall; radiators; fluorescent tubes. Never a plain cream box. Each kind of place in its own materials: a cellar is brick or stone, a Combine room is Combine metal, a culvert is damp concrete.

- **Why:** The owner's flats (picture), their 14 toilets and sinks; review, 20 November (cream rooms in a dozen modules). Valve's flats (d1_trainstation_03) for comparison: four inside walls in five are one material floor to ceiling; where two-toned the change is at 64.
- **How:** Line rooms with thin detail brushes; the plan's own walls are concrete or brick.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** interiors, city18

- **See:** `04_station.png`, `05_flat.png`, `14_flats_plan.png`, `16_ladder.png`, `20_payphone.png`, `24_toilet.png`, `25_station_plan.png`, `29_payphone.png`, `valve_c17/23_shop_and_office_cut.png`

### The Combine's places in the Combine's kit (`combine-kit`)

Use the Combine's own props: booths (combine_booth_short01a/med01a), buttresses (combinebuttress), bunker shields, fences with fields (combine_fence01a/b, combine_emitter01), railings (props_combine railing_128/256/512), lights (combine_light001/002), monitors, cameras (combinecamera001), locks, health chargers, metal (metalcombine002).

- **Why:** The owner's map: combine_fence01a/b 12 each, combine_light002a 75; review, 20 November (checkpoint, thumper, depot, armoury in brushes, prison fencing and cream plaster).
- **How:** grep source/hl2/props_combine in the lists. Into the old city the Combine comes as models (combine_barricade_*, combine_lock01, monitors, CombineInnerwallCluster1024, combine_light002a); its brushes are rare: 34 in d1_trainstation_02, metalcombine001 panels 4 to 8 thick, a third of them cut into the old wall, none in d1_trainstation_03 to 05. Valve's uprising (d3_c17_05 to 10a, 21 November): combine_lock01 on the wall beside 21 of 22 Combine func_doors, centred 52 up (44 to 58); the creeping wall (combineinnerwall001a, combineinnerwallcluster1024) high in the skyline in runs of 2 to 4 pieces 150 to 250 apart, each turned 15 degrees from the last; roadblocks in valve-combine-barricade-rows. Nova Prospekt (21 November): the Combine's metal is 0 to 1% of brush area in the old prison (d2_prison_01 to 05, 07), 20% in its depot and train yard (06) and 81% in its teleport works (08). In the old buildings it comes as props (pods, consoles, lights, fences, turrets), field walls (comshieldwall3 func_brush 2 thick, 126 high), consoles built of 25 to 30 small metalcombine001 brushes with doorbutton002a buttons (d2_prison_07 -632 -3390 -168), and thin metalcombine001 panels 2 to 10 thick bolted on at angles (87% angled). Where a plate is laid on an old wall it touches it (gap 0 on 34 of 38 in d2_prison_06 and 08); most of the Combine's slabs stand free as its own walls (115 of 153).
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** combine, city18
