### A Combine camera room: one screen, a button that steps through the cameras, and the sound of the room you watch (`valve-prison-cctv`)

A guard room has one monitor and three or four fixed cameras looking down into the block. Each press of the console button steps to the next camera with a click, a small light per channel shows which is on, and the picture breaks into static for a moment as it changes. A microphone at each camera plays that room's sound through the screen while its channel is on. The screen and microphones run only while someone is in the room. Choosing a camera can start something small there, seen only on the screen (two guards walking through and out of view). A new use for our Combine consoles: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** Four camera rooms in d2_prison_02, 03, 04, 05: one func_monitor, 3 or 4 point_camera (FOV 60 on 9 of 15, 20 to 75; pitched down 10 to 32 degrees on most, fog 2048 to 4096), func_button flags 1025, wait 0.3. Press: monitor_counter (startvalue 1, max 3 or 4, OnHitMax SetValue 0) OutValue > monitor_case.InValue > camera_N_relay: monitor_1.SetCamera, monitor_sound_1 (k_lab.monitorbutton, flags 48), monitor_sprite_N ShowSprite and the others HideSprite (glow01, scale .05), the button sprite Color 255 0 0 then 0 255 0 @0.3. d2_prison_02 camera_1 to 4_microphone: env_microphone MaxRange 512 to 1024, spawnflags 31, SpeakerName monitor_1, speaker_dsp_preset 58 or 59, enabled with their channel; a trigger_multiple round the room: StartTouch monitor Enable, EndTouch monitor and microphones Disable. d2_prison_05 laundry_camera_relay: the camera's two soldiers ForceSpawn, a schedule walks each to a path_corner whose OnPass Kills them, Surveillance_monitor_static1 Enable/Disable at 0, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 1.1, 1.2; a trigger_look on the screen fires it unasked (d2_prison_03, 05). d2_prison_08 logic_assault_blastdoor_1 and 2: the console's big monitor shows blastdoor_sec_camera_1 while the squad outside gathers (scripted run @0.1), the leader plays signal_group @0.2 and signal_advance @1.4, they move up @2.6, the monitor goes off @5 and the on-screen men are Killed @6; the real attack comes through the door later.
- **How:** Gamemode: the Combine console (city-events.md, 'Flying a scanner') could offer 'Watch the cameras': render-target views from 3 or 4 editor points ('Point: security camera') in the prison and the nexus, stepped with E or the number keys, a 0.1 to 1 s static overlay on each change, the nearest sounds at the camera relayed quietly to the watcher, nothing drawn when nobody watches. Map modules: lc_cam_N points (angles pitched 10 to 30 down) in prison and nexus pieces. The Resistance's own (d1_eli_01, Mossman's room, 304 by 216): a securitybank beside servers against one wall, one button stepping four cameras of rooms nobody walks (cam_chefy the kitchen, cam_recroom, cam_foyer, cam_stairs); a fifth camera over the outer airlock door feeds the guard's booth while he lets someone in. In d1_eli_02 the airlock monitor carries Eli's call and breaks to static (a noise brush for 0.5 s, then 7 s) when the attack hits. Our HQ's securitybank could show the base's own way in: ask the owner first.
- **Checked by:** review; a client smoke check that the render target is only drawn while someone uses the console
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, sound, gameplay

- **See:** `valve_eli/07_eli02_airlock2_cut.png`

### A Combine door opens in steps: lock light, beep, the model moves, then the passage clears (`valve-combine-door-steps`)

A Combine door does not just slide: its lock light comes on, an unlock beep follows a tenth of a second later, the door model starts its opening animation at half a second, the light goes out, and only at about 1.25 s does the way through clear (the solid part moves or the player clip goes off); the model settles into its open idle at 5 s. Big blast doors are many leaves opened one after another over about 2.5 s, and only some of the leaves make a sound. Changes how our Combine doors feel when they open: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_06 combine_door_rl_1: portal_boilerroom_1.Open @0, sprite_comblock_1 ShowSprite @0 (light_glow03, scale .08), sound_comblock_1 (d1_trainstation_01.combinelock_unlock, radius 1250, 48) @0.1, combine_door_model (combine_door01.mdl) SetAnimation Open @0.5, sprite HideSprite @0.8, combine_door_1 (func_door 64 by 128, spawnflags 544, lip 16, speed 150) Open @1.25, SetAnimation idle_Open @5. The interrogation room door (introom_door_1) is the same model with pClip_introom_door_1 Disabled as it opens. d2_prison_08 logic_door_comb_1_open: ten func_door leaves Open @0, 0.8, 1, 1.1, 1.5, 1.6, 1.9, 2.1, 2.3, 2.5, speeds 60 to 100, lips 16 to 68; 6 leaves sound (Doors.Move10, FullOpen10), 4 are flag 4096 silent; the close relay runs the same in another order with the player clip on first.
- **How:** Gamemode: a Combine door opened by a keycard, a unit or a hack could play the lock light and beep, then open 1.25 s later (one timer), instead of at once. Map modules: m.combine_door() as a prop_dynamic combine_door01 with a clip brush and a lock sprite; m.blast_door(leaves) giving each leaf its own delay and sound on every other leaf.
- **Checked by:** map_lint 'wire': a combine_door01 prop with no clip or door brush moving after its Open animation
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, doors, sound

### A Combine force field is a see-through shield and a clip that lets the Combine through (`valve-forcefield`)

Build a field from two parts: the shield you see (never solid) and a clip that does the blocking, either for everyone but Combine soldiers or for players only. Its poles show it is on, and turn dark with it. It hums within about 500 and buzzes while someone stands in it. Switched off, its sound fades out over half a second, sparks fly, the poles go dark. Our checkpoint forcefields block people by the gamemode: matching Valve's build, or letting Combine NPCs walk through, changes them: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_03 vort_field and hall_field: a nodraw brush with effects/combineshield/comshieldwall3 on two faces (func_brush Solidity 1, never solid) and a toolsclip brush with excludednpc npc_combine_s; d2_prison_07 func_croom2_field and func_room5_field_gate_1 to 4: the shield brush and a toolsplayerclip brush (only players held). Poles combine_fence01a and 01b Skin(1) when off. Sound: combine.sheild_loop (radius 500) and combine.sheild_touch started by a trigger_multiple round the field (StartTouch PlaySound, EndTouch StopSound). d2_prison_03 plug_pull2: hall_field Kill, forcefield3_sound_close and far Volume(.1) then Kill @1, sparks, turret_hall_poles Skin(1); d2_prison_06 logic_shield_off2: shutdown sound @0.2, Volume(0) @0.5. d2_prison_07 logic_room5_begin_assault turns four fields on across the gates @3 while the gates open and their node links TurnOn @5: players shut in, soldiers through.
- **How:** Map modules: m.forcefield(name, a, b, combine_pass=True) writing the shield brush, the clip (toolsclip with excludednpc, or toolsplayerclip), two pole props and the hum and buzz with their trigger. Gamemode: the checkpoint field (city-people.md) could buzz while touched and fade its hum when the power bank dies. The Citadel's shield walls (d3_citadel_03, 7 of them) add two things: a trigger_hurt of 10000 that the on relay Enables and Disables 0.1 s later, so whatever stands in the field as it rises dies; and the field fed by an energy ball in a socket (func_combine_ball_spawner flag 8192: OnLastBallGrabbed > off relay, OnBallReinserted > on relay; valve-ball-socket-power). The clip brush's excludednpc npc_combine_s lets the soldiers through; the poles' Skin 1 when off.
- **Checked by:** map_lint 'wire': a combineshield brush entity that is solid (Solidity not 1), or one with no clip twin in its box
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, sound, doors

- **See:** `valve_citadel/06_c03_ball_room_cut.png`, `valve_coast/08_coast08_bridge.png`, `valve_prison/17_prison08_teleport_hall.png`

### A door forced from outside is heard long before it gives: voices, blows, then beeps that quicken (`valve-breach-buildup`)

When attackers break into a held room, the defenders hear them first: voices behind the door now and then, a heavy blow and a shake on a slow beat, the beat quickening once the defenders are ready, then a charge's beeps coming faster and faster for about 8 s before the door blows and the first attackers come straight through. Nothing on screen says it. Changes how our raids and breaches play: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_08: timer_guards_bang (logic_timer random 0.8 to 3 s) > case_guard_talk (logic_case of 5, only Case01 plays d3_citadel.guards_talk: a line one time in five). logic_first_attack_sounds_1: big_crash_2 (explode_4, radius 5000) and shake_blastdoor_2 (amplitude 1, duration .75, radius 1240) @0, big_crash_4 @8, itself again @16. First turret set (math_count_turrets max 1) > logic_first_attack_sounds_2: crashes @0, 5, 12, 14, crash_4 @7, shakes @0 and 12, again @16. Both set (max 2) > logic_first_attack_sounds_3: logic_blast_beeps_1 @2 (beeps @0, 3, 4, 5, 6, 6.5, 7, 7.5), crashes @2, 2.5, 4, 5, 6.5, 7, 7.5, 8.5, 9, shakes @2, 6.5, 7.5, 9, 10, logic_first_attack_wave_start_1 @10: teleport_assault_door_L Break and explosion @0.2, smoke on @0.2 off @8, two first-wave makers Spawn, assault Activate @0.4 and BeginAssault @0.5, the guard chatter Killed.
- **How:** Gamemode: a unit's breach of a rebel door (Retake the block, a raid) and the Resistance's bypass charge on a Combine door could run this: muffled radio voices on random 1 to 3 s with one chance in five, a thump and a small shake every 16 s while units gather, beeps at 0, 3, 4, 5, 6, 6.5, 7, 7.5 and the door open at 10 s, the units in at once. One timer chain, cancelled if the breach is called off. The dead do it too (ep1_c17_02a hospital): a timer every 1 to 1.5 s opens a door a crack and shuts it 0.5 s later with a banging loop until a player opens it and the poison zombie comes out; the holdout room waits a random 20 to 40 s, then bangs at @0, 2, 4, 5, 5.5, 5.8, 6 and 6.1 and the door breaks at @6.3 with a group spawn, all cancelled if the door is broken first. A clinic under siege by the infested could run the same. EP2's numbers: blows on a door at 0, 2, 4, 5, 5.5, 5.8, 6 and 6.1 s before it bursts (ep2_outland_06 lrelay_sound_banging_01); a breaching charge beeps every 2 s, every 0.2 s from 8 s and blows at 9 s, the door swapped for a broken one and the assault at 9.5 s (ep2_outland_07 relay_bustfrontdoor, timer_frontExitBomb).
- **Checked by:** review; a test that a cancelled breach leaves no pending sounds
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, sound, gameplay

### A guard tower's gun is manned, lit while manned, and holds its fire until the tower is attacked (`valve-guard-tower`)

A tower gun has a man at it; its spotlight rides the gun and is lit only while someone mans it. It locks on for a second or more before it fires, and does little harm to a person at range. It does not reach far until one of the tower's men is hurt; then it opens its range and aims at the attacker. Its first shot sounds the alarm for the whole yard. A tower whose crew is dead is shut. Changes how our checkpoints and turrets behave: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_01: 7 func_tank (bunker_gun01.mdl, firerate 15, bullet_damage 26, bullet_damage_vs_player 5, maxRange 1400, playerlocktimebeforefire 1.5, spawnflags 1312, npc_man_point turret_N_standhere); turret_N_spotlight (point_spotlight length 1024, width 64) parented to the model: GotController LightOn and animations activate then idle, LostController LightOff, retract, idle_inactive. Tower squads of 3 (combine_soldier_prisonguard, smg1 near, ar2 further). Soldier OnDamagedByPlayer > tower_N_under_attack: relationship hate, turret SetMaxRange 3000, SetTargetEntityName @0.1. Fire > tower_N_alarm_relay @1: tower sprite ShowSprite, alarm_sound_relay: coast.bunker_siren1 and 2 (radius 10000, flags 17). tower_3_counter (max 3, OnDeath) > tower_3_blocker Enable. d2_prison_04 turret_1: maxRange 1700, ignoregraceupto 768, playerlocktimebeforefire 1; an npc_enemyfinder (search 100 to 2048) aims it (FoundEnemy > SetTargetEntity); a maker spawns its gunner and FindNPCToManTank @0.1.
- **How:** Gamemode: the Overwatch's turrets and the barricade with a mounted gun (editor-placing.md) could keep a spotlight on only while manned, wait 1 to 1.5 s on a target before firing, and set off the checkpoint's alarm on their first shot. Map modules: m.guard_tower() with the gun's stand-here point, the spotlight and a squad of three spots.
- **Checked by:** map_lint 'wire': a func_tank with a spotlight that no Got/LostController switches
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, npc, lighting

- **See:** `valve_prison/01_prison01_gun_tower_cut.png`, `valve_prison/02_prison01_yard.png`

### A held room under steady pressure: each attacker killed is replaced a second later, until the time is up (`valve-npc-steady-pressure`)

Besides waves, Valve holds a room by keeping a set number of attackers alive: each death spawns one replacement a second later at a randomly chosen spawn point, the first few deaths each add one more (it gets harder), a manhack comes back ten seconds after it falls. When the time is up nobody new comes, and the hold ends when the last attacker is dead. An attacker who is stuck must not keep it open: a trigger over every place they reach notices when none are left, and a failsafe ends it a few minutes after the time anyway. Fits the owner's exception to house rule 9 (holding ground against attacks); changes how our counterattacks run: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_07 room 5 (Valve's comments quoted): template soldier OnDeath > counter_room5_total_living_enemies Subtract 1 and logic_room5_on_combine_death: 'Spawn a single combine in the lower level' @1 (logic_case PickRandom over 5 lower makers, AddMaxChildren 1 and Enable; 3 upper makers likewise), counter_room5_combine_deaths Add @1 > OutValue > logic_spawn_extra_badguys ('At intervals, this case is used to add extra bad guys': cases 1 to 4 add a lower soldier, a manhack, a lower, an upper); manhack OnDeath > logic_room5_spawn_manhack @10. timer_room5_assault (RefireTime 190) > logic_room5_assault_can_finish: spawning Killed, compare_all_enemies_dead ('Whenever it finds itself at 0, it triggers the finish-assault relay'). d2_prison_08 the same with 184 s, the third way in opened only after 8 deaths, 5 s later and once no player is in its trigger. Control room 2: 'Trigger fires when all combine in the wave are killed. Should stretch over all the places combine get to' (trigger_multiple filter Combine and manhacks, EndTouchAll > finished @6) and logic_croom2_failsafe 180 s after the 480 s timer: 'on the assumption it's because a combine's stuck somewhere'.
- **How:** Gamemode: a counterattack on a held zone (city.md) could take a cap N, replace each death after 1 s at a random spawn of the zone's (never one a player can see), add 1 for each of the first 4 deaths, stop at the time limit, end when the zone holds no attacker or 180 s after the limit. Tests: the count never passes N plus 4; the hold ends with a stuck attacker. d3_citadel_04's lift wait caps the living, not the dead: a logic_timer of 4 to 10 s picks one of 8 cases (three doors with env_entity_makers of 3 soldiers, or manhacks) only while fewer than 4 are alive (soldier_counter: each spawn Adds 3, each death Subtracts 1; OutValue > logic_compare 4 Enables or Disables the timer); 6 waves are allowed until the lift is called, then the timer narrows to 2 to 5 s and 12 more are allowed; the lift's first stop Kills the timer. The stasis room does the same with 3 alive and 8 waves.
- **Checked by:** a unit test of the cap and the failsafe; robots.py: a hold where attackers cannot path still ends
- **Tags:** valve, npc, logic, combine, gameplay

### The PA speaks at each turn of events and names the block (`valve-pa-names-the-block`)

The prison's places are lettered and numbered, and the Overwatch voice names them: contact in block B2, the intruder in block D8, kill the prisoners in A7. A line plays once, when something changes (a stage is reached, a gate opens, enough guards are down), heard within a block (1250) and only the gravest across the prison. Soldiers' radio chatter names its control room the same way. Changes what our PA says: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** 13 Overwatch lines in 7 maps (prison.overwatch_antlions_inside_prison1, antlions_in_prison2, freeman_spotted, contact_block_b2, kill_prisoners_A7, mission_failure_punish, alyx_reprograms_turret, freeman_in_blockD8, communications_out) and 10 soldier radio lines (prison.sradio_A5_controlroom, A7, B4, D7, D8_turrets, D9_general), all one-shot (flags 48 or 49), radius 1250 except kill_prisoners_A7 (10000) and communications_out (8000). Fired by relays (33), trigger_once (16), a scene's script event, a gate's OnOpen (d2_prison_07 door_exit_gate_1), a counter of deaths (d2_prison_04 radio_counter max 12 > radio_2 @2).
- **How:** Gamemode: custom-announcements.md and the prison could give each cell block and Combine area a short name (from the editor zone) that PA lines put in ('Contact, block B2'); one line per change of state, never a repeat while it holds. The owner's own recorded lines come first.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, sound

### A prisoner held in beams, freed by pulling the plug (`valve-prisoner-in-beams`)

Valve's interrogation rig holds the prisoner inside a force field with beams from two poles, lit by thin spotlights and humming with three machine loops that start as someone comes near. Freeing him is one physical act, pulling the plug out of the wall: the beams cut out in two steps a quarter second apart, the end glows die, three shutdown sounds follow over half a second, the field goes, the poles go dark. A new piece for our interrogation room and prison breaks: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_03: torture_beam_A_01, B_01, A_02, B_02 (env_beam BoltWidth .75, laserbeam, Radius 256, spawnflags 3), torture_beam_sprites (glow01 .125 and .5) and two point_spotlight (2048 long, 12 and 16 wide); torture_beam_sounds k_lab.ambient_powergenerators, k_lab.teleport_malfunction_sound, k_lab.mini_teleport_warmup (radius 400, flags 16) started by a trigger_once. plug (func_physbox, forcetoenablemotion 5, damagetoenablemotion 1, held by breakable_plug_bit): OnMotionEnabled, OnPhysGunPickup, OnPlayerUse > plug_pull: beams A_01 and B_01 TurnOff @0, A_02 and B_02 @0.25, Endpoints TurnOn @0 and TurnOff @0.6, sounds apc_engine_stop @0, electrical_zap_1 @0.3, explode_7 @0.5, vort_field Kill, vort_field_poles Skin(1), plug_removal_sparks SparkOnce, the loops StopSound.
- **How:** Map modules: an interrogation piece with two pole props, beam ends and a plug in the wall (lc_rig_*). Gamemode: Civil Protection's interrogation (city-administration.md) could hold the prisoner in the rig while questioned, and the Resistance's prison break could end with pulling the plug instead of a minigame step (or after it).
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, sound, gameplay

- **See:** `valve_prison/13_prison06_interrogation.png`

### The prison's machines run on a beat of their own: pod lines halt and go, trains come through (`valve-machines-on-a-beat`)

The Combine's machinery keeps moving with nobody watching, on loops that call themselves: prisoner pods on their conveyors halt for 2 or 3 s every 18 to 22 s and move on; a train sounds its horn, moves off a second later, shakes the ground in steps as it passes and is removed at the end of its track; the next comes some 35 s after. Prisoners are shown this way, in pods and on monitors, not kept standing in cells. Changes how our trains and depot behave: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_06 logic_podtrains_L_control_1 (logic_relay): logic_podtrains_L_toggle_1 @0 and @2 (podTrain_L_3 and L_4 Toggle), itself @22; the upper line toggles @0 and @3, itself @18; pods are func_tracktrain (startspeed 100, novaprospekt.pods_track_move_1 and stop_1); five PodPerson citizens in pods play np_podperson.vcd and are Killed when the next scene starts. logic_Train_comethrough_start_1: outside_traindoors_1 Toggle, razortrain_horn (radius 6000) @4, StartForward @5, SetSpeed 500 @5.1; TrainPath_L2_2b OnPass: Idle_Trains_shake_1 (radius 700, frequency 5) StartShake, Amplitude 2 @2, 3 @6, 2 @9, 1 @12, StopShake @18; logic_Train_comethrough_over_1 calls the next @35; TrainPath_L_X OnPass > KillHierarchy @0.5; steam TurnOff as it passes. No npc_citizen stands in a cell in the eight maps.
- **How:** Gamemode: the train (city-people.md, lc_train_minutes) could sound its horn 1 s before it moves and shake those nearby 2, 3, 2, 1 over 18 s. Map modules: a depot or prison piece with a pod conveyor (func_tracktrain on a looped path) and the relay that halts it 2 s in every 22. The Citadel's pod rings keep in step another way (d3_citadel_01, 04, 05, ep1_citadel_00): 7 pods on a ring of 14 path_tracks (4749 long, speed 100), each stopping at its own node (OnPass !activator Stop) and adding 1 to one math_counter (max 7); OnHitMax fires the resume relay 3 s later (4 s on the other ring) and resets the counter, so the slowest pod holds the rest and the ring never drifts; one stop is a job (the zapper: warm-up @2.5, energy core discharge, beam, sparks and a trigger_hurt @4, all off 6 s later), another opens the pod (rotator Open @0, pod @1, Close @4 and @5). Diggers on 2-node rings are started by one logic_auto at 1, 4 and 6 s so they never move together. A train that seems to leave without moving (ep1_citadel_04): the escape train stands still while a ring of three point_spotlights and a brush on a func_tracktrain (train_trainlights_1) runs past it at 2200 round 9 path_tracks 2560 long (a pass every 1.2 s), with the razor-train wheel loop and shakes every 12 to 30 s.
- **Checked by:** map_lint 'wire': a relay that calls itself with no delay (Valve's loops wait 18 to 35 s)
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, sound

- **See:** `valve_citadel/01_c01_pod_bay_cut.png`, `valve_citadel/02_c01_zapper_station_cut.png`

### Lights come back bank by bank with a clunk, and go out the same way before an ambush (`valve-lights-bank-by-bank`)

When power comes back, a room's lights do not all come on at once: one bank, then the next two seconds later, the last after six, each with a clunk and its lamp model turning lit. When the Combine strike in the dark, they kill the lights the same way, one by one half a second apart, each with its own clunk, and then light the target with flares. No flicker (the owner's rule): each light changes once. Changes how our blackouts end: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_06 relay_room1_lights: light_room1_1 TurnOn and prop_light_room1_1 Skin(1) @0, sound_lighton_3 (Buttons.snd4) and light 3 @2, light 4 @2.5, sound_lighton_5 and light 5 @6, light 6 @6.5. d2_prison_08 ambush_relay (fired by a trigger, any ambush soldier's OnFoundPlayer, or OnDamaged @1): light_shutdown_relay: light_booth off with light_booth_sound (Doors.FullClose9, radius 2500) @0, light 1 @0.5, light 2 @1, lights 3 and 4 @1.5, each with its own sound and its sprite hidden, fog SetColor 0 0 0; combine_flare_chatter @1; flare_relay @4.5: flares Launch(800 to 1100) @0, 0.4, 1.3, their lights on 0.4 to 0.5 s after each; soldiers Wake @1.6, assault @1.7.
- **How:** Gamemode: when the grid comes back after a blackout (city-people.md, transformers), turn the district's lamps on in three or four groups 0, 2, 2.5 and 6 s apart, a clunk at each; a Combine night raid could kill a block's lamps one by one before the units go in.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, logic, lighting, sound

### A place's soundscape comes in combat levels (`valve-soundscape-combat-levels`)

Each kind of room has the same soundscape in up to three levels (very light, light, heavy combat): the distant fighting in it grows the nearer the war is. Valve places the level by section; a running city could switch it with what is happening. Changes how our places sound: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** 186 env_soundscape in d2_prison_01 to 08, none switched by logic (2 named): prison.general_hallway_lightcombat 29 and vlightcombat 26, larger_cellblock_hvycombat 19, lightcombat 9, vlightcombat 17, outdoor_courtyard 4 and hvycombat 5, control_room 12 and control_room_simple 8, showers 6, laundry 6, general_dripping_tunnel 15, util_quiet_cellblock 2 and util_louder_cellblock 3. Heavy in p1 and p2 (the war at the gates), very light from p6. Radius 128 (79) or -1 (57).
- **How:** Gamemode: while a skirmish, raid or counterattack runs in a district, its places could play their heavier soundscape (env_soundscape_triggerable or the client's own), back to the quiet one when it ends. Map modules: each piece's soundscape name with a _combat twin.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, sound, combine

### Antlions set on a post by bait: a zone per target, its own burrows, the others shut (`valve-bait-zones`)

Turning a horde on a defended post is done by zones: bait landing in a zone sends the antlions at that post's gunner, opens that zone's burrows (a plug in the floor bursts) and shuts the others, and raises how many may be out at once. The gunner sent to man a gun is ignored for his first few seconds so he gets there. A new kind of raid: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_01: point_bugbait tower_bugbaits (radius 512 to 830) OnBaited > tower_N_bugbait_relay: disable_spawnpoints (every group DisableHint), antspawner_1 SetFightTarget(turret_N_standhere), tower_N_spawn_nodes (info_node_hint hinttype 400, StartHintDisabled 1) EnableHint @0.1, SetMaxLiveChildren(4) @0.2, the tower's own maker (2 or 3, every 0.1 s) Enable @0.2. 19 point_bugbait in 5 maps (radius 112 to 830, median 512). Indoors (d2_prison_03 to 05): burrow_N_relay: spawnplug_N (concrete_spawnplug001a, 12) Break, spawnplug_breaker_N (env_physexplosion) Explode, burrow hints on, maker Spawn @0.1. d2_prison_04 turret_guy_spawner OnSpawnNPC: ants_vs_func_tank (antlions neutral to the gunner) ApplyRelationship @0.1, RevertRelationship @7.
- **How:** Gamemode: if the owner wants a Resistance raid with antlions (the forest's nest, a vortigaunt's call), give each Combine post a bait zone and a few burrow spots; a burrow bursts a floor plug as the first comes out. Map modules: lc_burrow_N points with a plug prop.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, npc, logic, gameplay

### A hold's clock is a machine players can watch and hear, never a number (`valve-progress-in-the-world`)

When players must hold out until something finishes, show how far it has got in the world: a machine moving slowly through set stops, a hum rising in pitch at each stop, dials that fill one after another and light when full, and a person saying 'halfway', 'almost there', 'just seconds'. No timer on screen. Fits house rule 10 (keep what players read to a minimum); changes how our timed holds and objectives show their time: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d2_prison_08: logic_turretsdeployed > logic_start_teleporter_powerup > logic_teleporter_powered_up @184. teleport_lift_train_1 (func_tracktrain, SetSpeed .06) sinks from 1494 to 1070 past 12 path_tracks about 38 apart (teleport_track_2a_1 to 12); each OnPass: teleporter_charging_timed_sound_1 (teleport_active_loop, radius 5000) Pitch 60, 65, 70, 75, 80, 85, 90, 100, 110, 120, 140, then 165 at teleport_track_3; every second one Triggers logic_wheel_1 to 6 (tp_timer_N, combine_tptimer.mdl, SetAnimation 30sec, its beam Toggle, lamp TurnOn and sprite at 28 s, back to loop at 30); telelightning sequences at 2a_3, 5, 6, 8, 9, 10, 11; Alyx's scenes lcs_al_halfway @2 at 2a_7, lcs_al_takingforever @7 at 2a_9, lcs_al_almostthere @6 at 2a_11, lcs_al_justseconds @8 at 2a_12.
- **How:** Gamemode: a timed hold (a counterattack, an upload at a data terminal, a flush gate opening) could raise a looping sound's pitch in 6 to 12 steps over its length and have a person or the radio say halfway and almost there, instead of a countdown on screen. Map modules: a hold piece's machine with stops (a lift, a gate, dials) driven from the gamemode. ep1_c17_00a's lift: the floor lamps over the shaft step 1, 2, 3 at @0, 20 and 40 (a logic_case of sprites) and 4 when the car arrives about 80 s after the start, while zombie waves come at @5, 35, 65 and 85: the lit row is the clock.
- **Checked by:** house_rules.py already counts text on screen; review
- **Tags:** valve, logic, combine, sound, gameplay

- **See:** `valve_citadel/12_breen_portal_chamber.png`, `valve_citadel/21_e03_core_chamber_cut.png`, `valve_citadel/24_style_core_cut.png`

### Combine floor turrets stand in pairs, with a soldier close by (`valve-turret-pairs`)

Valve's Combine floor turrets are set two together, about 90 to 110 apart, covering a corridor's end, a stair or a doorway, and a soldier stands within about 300 of half of them; they arrive with that soldier, spawned together out of sight when players come. A soldier can also carry one in and set it down. Where our editor or Overwatch places turrets, this is a placing practice; changing how turrets are deployed: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** 30 Combine npc_turret_floor in d2_prison_02 to 06: nearest other turret 64 / 87 / 111 / 328 / 608 (10th, 25th, 50th, 75th, 90th percentile), nearest soldier 182 / 254 / 323 / 897. d2_prison_05 laundry_defense (point_template Template01 laundry_soldier_1, Template02 laundry_turret), stairway_turret_spawn, kitchen_turrets 4 from templates. d2_prison_03 counterattack_spawner_2: prop_turret1 SetParent to the soldier, SetAnimation carry_pose @0.1, SetParentAttachment anim_attachment_LH @0.1, ss_turretDrop01 (turret_drop) @0.2; its OnScriptEvent01 Kills the prop and ForceSpawns template_turretFloor where it is set.
- **How:** Map modules: Combine pieces mark turret spots in pairs (lc_turret_N_a and _b, 96 apart, facing the way in) with a soldier spot within 300. Gamemode: an Overwatch unit deploying turrets could set two at once.
- **Checked by:** map_lint: a lone lc_turret spot with no partner within 160
- **Tags:** valve, npc, combine, props

### A hidden base's way in is an airlock a guard watches: two blast doors 264 apart, a booth with a camera, a check that takes half a minute (`valve-base-airlock`)

From outside the way into a dug-in base is a plain door in the wall it is cut into, with only a few drums, pallets and a fence by it. Behind it: an airlock about 208 wide, 175 high and 264 to 272 between its doors; the outer door two leaves sliding apart quickly, the inner one slower and heavier; a keypad about 52 up beside each door. A small booth (about 115 by 192, ceiling 96) looks into it, with a screen fed by a camera over the outer door and monitors angled at the seat. Letting someone in is a show of about 30 s: spray, smoke, a scan, then the inner door opens. Our bunker's sliding door is settled (the owner liked it, 19 November): an airlock in front of it, or a guard's check at the way in, is new and changes how players enter a base: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d1_eli_01 (21 November): outer doors_Airlock_Outside and b, func_door movedir 0 and 180, speed 100, lip 8, wait 4, each under a blastdoor001a or b prop; inner airlock_door and b at speed 40 and 50, lip 12; doors at y 2872 and 2608 (264 apart), 208 wide inside, ceiling 173. The second airlock (to the scrapyard, d1_eli_01 and 02): doors at y 1160 and 888 (272 apart), 208 wide, ceiling 177, speed 25 to 45, its own booth at x -720. Booth: ceiling 96, 115 by 192, controlroom_monitor001b pitched 30, four monitor01b pitched 37 to 54 and turned 7 to 47 toward the seat; point_camera camera_mossman_airlock over the door feeds func_monitor monitor_airlock_entrance; env_microphone mic_airlockMossman. 21 keypad prop_dynamics in the two maps, 48 to 54 over the floor (median 53), 14 to 64 from a door leaf. Outside the door: the dam's own concrete (concretewall064b, c, d: 59% of walls within 2000), gravel and red grass, and 15 props: 5 oil drums, 4 fence pieces, 3 pallets, 2 crates, a garbage pile.
- **How:** Map modules: an `airlock` piece for a Resistance site (two door pairs 264 apart named lc_airlock_outer and lc_airlock_inner, a booth with lc_airlock_cam pointed at the outer door, keypads 52 up), the outer front dressed like the wall it sits in. Gamemode: the Resistance's guard (a player or the quartermaster) opens the inner door from the booth; the 30 s check (steam, a hum, a light sweeping) plays as sound and effects, never as text. Correction from lifting the piece (valve_eli_airlock, 21 November): the camera is not over the outer door; it sits in the booth on the guard's face, and the airlock's screen shows the guard to the visitor. The whole check takes about 43 s in the piece's wiring.
- **Checked by:** map_lint: an airlock piece whose doors are under 192 apart, or a door with no keypad or button within 64
- **Tags:** valve, doors, gameplay, layout

- **See:** `valve_eli/02_eli01_airlock_cut.png`, `valve_eli/07_eli02_airlock2_cut.png`

### Guns that seem to follow you are props: six floor cells choose where they aim (`valve-turrets-follow-by-cells`)

When a defence only has to look alive (turrets over a door, a camera that turns), make it a prop with aiming animations and lay a grid of touch triggers on the floor: each cell plays the aim for that cell, and the aiming sound's pitch is picked at random from three each time. The guns drop from ceiling panels when the place is on guard and go back when the guard lets you in. Turrets that shoot are another thing (valve-turret-pairs).

- **Why:** d1_eli_01 airlock (21 November): two labturret prop_dynamics behind func_door panels (speed 175, lip 2, opened by logic_turrets_1_deploy, shut 2.2 s after retract); six trigger_multiple Trigger_Prox_turrets in two columns of three (centres x -124 and 8, y 2646, 2744, 2846: cells about 132 by 100), each OnStartTouch > prop_turret_1_L SetAnimation aimN and prop_turret_1_R aim_MN (0 to 0.3 s apart) and > Case_turrets_variety PickRandom, which sets ambient_sound_turrets_1_aim to pitch 200, 215 or 230. A glow sprite on each gun's light attachment. Retracted when Mossman opens his blast door (lcs_airlock01 OnTrigger2).
- **How:** Gamemode: a fixture 'Turret, watching' for the Resistance's way in (props_lab/labturret.mdl) that turns toward the nearest stranger in its cone a few times a second (in Lua no grid is needed), with a whir at a random pitch, and folds away when a member is near. New: ask the owner before adding it to a base.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, props, logic, performance

- **See:** `valve_eli/02_eli01_airlock_cut.png`

### A long lift ride down into a base shows its life floor by floor (`valve-lift-diorama`)

When the way into a base goes down a long way, use the ride: an open cage in a shaft that passes floors you can see into but not reach (sleeping quarters, a kitchen, a workshop), each with a few people at work started just before it comes into view and removed once the lift is past. The floor ahead is opened to sight as the lift reaches it and the one behind is shut a moment later. A guide rides along. A lift, or floors only seen, would be new to our bases: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d1_eli_01 (21 November): func_tracktrain elevator_lab (startspeed 65) carrying a freightelevator prop on seven path_tracks from z -1214 to -2672 (1458, about 22 s); shaft 176 by 800, 1456 high. Floors passed at z -1584 (dormitory), -1872 (kitchen), -2160 (repairs): 288 apart, no AI nodes on any of them, only elevatordoor props as gates. elevator_trigger_go_up_1 starts them on the ride's clock: logic_loungers_start @4, logic_kitchenscene_start @6.5, logic_RepairmenScene_Start @14.2, lift Resume @3. path_tracks 2a, 2b, 2c: OnPass > portal_elevator_ride_N+1 Open @0 and portal_elevator_ride_N Close @3. The last stop: shake, ding @1, doors open, logic_KILL_EM_ALL KillHierarchy on the vignettes' NPCs. The lift moves only once the guide is in (trigger_once for NPCs enabling the player's trigger).
- **How:** Map modules: a `lift` piece (a func_tracktrain or func_movelinear cage, doors of two leaves at top and bottom, lip 16, speed 100, wait 4) with lc_life_N spots on the floors it passes. Gamemode: life spots on a passing floor are filled when a rider is within reach and emptied after, like the camps' fighters who slip away out of sight (city.md). d3_citadel_02's pod ride is the same idea at the Citadel's scale: 56 path_tracks, 24581 units, about 147 s at 100 to 250, 13 nodes that set something off (a thing to see about every 11 s): the next hall's door Opens as the pod nears and the last Closes behind it, workers and guards are ForceSpawned ahead and Killed behind, container trains on a 15206 ring start 0, 2 and 4 s apart as the pod passes.
- **Checked by:** map_lint: each areaportal a lift opens on the way is shut again by a later stop

### A Resistance base is shown at work: cooks with their tools, sleepers, a game of chess, vortigaunts mending with their beams (`valve-base-at-work`)

Fill the base with work, each person on his own spot with the tools in his hands: two cooks at a stove (a hat on the head, a ladle or a cleaver in the hand, put there as the scene starts), one man asleep on a bed and two at a chess table in the quarters, vortigaunts zapping a broken machine in short bursts, a lab worker at his bench who stops when spoken to and goes back after, and one person walking a loop between two places with a pause at each end. Changes how our camps' people behave: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d1_eli_01 (21 November): vortigaunts sheffy_soup and sheffy_butcher on scripted_sequences soupstir and Butcher, sheffyhat, sheffyLadel and sheffyCleaver prop_dynamics put on by SetParentAttachment (hat, ladel_attachment, cleaver_attachment) in logic_kitchenscene_start; cit_lounger02 on SS Lying_Down over a prison bed, cit__lounger01 on Sit_Chair and vort__lounger01 on chess_Pose 48 apart across props_lab/chess; vort_repairman_01 and 02 made to hate npc_bullseye bullseye_repairman1 (ApplyRelationship @0 and @0.75, Revert @6, the scene removed @7); Eli, Mossman, Alyx and the lab vort each on a work loop scene that a talk pauses (busyactor 1) and resumes after; the walker: SS_bgwalker1 OnEndSequence > SS_bgwalker2 @3, its end > SS_bgwalker1 @6. 7 vortigaunts and 5 citizens in the map, none idle.
- **How:** Gamemode: the camps' fighters and the vortigaunts (loyalty-and-vortigaunts.md) could take work spots from the editor ('Point: cook', 'Point: mend', 'Point: chess', 'Point: sleep') with a prop parented to the hand while there; a mending vortigaunt plays its beam at the spot's machine every few seconds. Map modules: lc_busy_cook, lc_busy_mend and lc_busy_chess points in kitchens, workshops and quarters (valve-npc-busy-poses). EP2's White Forest (ep2_outland_11, 12a): a vortigaunt with a broom walks between four sweeping spots picked at random after each sweep (case_sweepSpots PickRandom on the scene's completion), a dust puff on while it sweeps; two welders (a spark and sound every 1.5 to 3 s; skip their light, no flickering); a gun class (a teacher with an AR2, three pupils with arms crossed); a consoling couple (ai_goal_actbusy); odd-job rebels from makers, each given one of two random jobs (ep2_outland_10a).
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, npc, props, gameplay

- **See:** `valve_coast/12_coast11_camp_cut.png`, `valve_eli/03_eli01_kitchen_floor.png`, `valve_ep2/27_o11b_base_workrooms_cut.png`, `valve_ep2/30_o12a_launch_control_cut.png`

### Give players a moment to look round, and let the person there answer what they look at (`valve-look-and-answer`)

After a talk, leave a short free moment (about 25 s) in which a person in the room says something about whatever a player stops to look at: a photo, a machine, a board on the wall. Looking means within reach (70 to 190) and the thing near the middle of the view (a cone of 32 to 80 degrees) for half a second to a second. Once one is said the others are dropped and things move on. Spoken, never written. New: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d1_eli_01 (21 November): four ai_script_conditions for Eli, enabled @0.7 to 1.5 by logic_timer_lookaround_enable_1 and switched off @25 by a kill switch: conditions_photo (frame002a, within 120, cone 32, 0.5 s), conditions_teleport (190, 80, 0.5 s), conditions_thing (70, 35, 0.5 s), conditions_breen (corkboard002, 150, 35, 1 s). OnConditionsSatisfied starts that line once and disables the other three; each line ends by resuming Eli's work loop.
- **How:** Gamemode: a base's quartermaster or a vortigaunt answers a player who looks at a marked thing ('Point: talking point' with a recorded line, recorded-lines.md) for 0.7 s within 150 and a 40 degree cone, once a player in 10 minutes. No text. EP2's monitor watcher (ep2_outland_11): a button steps a screen through five channels (math_counter 1 to 6 wrapping, logic_case); the rebel thanks you when it is back on his, reacts when you change it, and after four changes away says he is fed up (math_mon_hatelife max 4).
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, npc, gameplay, sound

### The Combine's attack on a base is told by the base itself, and comes at a moment nobody chose (`valve-raid-on-the-base`)

When the Combine hit the Resistance's base, the base tells it: a blast and a shake far off, emergency lamps blinking and a fire bell, the screen in the booth breaking into static and going dark, doors shutting behind the defenders, scanners and canisters over the yard, and only then the first enemy inside. Part of the base can be lost to a cave-in. It starts at a random moment in a window of minutes, not on a clock players can learn. The raid on a base is not built (resistance-base.md): ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d1_eli_02 (21 November): timer_attack refire 80, upper bound 180, fires once during the scrapyard play. logic_attack_start: scenes @0, battlefx_0 @8 (six env_particlescript scanner swarms, three npc_cscanner on aiscripted_schedules, env_headcrabcanister HCC1 to 4 @4, 9, 12, 15, chopper_flyby_04 @12), battlefx_1 @12 (shake, explode_9, the battle loop @3, explode_2 @12). lcs_attack01: scrapyard gate @12, airlock @14, rockets armed @20. trigger_attack02 shuts the airlock behind. lcs_attack02: Eli on monitor_airlock_south, then monitor_airlock_south_noise on for 0.5 s, later for 7 s, and the off brush. logic_start_fighting_1: cop_fight_maker against vort_fight_maker, cops_vs_Eli_ignore. The cave-in (battlefx_3, trigger_cavein): breakable_rockfall_2 @0.5, physboxes dropped @1, rope_L_5 Break @1 with sparks, rock showers @1 and @3.5, dust @0.5 and @3, a ceiling func_door (speed 450, lip -128) closing @2. sound_firebell_1 loop on lcs_attack01's end.
- **How:** Gamemode (when the owner wants raids on the base): the HQ's monitors show static, its lamps blink and a bell rings for the defenders; far blasts and a shake reach everyone in the base; units arrive 20 to 60 s later through the way in; the start chosen at random within a few minutes of the order. Map modules: lc_hq_alarm lamps and an lc_hq_bell sound in the HQ pieces.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, gameplay, sound, combine, lighting

- **See:** `valve_eli/06_eli02_scrapyard.png`, `valve_eli/07_eli02_airlock2_cut.png`, `valve_eli/08_eli02_raven_tunnel.png`, `valve_ep2/25_o11a_base_halls_cut.png`

### Teach a tool by play: someone reacts to every try, lost things come back, and a goal shows when it is hit (`valve-play-teaches`)

To teach a tool, give players something to play with and a person (or animal) who reacts: pleased when they catch or carry it, let down when it breaks. Throwables that break or get lost come back from where they came. A goal (a hoop, a target) is a pair of touch zones, the upper one arming the lower for a second, so only a real score counts; a hit lights a sprite and gets one word of praise. The first real test opens the way on; a way round it opens anyway if they cannot (valve-teach-then-give-up). Fits our job games (house rule 9).

- **Why:** d1_eli_02 scrapyard (21 November): 10 dogobject crates each from an env_entity_maker (maker_box1 to 10) with its point_template; w_1_obj_N OnPhysGunPickup > logic_C_yes_N (Alyx's praise picked by a logic_case), OnBreak > logic_C_no_N (lcs_dog_dissapointed). Ball from maker_ball; trigger_Ball_vent and trigger_lost_ball_top start 'where's the ball' and break it @6 so the maker makes another. Hoop: trigger_BBall_score_top enables trigger_BBall_score_bottom, which is disabled again 1 s after the ball leaves the top; a score shows Sprite_score for 2 s and lcs_al_niceshot once. timer_fetch 60 s: Alyx suggests fetch if nobody has played. First test: a ledge 130 up beside the gate button (trigger_scrap_1st_success unlocks button_scrapyard_gate); env_speaker AlyxStackNag 12 to 24 s.
- **How:** Gamemode job games: a crate-stacking or catch game for the Resistance's recruits where a vortigaunt or the quartermaster reacts aloud to each try; props a game uses come back from a spawn spot when broken or lost; any goal made of two zones. No text beyond the one key. EP2's striderbuster school (ep2_outland_11b): the practice devices are duds (dud 1); the teacher's scene pauses at each step and resumes when it is done (Pause, then Resume from a trigger), a nag after 20 s of dawdling; a missed throw (OnShatter) gets one of three lines and a new device from the pad 1 s later.
- **Checked by:** review
- **Tags:** valve, gameplay, logic

- **See:** `valve_eli/05_eli02_scrapyard_cut.png`, `valve_eli/06_eli02_scrapyard.png`

### A base dug into the ground is one plain concrete, banded low, hung with salvage (`valve-base-concrete-and-salvage`)

Build the Resistance's underground rooms in one plain board-marked concrete for walls, floors and ceilings alike, with a lower band of a second concrete to 112 or 128 (the owner's two tones hold here too), rubber mats on the lab floor, and dress it with what was carried in: runs of pipe props along walls and ceilings, chain-link frames standing as partitions, the prison's tube and cage lamps, a keypad by every door. Labs are higher than homes (ceilings 320 to 432). Light the work rooms a pale green fluorescent and dim, the quarters warmer and brighter, with plaster and painted walls. Where our bunker or cave office differs, ask the owner first.

- **Why:** d1_eli_01 (21 November): concretewall002a on 4610 of 15794 faces; in the lower lab 46% of wall, 55% of floor and 50% of ceiling area; rubberfloor009a 14% of the lab floor; first wall change at 112 on 422 and at 128 on 565 of 2412 walls (concretewall002c or 020a below, 002a above). 147 pipe props (93% square), 70 fence props (interior_fence002a to e, 001g; 91% square), 29 prison_flourescentlight001a, 22 prison_cagedlight001a, 7 walllight001a, 21 keypads in the two maps. Main lab 304 to 554 by 800 to 874, ceiling 320 to 432; Mossman's room 304 by 216, ceiling 128. Lower lab light: 37 light_spots and 6 lights, the commonest colour 169 205 188 (18 of them), median brightness 140, no styled light. Quarters: median 700, 232 213 174 and 202 222 165; walls concretewall002c 36%, concretewall021c 17%, plasterwall030c 15%; ceilings concreteceiling003a and plasterceiling006a.
- **How:** Map tools: a `base` palette for Resistance interiors under ground (walls concrete/concretewall002a over 002c to 128, floors 002a with rubberfloor009a mats, ceilings 002a), pipes and interior_fence002 frames as dressing, keypads 52 up beside doors, lights 169 205 188 at about 140 in work rooms. White Forest (ep2_outland_11, 11a, 12a; 21 November) is the same idea larger: concretewall010a on 43 to 45% of all brush area, banded in 59 to 63% of wall columns with the first change at 64 or 128 (ep2_concretewall01a over 01c, concretewall_bunker05b over 05a), ceilings median 192 (quartiles 186 and 229), corridors median 181 to 248 wide; steel sliding doors are props_silo/silo_door01 carried by a func_door 99 by 123; light cold fluorescent 224 254 248 (61 of 161 lights in 11a) with red light_security lamps (12 to 32 a map); props_silo dressing (prodcables256, wall_wires1/2, wall_console1..3, desk_console1a/2/3, fuel_cask, tirestack2/3).
- **Checked by:** map_modules_check: a Resistance interior with no material change between 96 and 136 over its floor

### The Resistance's quarters by their props: a dormitory of prison beds, a kitchen of fridges, a rec corner (`valve-base-quarters`)

A base's dormitory is a row of prison bed frames, some side by side 52 apart, each with a mattress that sags on it, lockers in a row 48 apart along one wall, an armchair, a couch and a table. The kitchen is four fridges, counters, two stoves, two sinks and shelves along the walls with the middle clear. A rec corner is a chess table and a cafeteria table with its bench. Rooms are low (ceiling 128 to 200). Rooms already built keep their look: ask the owner first before changing them.

Kitchen at z -1872 (504 by 736, ceiling 200): kitchen_fridge001a 4 (not solid), kitchen_counter001d 4 and 001a 2, kitchen_stove002a 2 and 001a, sinkkitchen01a 2, kitchen_shelf001a 2, soupprep, headcrabprep.

### A hospital ward: the bed off the wall with its kit round it, a washroom to each ward (`valve-hospital-ward`)

A ward is a room about 232 by 304 under 128 for one bed (two at most), with its own washroom 152 by 112 under 120. The bed is a loose prop standing 32 to 56 off the wall (never against it), often turned 3 to 20; round it an IV stand 42 to 51 away, a cart 52 to 58, a bedside cabinet 16 off the wall with a monitor on it, a radiator on the wall, a wall rail at 36, and over the bed's head a tube on the wall at 64 up, tilted 75, lighting the pillow. The washroom holds a bath 16 off the wall, a basin and a toilet, a radiator and a rail by the bath. Our clinic is the owner's field hospital (four beds in one ward with screens): ask the owner first before changing it; use this for a new hospital or clinic piece.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_02a (21 November): 13 hospital_bed01, all prop_physics flags 256; 6 in rooms 232 by 304 by 128 (sections cast from the beds); wall gap 32 (3), 40 (3), 48 (4), 56 (2); yaw 3 to 20 on 7. Within 128 of the ward beds: hospital_ivstand01 42 to 51, hospital_cart01 52 to 58, hospital_shelf01 53 to 87 (16 off its wall, 18 up), consolebox03a 57 to 111 (36 up, on the shelf), radiator01a 111 to 124 (21 up, on the wall), lights_florescent01a at 64 up pitched 75 (9 of 34) with a light_spot 220 235 245 600 cone 40 pitch -35 and a point 20. Nine washrooms all 152 by 112 by 120: bathtub01a 16 off, furnituresink001a 8 to 16 off at 23 up, furnituretoilet001a 16 off, a radiator, hospital_railing pieces. Ours (map_mods_b.py m_clinic): a ward 576 by 320 under 160 with four beds along its back wall and cloth screens between.
- **How:** A hospital room helper for new pieces: bed as m.dynamic or a loose prop 40 off the back wall, yaw jittered 0 to 15, the kit placed on a ring 40 to 60 round it (IV at the head, cart at the foot side, cabinet against the wall beside the head), the reading tube on the wall over the head at 64 pitched 75 with its light_spot; a washroom cell 152 by 112 off the ward. ### An operating theatre: the table in the middle, two lamps held still and turned, scrub sinks (`valve-operating-theatre`)

The operating table is fixed and square, 48 to 120 from every wall, in a room 288 to 451 wide under 124 to 192 (a big theatre has three tables in a row 92 apart). Over each table hang one to three surgery lamps about 100 over the floor and 24 to 32 under the ceiling, each rolled 45 and turned its own way so they cross over the table; they are physics props held still (no damage, motion off). Beside: a pair of scrub sinks on a wall, monitors on a cart or shelf, an IV stand, a cart. Our clinic's theatre lamp hangs from its top and shines straight down: ask the owner first before changing it.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_02a (21 November): 8 hospital_surgerytable01, prop_static, square on 7, 20 up, ceiling 104 to 172 over them, wall gaps 48 to 120; one room 376 by 288 by 128 with three tables at x -656, -564, -472. 6 hospital_surgerylight01: prop_physics spawnflags 266 (2 + 8 + 256), angles roll -45, yaws 0 with 120, 225 with 0, 30 with 225; 100 over the floor, 24 to 32 under the ceiling. sinkkitchen01a in pairs (2 rooms), monitor02 and controlroom_monitor001a/b, hospital_ivstand01, hospital_cart01. Ours (m_clinic): the theatre 192 by 320 under 176, one lamp with its top on the roof shining straight down.
- **How:** In a theatre piece: table static at the room's middle; two surgery lamps as held-still physics (spawnflags 266) or prop_dynamic_override (the owner's rule for standing things) at table + 80 up, roll 45, yaws 120 apart; their light_spot aimed at the table top. Two kitchen sinks side by side on one wall.

### A hospital corridor: 224 wide, a rail all along both walls, benches with a picture over each (`valve-hospital-corridor`)

Hospital corridors are wider than a flat's: 160 to 256 (commonest 224) under 128, the main one 192 high. Four beds in corridors 176 to 253 wide, yaws 105 to 195. The valve-sightlines median corridor is 112.

### A building without power: lamps named and off until the power comes back, flares and signs in the dark (`valve-power-out`)

To make a place dark that can be lit again, its lamps are real lights named in banks and set to start off, each a warm cage lamp spot (254 216 146 75, cone 70, pitch -70) with a glow sprite 10 under it, turned on together when the power is restored. In the dark, a road flare on the floor carries an orange light (254 174 10, 100 to 200) 12 over it, a green sprite marks the exit, a blue light by sparking wires. Even dark places keep a light within about 200 to 350 of most of their floor. In Living City this suits an outage or a raid's cut power: ask the owner first, since it is a new feature (and never a flicker style).

- **Why:** ep1_c17_02a (21 November): 76 lights; 10 named power_light_1 to 5 with spawnflags 1 (start off): light_spot 254 216 146 75 cone 70 pitch -70, each with an env_sprite light_glow03 (254 216 146) 10 under it, named power_light_sprite_N. Orange lights 254 174 10 at 100 and 200, 12 over the floor, beside the two flare props and small fires; sprite_exitdoor 37 186 60; electric_flickerlight 41 166 226 at 100 to 120. Named lights that start off per map: 00 10 of 48, 00a 7 of 56, 01 7 of 25, 02a 10 of 76. Node to nearest light, median: 02a 211, 00a 297, 00 343; nodes over 512 from any light: 00 25%, 00a 21%, 02a 9%.
- **How:** A bank of lamps for a module: each lamp prop with its light_spot and sprite named lc_NAME_power, spawnflags 1 on the lights, sprites hidden; one relay lc_NAME_power_on (TurnOn, ShowSprite) and _off (TurnOff, HideSprite) for the gamemode. A flare model with its own baked orange light where a dark room wants a hint.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_lint notes a named light that starts off with no output anywhere that turns it on
- **Tags:** valve, lighting, logic

### A car park: 112 clear, columns every 256, two cars to a bay, a deck that slopes 1 in 12 (`valve-car-park`)

A car park deck is 112 clear under its slab (96 under the beams), with columns 32 by 32 every 256 along, in rows that leave a drive aisle of about 384; each column bay takes two cars (128 a car) and is 176 to 192 deep. The deck itself slopes about 4.8 degrees (1 in 12), or decks are joined by ramps of 9.2 degrees (1 in 6) 224 to 240 wide. Bay lines, arrows and letters are overlays on the floor; wall markings on the columns. Cars mostly nose in (yaw square to the wall, 2 in 3), a few turned free; the kit is fluorescent tubes, sprinklers, pipes along the ceiling, power boxes, cones, a dumpster, bicycles, handrails along the ramps. A new piece, if the owner wants one. Pieces already built keep their look: ask the owner first before changing them.

- **Why:** styleguide_garage (21 November): deck x 2176 to 4464 by y -2176 to -1104, floor 0, ceiling 112 (96 under beams); columns 32 by 32 at x 2544, 2800, 3056, 3312, 3568, 3824, 4080, rows at y -2000, -1616, -1424; floor faces sloped 4.8 degrees over 1407k square units; 13 cars, yaws 86 to 92 or 272 on 9, 120, 226, 302 on 3, at 112 to 128 side by side; overlays garagegroundmarking01h 22 (32 by 128, pairs), 01i 12 (8 by 128), 01f, 01a, 01b, garagewallmarking02e 12 on the columns; prison_sprinkler001a 13, lights_florescent01a 8, handrail04_singlerise 12. ep1_c17_00 and 00a (the same deck): ceilings at nodes 112 (47 of 230) and 120 (96 of 317); ramps 9.2 degrees (444k square units in 00a), world brushes 792 by 224 and 752 by 240. Car models 76 to 87 wide, 152 to 203 long.
- **How:** A car park module: deck brushes sloping 1 in 12 (or flat decks with 1 in 6 ramps), column brushes 32 by 32 on a 256 pitch, slab 16 deep beams under the ceiling, bay line overlays every 128 along each row, car props in two of three bays nose in with a skin each, and the garage soundscape (ep1.antlion_parking_garage) inside.

### A fallen floor: the slab broken into tilted pieces, what stood on it slid down with it (`valve-collapsed-deck`)

When a floor or deck falls, build it as separate detail slabs 96 to 300 across and 27 to 56 thick, each tilted its own way (5 to 60 degrees), the floor's material on top and the ceiling's underneath, resting on the level below and on each other. What stood on the floor lies on the slabs tilted with them (a car pitched 8 to 15 and rolled onto its side or roof), and one thing may rest on another. Rubble piles, rebar and spawn chunks at the slabs' feet. For a wrecked district or a raid's aftermath: ask the owner first if it changes a piece they have walked.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_00a and styleguide_garage_antlionroom (21 November): the car park's upper deck as func_detail slabs: 96 by 284, 104 by 284, 120 by 284 at 7.1 degrees; 192 by 234 at 5.3; 135 by 192 at 15.9; 167 by 195 at 29.9; 102 by 237 at 47.8; 154 by 250 at 50.2; thickness 27 to 56; tops concretefloor033o, undersides concretewall075a and concretefloor013a. Cars on them: car001a_hatchback, car001b_hatchback, car003b, car002a static at pitch -11 roll 136 (7 of 15), -8 to -15 roll 17 to 29, one hatchback 76 over a car005b at the same x and y. 233 small displacements break the walls and ceilings round it (32 by 8, 128 by 32).
- **How:** map_build a broken_slab(x, y, z, w, d, thick, pitch, roll, top, under) that writes one tilted func_detail box; a collapse helper that cuts a deck into four to eight such slabs and lays a vehicle or furniture prop on one with the slab's pitch.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_floating.py checks that props on a tilted slab are tilted within 10 degrees of it (nothing level on a slope)

### A road tunnel under the city: 608 wide, 196 high, tiled, two carriageways and a central strip (`valve-road-tunnel`)

A road tunnel is about 608 wall to wall and 196 clear: tiled walls under a concrete ceiling, two carriageways of 240 to 272 with a 48 edge strip each side, a central strip 96 wide raised 4 carrying pillars about 875 apart. Sprinklers hang 20 under the ceiling in two rows 384 apart, one every 512. A new piece (our tunnel to the town is a 192 walkway): ask the owner first if it would replace a tunnel already built.

Ours (map_raven tunnel): 192 wide, z 304 to 448.

### A pipe run is straights with a connector at every joint, in stacked rows along the wall (`valve-pipe-runs`)

Run pipes along a service tunnel or plant room as straight pieces end to end with a connector at every joint (one connector for every straight), static, in rows: the big pipes (pipe03, 96 long) in three rows at about 80, 104 and 120 up, 20 to 44 off the wall; thin pipes (pipe02, pipe01, 48 long) higher, 184 to 208 up under the ceiling. Use the pipes' second skin on most. Bends and 45s turn the run; no open end shows (cap-open-ends). Pieces already built keep their look: ask the owner first before changing them.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_00a (21 November), the wet service tunnel: pipe03_straight01_long 112 (box 17 by 96) and pipe03_connector01 112, connectors 48 to 49 from a straight's middle on 75 (the joints), over the floor 104 (28), 120 (26), 80 (24), wall gap 36 (25), 20 (15), 16 (10), 44 (10), pitch 90 on 84 and -90 on 12, skin 1 on 88; pipe02_straight01_long 24 (48 long) with 25 connectors at 88 and 184 to 200 up; pipe01 11 at 208. ep1_c17_00: pipe02 12 straights and 12 connectors at 120 up. ep1_c17_02: pipe01 13 at 80 to 144, 4 off the wall.
- **How:** A pipe_run(points, family, height, off_wall) helper in map_build: lays straights every model length along the path, a connector at each joint, a 90 or 45 piece at each turn, skin 1 by default; three heights for a rack.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_lint notes a pipe straight whose ends have neither a connector, a bend nor another straight within 8

### Where antlions dig in: a broken wall with chunks and rebar, a rough cave behind, holes in floors (`valve-antlion-breach`)

Where antlions break into the city's underground, the tunnel's wall opens into a cave of displacements (power 3 and 4, pieces 128 to 512 across, rock with blends where it meets concrete), 120 to 680 high and never even. At the break lie about a dozen concrete spawn chunks (loose, within 80 of each other) and five or so rebar pieces sticking out of the broken edge at 60 to 90 degrees. On a floor they come up through a round broken hole model (concrete_spawnhole, 128 across) laid on the slab. Grubs, if any, cluster in threes to sevens, each cluster with a faint green light. The owner's nest is its own piece: ask the owner first before changing it.

- **Why:** styleguide_antlion_tunnel (21 November): service tunnel 352 wide (148 + 204) and 176 high; 119 displacements, power 3 on 74, 4 on 24, 2 on 21, all lightmapscale 16, sizes 128 by 256 (12), 128 by 384 (10), 384 by 512 (8); rockwall015a 74, blendrockdirt_tunnel01a 20, blendconcreterock001a 16; heights probed 120 to 680. concrete_spawnchunk001d/f 11 physics within 80; rebar002d_96 and 004d_96 5 at pitch 60, -60, 90, -90. npc_antlion_grub 27 in 5 clusters, each with a light 154 168 122 80 12 over it. concrete_spawnhole001a: styleguide_antlionhole 3, ep1_c17_00 2, 00a 3.
- **How:** map_build: a breach(wall, centre, w, h) that cuts the brush wall, lays the chunk and rebar props, and starts a displacement cave behind; a spawnhole prop where the nest's makers bring antlions up through a floor.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_lint notes a cave displacement of power 2 wider than 256 in an antlion piece

### A train shed: docks 96 over the bed, a tight grid of bell lamps under the roof (`valve-train-shed`)

Freight docks stand 96 over the gravel track bed, reached by steep metal steps; a loading shed holds two or three sidings 224 apart between a narrow dock and a wide loading floor. A repair shed puts the train at floor level with a sunk walk 64 deep and 120 wide along each side and galleries 256 up along the walls at the height of the car roofs. The track itself is a texture strip 128 wide on the gravel. Light a tall shed with many bell lamps hung right under the roof, 480 to 704 up, in rows 192 to 224 apart, each with a strong spot straight down; not with a few floods. Ours: the street's railway shed is one track 192 wide under 320 and its tracks are sleepers and rails: ask the owner first before changing it.

- **Why:** styleguide_train_loadingdock (21 November), cut at x 11568 and 10400: walls at y 8260 and 10084; dock 8292 to 8548 and floor 9284 to 10084 at -736, gravel bed -832 from 8548 to 9284; roof 608 over the docks, 704 over the bed; metal steps -816 to -736 over 128. train005 and train003 prop_dynamic at y 8985 and 9209. light_industrialbell01_on 48 (704 up 26, 480 up 17; nearest 224 on 24, 208 on 14); measure.json: light_spot 221 249 241 3000 cone 50/20 pitch -90. styleguide_train_repair, cut at y 9900: walls 7640 and 8936, floor -768, pits -832 at 8072 to 8192 and 8392 to 8512, grates at -512; bells 25, 488 to 552 up, 192 apart. ep1_c17_05 37 bells, 06 25. Track: metaltrack001a on brush strips 128 wide. Ours (map_corridor.py): SHED_H 320, the railway 320 to 512 from the canal's middle.
- **How:** A train shed or depot hall: a bell lamp grid helper (rows every 208, lamps every 208 along, hung at the roof's underside, each a light_spot 3000 cone 50 straight down); docks at track bed + 96 with metal steps; a repair bay with side pits 64 deep. ep1_c17_06 (picture C15, C13): docks 96 over a track bed 736 wide, three sidings 224 apart, 48 lamps in the shed.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_lint notes a hall over 400 high lit by fewer than one lamp per 50,000 square units of floor

### A Resistance hideout: the slotted door, barricades leaned on the windows, a radio corner (`valve-rebel-hideout`)

A Resistance hideout in a block of flats is shown by a few things: its door is the slotted door (a door with a sliding peephole); tall barricade pieces lean back 30 degrees against the windows from inside, 40 to 46 off the wall, and boards are nailed across the others; a radio corner on a shelf (a radio, a hard drive, a receiver cart); the flat lived in (couch, several chairs, a TV, fridge, sink, stove and a pot, cupboards, a bed frame tipped); warm dome lights and bulbs; a hoist or a crank as the way up. Ask the owner first before any of our hideouts' doors change.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_02b (21 November), the flats round x 1200, y 2000 (Barney and five citizens): door03_slotted_left (prop_door_rotating) with door03_slot (prop_dynamic at the same origin) twice (hideout_slit_door_1, barney_door_2); barricade_tall01a twice and tall02a at pitch 30, 39 to 46 off the wall, 52 up; barricade_short03a in a doorway; wood_board06a roll 105, flags 328, 41 to 61 out; citizenradio (prop_dynamic_override) 13 off the wall, harddrive02 12 off, reciever_cart 16 off; furniturecouch001a, chair02a 5, tv_monitor01, furniturefridge001a, sinkkitchen01a, kitchen_stove001a, metalpot001a, furniturecupboard001a 2, prison_bedframe001a pitch -7 roll 17; light_domelight02_on 5, lightbulb01a 3; pulleywheels_large01 with two w_crowbar wedged (pitch -71).
- **How:** A hideout kit in map_town (props only): the radio corner, leaned barricades at windows (pitch 30, back 40 off), boards on others, the living props; the slotted door only where the owner says (it is a door: real-doors). The coast's Resistance posts have the same radio corner, bigger: New Little Odessa's house (d2_coast_03) has four receivers, two more on a cart, a radio, an antenna, a control desk and five storage closets, with six floodlamps outside; the lighthouse (d2_coast_10) two receivers, a radio and the canal map, which also hangs in d2_coast_01's dock post.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_lint notes a barricade prop pitched over 20 that is more than 64 from a wall (a lean needs something to lean on)
- **Tags:** valve, props, interiors, story

- **See:** `valve_coast/02_coast01_dock_outpost_cut.png`, `valve_coast/03_coast03_odessa_house_cut.png`, `valve_coast/10_coast10_lighthouse.png`, `valve_ep1c17/11_c17_02b_hideout_cut.png`, `valve_ep1c17/12_c17_02b_hideout_radio.png`, `valve_ep2/38_sg_inn_rooms_cut.png`, `valve_ep2/39_sg_radio_station_overview.png`

### The Combine's walls through the old city run in straight lengths, each turned a little from the last, battered on one face (`valve-combine-wall-chain`)

Where the Combine walls off the old city it builds straight lengths 200 to 1000 long, each turned 3 to 17 degrees from the one before, so the line wanders off the grid; a street wall is 48 thick and 160 to 256 high with end caps 48 to 64 wide, its outer face leaning back 4 degrees and its inner face plumb. The great walls stand 768 high and 96 thick in lengths of 690 to 1093 turned 10 to 34 degrees off the grid, one face battered 4 to 5 degrees, the other plumb: lop-sided in section. Ask the owner first before changing a Combine wall or checkpoint already built.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_01 (21 November): metalcombine001 func_detail faces 433, 208, 574, 496, 375, 984, 858 wide, yaws 357, 357, 13, 30, 94, 77, 105; heights 160 to 256; thickness 37 to 40 on 78 brushes; outer faces 4 degrees off upright, all facing up (295k square units). ep1_c17_06: citadel_metalwall077a world brushes 768 high, faces 1093, 1089, 879, 877, 694 wide, normals at 10, 159, 190, 236 degrees, one face of each slab 4.4 to 4.7 off upright, its partner 0; 96 thick. Combine metal lean in 06: 4 (1100k) and 5 (839k), all facing up.
- **How:** A combine_wall(path, height, thick) helper: break the path into lengths of 200 to 1000, turn each by 3 to 17 from the last, lean the outer face back 4 (one plane tilted), cap the ends.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_lint notes a Combine metal wall longer than 1200 in one straight length
- **Tags:** valve, combine, geometry

### After a blast the tall things lean: lampposts, poles, signs and trees, in round steps (`valve-blast-lean`)

In a district wrecked by a blast, most tall street furniture leans: three in four lampposts and utility poles, half the signs, a quarter of the trees, each tilted 10 to 45 degrees (15, 22, 30, 45: round steps) and leaning many ways, one way a little more often than the others. They stay fixed (static). Low things (benches, bins, phones, awnings) stay upright. For a wrecked district only; ask the owner first before tilting anything in a street they have walked.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_01 (21 November): lamppost03a_off 12, tilted 9 (13, 22, 22, 22, 30, 30, 30, 30, 45); utilitypole02b 12, tilted 10 (10, 15 x5, 22, 30, 45, 45); signs 13, tilted 7 (22 x5, 30 x2); trees 18, tilted 5 (13 to 22); all prop_static; benches 4, bins 3, phones 6, awnings 1 upright. Lean toward 180 degrees on 7 of 22, 90 on 5, the rest spread. ep1_c17_01a: poles 4 of 10 (10 to 45), lean toward 180 on 5 of 6. ep1_c17_02b: lampposts 6 of 9 (6 to 66).
- **How:** A wrecked flag on street dressing (map_town, map_corridor): lampposts and poles get pitch or roll from (0, 15, 22.5, 30, 45) with three in four tilted, the base kept at the pavement (rotate about the foot: move the origin so the base stays put), yaw of the lean random with a bias.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_floating.py checks a tilted pole's foot still meets its pavement (the model rotated about its origin lifts or sinks the foot)
- **Tags:** valve, props, streets

- **See:** `valve_citadel/13_e00_citadel_foot.png`, `valve_ep1c17/05_c17_01_leaning_poles.png`

### Far haze and far ruins are flat cards, a long way off (`valve-haze-cards`)

Make the dust in the air and the far ruined skyline with flat cards rather than geometry: detail faces textured as fog (fake_fog01/02), as a far building or as a rubble heap, about 500 high and 500 to 1400 wide, standing 1300 to 4000 from where players walk, at a coarse texture scale (about 5), with fog that starts near (100 to 300) and closes at 3000 to 7000. Far rubble heaps are single faces sloped 50 to 60 degrees. A new tool for district edges; ask the owner first before changing a skyline they have seen.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_01 (21 November): 25 func_detail card faces (fake_fog01 8, fake_fog02 5, fake_fog01a 1, rubble_card01 10, building_card01 1), median 496 high and 1024 wide, from nodes p10 1281, median 2477, p90 4090; fake_fog at texture scale 5.31 and 4.88 (10% of the map's visible area). ep1_c17_02b 9 (fake_fog02 7, building_card02 2, median 832 high, 1280 wide, about 2500 out); 06 5; 01a 3; 02 3. Fog 210 176 130 from 200 to 300 out to 3000 to 7000 in 01, 01a, 02, 02b, 05; 214 198 169 to 13000 in 06.
- **How:** map_build: a haze_card(path, height, mat) that stands one thin func_detail slab per stretch of a district's far edge, 1500 to 3000 out from the play space, textured fake_fog01 at scale 5; a rubble_card slope where a far collapse should show.
- **Checked by:** proposed: map_assets.py confirms the card materials exist on the owner's server (fake_fog01 and building_card01 are Episode One materials)
- **Tags:** valve, geometry, lighting

- **See:** `valve_ep2/16_o07_farmyard_edge_eye.png`, `valve_ep2/28_o12_battle_woods_overview.png`

### In the dark the dead come where nobody is looking, and a light keeps them off (`valve-dark-spawns`)

Where a place is meant to be dark, the dead are not set out in advance: they come when a player's light goes off, only at spots no player can see, one to three at a time a few seconds apart, then not again for 20 s, and never more than two alive in one dark stretch. Turning the light back on cancels those still to come; a lit room (a fire, a lamp) is safe; a lit flare keeps them 1000 away for 30 s. Changes how our night and dark-district zombies behave: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_00 (tunnel_darkness): logic_playerproxy OnFlashlightOff sends the charge to a logic_compare (CompareValue .7); OnLessThan > a relay > logic_case PickRandom of 3, each spawning zombine, zombie or torso at @1, @3 or @5 from npc_template_maker (CriterionVisibility 1, MaxLiveChildren 1, Radius 256) into 32 info_npc_spawn_destination (ReuseDelay 20, nearest neighbour median 114) along about 5000 of tunnel; each one gets an aiscripted_schedule to walk at the player @0.1; OnSpawnNPC shuts the relay and opens it again @20; OnFlashlightOn CancelPendings all three; trigger_darkness_spawners round the lit rooms disable it. Flares: prop_physics flare.mdl (5 in 00, 8 in 00a) OnPhysGunPickup > SetMinimumSpawnDistance 1000, back to 0 by a 30 s logic_timer. ep1_c17_00a: math_count_flashlight (max 2, fed by OnAllLiveChildrenDead) shuts the dark spawns while 2 are alive. The companion sees in the dark only in lit pools: env_global ep_alyx_darknessmode switched by 6 triggers, 25 info_darknessmode_lightsource (22 in 00, radius 59 to 512, median 128).
- **How:** Gamemode: at night and in the infestation's dark stretches (docs/features/vortigaunts-and-weather.md, infestation-cleanup.md), dead that come from the dark could appear only out of every player's sight at placed spots, at most 2 alive per stretch and 20 s apart, held back while that player's flashlight is on or a flare burns within 1000; each walks to the nearest player (never Valve's !player, which is player 1). Map modules: lc_dark_spawn points about 100 apart in tunnels and cellars; lit pools of radius about 128 by fires and lamps.
- **Checked by:** A test: no dark spawn within sight of a player, none within 1000 of a burning flare, none while 2 are alive; robots.py walks a dark stretch with the light on and meets nothing.
- **Tags:** valve, npc, logic, gameplay, lighting

### Antlion burrows are shut by pushing something heavy over them (`valve-burrow-plugs`)

Where antlions break into a district, each burrow is a few holes close together; a car, a skip or a crate pushed over all of a burrow's holes shuts it and its spore puff stops; while any is shut the open ones send more, faster; push the thing off and the burrow opens again. All shut ends the incursion. A new way to clear antlions, by hand: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_00a car park: 3 npc_antlion_template_maker (pool_start 1, pool_regen 1 or 12 s, MaxLiveChildren 1, spawnflags 13344), each with its own group of 1 to 3 info_node_hint hinttype 400 about 30 to 40 apart. OnAllBlocked (every hint covered by a physics prop) > counter_BlockedHoles (max 3) Add 1 and that burrow's env_spore Disable; a spawn there afterwards (the car moved) > relay_Burrow_N_unblocked > Subtract 1 and the spores come back. A second counter (max 1): OnHitMax SetPoolRegenTime 2 and SetMaxLiveChildren 2 on all three, OnHitMin back to 12 and 1. At 3: Alyx's 'holes blocked' and the way on. ep1_c17_00: the training burrow (MaxNPCCount 4, pool 4, regen 5) OnAllBlocked kills the nag scenes and plays 'good work'. ep1_c17_01: counter_coveredburrows (max 2) fed by trigger_multiples (StartTouch Add, EndTouch Subtract) round the holes. All 11 antlion makers in the ten maps: createspores 1, ignorebugbait 1.
- **How:** Gamemode: an antlion incursion (docs/features/infestation-cleanup.md: the cave's nest is not built yet) gets 2 to 4 burrows of 1 to 3 holes; a burrow counts as shut while physics props cover all its holes (a box check every few seconds), puffs spores while open, and the open ones spawn every 2 s instead of 12 while any is shut. Map modules: lc_burrow_N_M hole points, with a car, skip or big crate within 300 of each burrow.
- **Checked by:** A test: covering every hole of a burrow stops its spawns and raises the others' rate; uncovering opens it again; all covered ends it.
- **Tags:** valve, npc, logic, gameplay

### An evacuation runs in trips: a small group waits at a door, follows its escort to the train, and the next group is ready as the escort heads back (`valve-evac-trips`)

Refugees wait out of sight by a door in groups of two to four; they join whoever comes for them and follow him to the train; one who boards is safe; the trip ends when every one of them is aboard or dead, so it never stalls; if a whole group dies, or the escort walks off without them, the next group comes out. Each trip brings a different answer from the Combine (scanners, then a dropship, a sniper, an APC, a breach), and soldiers left on a leg are cleared so each trip starts clean. People talk instead of text: a nag for the next group 10 s after a boarding, a citizen at the train door turning the escort back, one line from a boarded refugee when used. The first group cannot be hurt: it is the rehearsal. A new job or event for us: ask the owner first.

- **Why:** ep1_c17_05: counter_citizens_to_spawn starts at 4, then compare_repairroom_trips_FROM sets groups of 2, 4, 2, 2; refugees stand under assault_citizen_wait, neutral to the player, until trigger_pickup_citizens: SetSquad player_squad and like the player. At the train, trigger_citizen_boardtrain (filter_citizens): FireUser1 (leave the squad), FireUser3 (board), SetDamageFilter filter_citizens_safe. counter_citizens_boardtrain's max is set to the group size and fed by both OnUser3 and OnDeath; counter_deadCits (max = group) spawns a new group, and trigger_outStation Adds 10 to it. counter_trip_TO and counter_trip_FROM (max 6) drive 16 logic_compares: FROM 1 three scanner bombers and manhacks, TO 2 a dropship and soldiers @5 to 6, FROM 2 a random bomber and the sniper, FROM 3 the APC and a car group, TO 3 the trainkiller and roof soldiers, TO 4 a breach, TO 5 the area done and the last train. relay_resetparkinglot and relay_resetRepairRoom SetHealth 0 the soldiers left there. aisc_nag_evacNextGroup re-armed every 10 s; relay_denytrain (citizen_blocker, TLK_CITIZEN_PLAYER_DENYTRAIN) at most every 20 s; relay_use_cit_N every 10 s; filter_citizens_first_four (only !null may hurt) Killed when the first group has boarded. The last train goes when counter_everyone_in_place_for_barney_goodbye (max 5: refugees aboard or dead, Alyx and Barney at their spots, the player aboard) is full. ep1_c17_02: fleeing citizens run to 7 assault_rallypoints (nearest neighbour 148 to 640) that lead 72 to 1075 on to one assault point (tolerance 36, timeout 3); a second set of 7 leads to a second point.
- **How:** Gamemode: an evacuation event, or a Resistance job smuggling people to the station or a trainyard, could run as trips: 2 to 4 refugee NPCs at a door follow the player who presses E on one, are counted aboard or dead at the train, and the next group waits when the last is done; each trip draws a different Combine answer from a short table; refugees cannot be hurt once aboard. Map modules: lc_evac_door (the waiting spot), lc_evac_board (the train door), and rally spots 150 to 650 apart that lead on to one gathering place for citizens fleeing.
